NAME DWPURPLE WINDOWAPI
DESCRIPTION 'DWPurple instant messenger (libpurple + Dynamic Windows)'
STACKSIZE 0x100000

===== END FILE: dwpurple.def =====

===== BEGIN FILE: Makefile =====
# DWPurple - GNU make build for Linux/BSD (GTK3 Dynamic Windows), macOS and
# MSYS2/MinGW.  OS/2 uses Makefile.os2.
#
#   make                      build ./dwpurple
#   make HAVE_XSS=0           build without X11 screen saver idle detection
#   make DW_CFLAGS=... DW_LIBS=...   when Dynamic Windows has no .pc/config
#   make install PREFIX=/usr/local [DESTDIR=...]

PREFIX   ?= /usr/local
BINDIR   ?= $(PREFIX)/bin
SHAREDIR ?= $(PREFIX)/share/dwpurple

CC       ?= cc
PKG_CONFIG ?= pkg-config
CFLAGS   ?= -O2 -g
WARN     := -Wall -Wextra -Wno-unused-parameter -Wno-missing-field-initializers

UNAME_S  := $(shell uname -s 2>/dev/null)

# ---- Dynamic Windows: pkg-config, then dwindows-config, then defaults
DW_CFLAGS ?= $(shell $(PKG_CONFIG) --cflags dwindows 2>/dev/null || dwindows-config --cflags 2>/dev/null)
DW_LIBS   ?= $(shell $(PKG_CONFIG) --libs dwindows 2>/dev/null || dwindows-config --libs 2>/dev/null || echo -ldwindows)

# ---- libpurple 2.x and GLib
PURPLE_CFLAGS ?= $(shell $(PKG_CONFIG) --cflags purple glib-2.0 gobject-2.0 gmodule-2.0)
PURPLE_LIBS   ?= $(shell $(PKG_CONFIG) --libs purple glib-2.0 gobject-2.0 gmodule-2.0)

EXTRA_CFLAGS :=
EXTRA_LIBS   :=

ifneq (,$(findstring MINGW,$(UNAME_S))$(findstring MSYS,$(UNAME_S)))
  EXE := .exe
  EXTRA_LIBS += -lwinmm -mwindows
else
  EXE :=
  # gdklock.c: dlsym() and readable function names in its stack reports
  EXTRA_LIBS += -rdynamic
  ifeq ($(UNAME_S),Linux)
    EXTRA_LIBS += -ldl
  endif
  # X11 idle detection (optional; falls back to activity in DWPurple windows)
  HAVE_XSS ?= $(shell $(PKG_CONFIG) --exists xscrnsaver x11 2>/dev/null && echo 1 || echo 0)
  ifeq ($(HAVE_XSS),1)
    EXTRA_CFLAGS += -DHAVE_XSS $(shell $(PKG_CONFIG) --cflags xscrnsaver x11)
    EXTRA_LIBS   += $(shell $(PKG_CONFIG) --libs xscrnsaver x11)
  endif
endif

SRCS := main.c core.c eventloop.c util.c blist.c accounts.c conv.c emoji.c \
        request.c notify.c xfer.c plugins.c debugwin.c sound.c idle.c \
        pounces.c prefs.c roomlist.c privacy.c tray.c gdklock.c ircconsole.c
OBJS := $(addprefix build/,$(SRCS:.c=.o))
TARGET := dwpurple$(EXE)

ALL_CFLAGS := -std=gnu99 $(WARN) $(CFLAGS) $(DW_CFLAGS) $(PURPLE_CFLAGS) $(EXTRA_CFLAGS)

.PHONY: all clean install uninstall

all: $(TARGET)

$(TARGET): $(OBJS)
    $(CC) $(LDFLAGS) -o $@ $(OBJS) $(DW_LIBS) $(PURPLE_LIBS) $(EXTRA_LIBS)

build/%.o: src/%.c src/dwpurple.h | build
    $(CC) $(ALL_CFLAGS) -c $< -o $@

build:
    mkdir -p build

clean:
    rm -rf build $(TARGET)

install: $(TARGET)
    install -d $(DESTDIR)$(BINDIR) $(DESTDIR)$(SHAREDIR)/icons
    install -m 755 $(TARGET) $(DESTDIR)$(BINDIR)/$(TARGET)
    install -m 644 icons/*.png $(DESTDIR)$(SHAREDIR)/icons/

uninstall:
    rm -f $(DESTDIR)$(BINDIR)/$(TARGET)
    rm -rf $(DESTDIR)$(SHAREDIR)

===== END FILE: Makefile =====

===== BEGIN FILE: README.md =====
# DWPurple

DWPurple is an instant messenger in the style of Pidgin. It uses **libpurple 2.x** for
the protocols and **Dynamic Windows** for the user interface, so the same C sources build
for GTK 3 (Linux/BSD), OS/2 Presentation Manager, Windows and macOS.

## Features

- Buddy list with groups, contacts, chats, collapse/expand, offline/empty-group filters,
  status selector and status message
- Tabbed conversations (IM and chat), typing notifications, chat user list with
  op/voice prefixes, nick Tab-completion, `/commands`, sent-message history (Ctrl+Up/Down)
- Reaction notices ("Reacted with :x:") can be hidden; `:emoji_codes:` conversion,
  both per conversation (Options menu) with global defaults
- Emoji picker, send image (inline when the protocol supports it, otherwise as a file)
- Account manager and editor (user splits, protocol options, registration)
- Join/add chat dialogs built from each protocol's chat fields
- Room list browser with filter, category expansion, join and add
- Privacy settings and block list; Block/Unblock from menus
- Buddy pounces (same `pounces.xml` actions as Pidgin)
- Sounds per event with automatic, command, beep or no playback
- System idle detection: Windows input, X11 screen saver extension, or activity in
  DWPurple windows
- System tray icon with status menu and unread indicator
- Desktop notifications for messages in background tabs (where Dynamic Windows supports them)
- File transfers, plugin manager (load, configure, actions), request/notify dialogs,
  debug window, preferences dialog
- Automatic reconnect with backoff after non-fatal disconnects

## Building on Linux (for example openSUSE Tumbleweed)

Requirements: GCC, GNU make, pkg-config, the libpurple 2.x development package, the
GLib development package, Dynamic Windows (installed, with `dwindows.pc` or
`dwindows-config`) and, optionally, the XScreenSaver development package.

```sh
sudo zypper install gcc make pkg-config libpurple-devel glib2-devel libXss-devel
make
./dwpurple
sudo make install            # /usr/local/bin/dwpurple, /usr/local/share/dwpurple/icons
```

If Dynamic Windows provides neither a `.pc` file nor `dwindows-config`:

```sh
make DW_CFLAGS="-I/usr/local/include" DW_LIBS="-L/usr/local/lib -ldwindows"
```

Add `HAVE_XSS=0` to build without the X11 screen saver extension.

## Building on OS/2

```sh
make -f Makefile.os2 DW_DIR=/path/to/dwindows
```

If pkg-config is not available, set `PURPLE_CFLAGS` and `PURPLE_LIBS` yourself. Copy
the `icons` directory next to `dwpurple.exe`. OS/2 has no built-in player for automatic
sound playback. Set the sound method to **Command** in Preferences, or the program beeps.
The tray icon requires a running SysTray/xCenter tray server.

## Command line

```
dwpurple [-c DIR] [-d] [-n] [-h] [-v]
  -c, --config DIR   configuration directory (default ~/.dwpurple)
  -d, --debug        print libpurple debug output to the terminal
  -n, --nologin      start with all accounts offline
```

The configuration directory is separate from Pidgin's `~/.purple` by default. To reuse
Pidgin's accounts, run `dwpurple -c ~/.purple` while Pidgin is closed.

## Files

| Path | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `<config>/emoji.txt` | Custom emoji codes, one `code = emoji` per line |
| `<exe dir>/icons`, `<prefix>/share/dwpurple/icons`, `<config>/ui-icons` | Status, chat and application icons (PNG; ICO on Windows) |
| `<datadir>/sounds/purple`, `<exe dir>/sounds`, `<config>/sounds` | Default sound files (Pidgin's sounds are used if installed) |

## Limitations

- Message history is plain text. Dynamic Windows has no rich-text widget on every
  platform, so formatting is removed and images appear as `[image]`.
- No voice/video. libpurple media requires GStreamer and a UI that Dynamic Windows does not provide.
- Buddy icons are not shown.

## License

GPL version 2 or later, the same as libpurple. Dynamic Windows is distributed under its own
license.

===== END FILE: README.md =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/accounts.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - account manager, account editor and account action menus.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

/* =========================================================== action menus */

static GPtrArray *plugin_actions = NULL;     /* PurplePluginAction, popup lifetime */

static void reset_plugin_actions(void)
{
    if(!plugin_actions)
        plugin_actions = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func((GDestroyNotify)purple_plugin_action_free);
    g_ptr_array_set_size(plugin_actions, 0);
}

static void act_edit(gpointer account, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b; (void)s;
    if(dwp_account_exists(account))
        dwp_account_edit(account);
}

static void act_privacy(gpointer account, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b; (void)s;
    if(dwp_account_exists(account))
        dwp_privacy_show(account);
}

static void act_console(gpointer account, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b; (void)s;
    dwp_irc_console_show(account);
}

static void act_connect(gpointer account, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b; (void)s;
    if(dwp_account_exists(account))
        dwp_core_reconnect_now(account);
}

static void set_enabled(PurpleAccount *account, gboolean enable)
{
    if(!dwp_account_exists(account) || !!purple_account_get_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID) == !!enable)
        return;
    if(enable)
    {
        /* Make sure the account comes up with the current global status. */
        PurpleSavedStatus *saved = purple_savedstatus_get_current();

        if(saved)
            purple_savedstatus_activate_for_account(saved, account);
    }
    purple_account_set_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID, enable);
}

static void act_enable(gpointer account, gpointer enable, const char *s)
{
    (void)s;
    set_enabled(account, GPOINTER_TO_INT(enable));
}

static void act_plugin(gpointer act, gpointer gc, const char *s)
{
    PurplePluginAction *pa = act;

    (void)s;
    if(dwp_connection_exists(gc) && pa->callback)
        pa->callback(pa);
}

static void add_account_items(HMENUI sub, PurpleAccount *account)
{
    PurpleConnection *gc = purple_account_get_connection(account);

    dwp_menu_add(sub, "~Edit Account", act_edit, account, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(sub, "~Privacy...", act_privacy, account, NULL, NULL);
    if(dwp_irc_console_supported(account))
        dwp_menu_add(sub, "Server ~Console", act_console, account, NULL, NULL);
    if(gc && purple_account_is_connected(account))
    {
        PurplePlugin *plugin = purple_connection_get_prpl(gc);

        if(plugin && PURPLE_PLUGIN_HAS_ACTIONS(plugin))
        {
            GList *actions = PURPLE_PLUGIN_ACTIONS(plugin, gc), *a;

            if(actions)
                dwp_menu_separator(sub);
            for(a = actions; a; a = a->next)
            {
                PurplePluginAction *pa = a->data;
                char *label;

                if(!pa)
                {
                    dwp_menu_separator(sub);
                    continue;
                }
                pa->plugin = plugin;
                pa->context = gc;
                g_ptr_array_add(plugin_actions, pa);
                label = dwp_mnemonic(pa->label);
                dwp_menu_add(sub, label, act_plugin, pa, gc, NULL);
                g_free(label);
            }
            g_list_free(actions);
        }
    }
    else if(!gc && purple_account_get_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID))
        dwp_menu_add(sub, "~Connect", act_connect, account, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_separator(sub);
    if(purple_account_get_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID))
        dwp_menu_add(sub, "~Disable", act_enable, account, GINT_TO_POINTER(FALSE), NULL);
    else
        dwp_menu_add(sub, "~Enable", act_enable, account, GINT_TO_POINTER(TRUE), NULL);
}

void dwp_account_actions_menu(PurpleAccount *account, HWND parent)
{
    HMENUI menu;

    if(!dwp_account_exists(account))
        return;
    dwp_actions_reset();
    reset_plugin_actions();
    menu = dw_menu_new(0);
    add_account_items(menu, account);
    dwp_menu_popup_here(&menu, parent);
}

void dwp_accounts_popup(HWND parent)
{
    HMENUI menu;
    GList *l;

    dwp_actions_reset();
    reset_plugin_actions();
    menu = dw_menu_new(0);
    for(l = purple_accounts_get_all(); l; l = l->next)
    {
        PurpleAccount *account = l->data;
        HMENUI sub = dw_menu_new(0);
        char *name = dwp_account_display_name(account);
        char *label = g_strdup_printf("%s - %s", name,
                                      purple_account_get_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID)
                                      ? dwp_primitive_label(dwp_account_primitive(account)) : "Disabled");

        add_account_items(sub, account);
        dw_menu_append_item(menu, label, DW_MENU_POPUP, 0, TRUE, FALSE, sub);
        g_free(label);
        g_free(name);
    }
    if(!purple_accounts_get_all())
        dw_menu_append_item(menu, "(no accounts)", DW_MENU_POPUP, 0, TRUE, FALSE, DW_NOMENU);
    dwp_menu_popup_here(&menu, parent);
}

/* ======================================================== accounts window */

typedef struct {
    HWND window, list;
    gboolean refresh_pending;
} AccountsDlg;

static AccountsDlg *accounts_dlg = NULL;
static int accounts_handle;

static char *account_status_text(PurpleAccount *account)
{
    const PurpleConnectionErrorInfo *err;

    if(!purple_account_get_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID))
        return g_strdup("Disabled");
    if(purple_account_is_connected(account))
    {
        PurpleStatus *status = purple_account_get_active_status(account);

        return g_strdup(status ? purple_status_get_name(status) : "Online");
    }
    if(purple_account_is_connecting(account))
        return g_strdup("Connecting...");
    err = purple_account_get_current_error(account);
    if(err && err->description)
        return g_strdup_printf("Error: %s", err->description);
    return g_strdup("Offline");
}

static PurpleAccount *selected_account(void)
{
    PurpleAccount *account;

    if(!accounts_dlg)
        return NULL;
    account = (PurpleAccount *)dw_container_query_start(accounts_dlg->list, DW_CRA_SELECTED | DW_CR_RETDATA);
    return dwp_account_exists(account) ? account : NULL;
}

static void accounts_refresh(gpointer unused)
{
    PurpleAccount *selected;
    GList *l;
    int count = 0, z = 0;
    void *alloc;

    (void)unused;
    if(!accounts_dlg)
        return;
    accounts_dlg->refresh_pending = FALSE;
    selected = selected_account();
    dw_container_clear(accounts_dlg->list, TRUE);
    count = (int)g_list_length(purple_accounts_get_all());
    if(!count)
        return;
    alloc = dw_container_alloc(accounts_dlg->list, count);
    for(l = purple_accounts_get_all(); l; l = l->next, z++)
    {
        PurpleAccount *account = l->data;
        char *enabled = purple_account_get_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID) ? "Yes" : "No";
        char *user = (char *)purple_account_get_username(account);
        char *proto = (char *)purple_account_get_protocol_name(account);
        char *status = account_status_text(account);

        dw_container_set_item(accounts_dlg->list, alloc, 0, z, &enabled);
        dw_container_set_item(accounts_dlg->list, alloc, 1, z, &user);
        dw_container_set_item(accounts_dlg->list, alloc, 2, z, &proto);
        dw_container_set_item(accounts_dlg->list, alloc, 3, z, &status);
        dw_container_set_row_title(alloc, z, user);
        dw_container_set_row_data(alloc, z, account);
        g_free(status);
    }
    dw_container_insert(accounts_dlg->list, alloc, count);
    dw_container_optimize(accounts_dlg->list);
    if(selected)
        dw_container_cursor_by_data(accounts_dlg->list, selected);
}

static void schedule_refresh(void)
{
    if(!accounts_dlg || accounts_dlg->refresh_pending)
        return;
    accounts_dlg->refresh_pending = TRUE;
    dwp_defer(accounts_refresh, NULL);
}

static void accounts_changed(PurpleAccount *account, gpointer data)
{
    (void)account;
    (void)data;
    schedule_refresh();
}

static void account_removed(PurpleAccount *account, gpointer data)
{
    (void)account;
    (void)data;
    /* The account is destroyed right after this: drop rows synchronously. */
    if(accounts_dlg)
        dw_container_clear(accounts_dlg->list, TRUE);
    schedule_refresh();
}

static int DWSIGNAL acc_add(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w; (void)data;
    dwp_account_edit(NULL);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL acc_modify(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = selected_account();

    (void)w; (void)data;
    if(account)
        dwp_account_edit(account);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL acc_enter(HWND w, char *text, void *data, void *itemdata)
{
    (void)w; (void)text; (void)data;
    if(dwp_account_exists(itemdata))
        dwp_account_edit(itemdata);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL acc_delete(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = selected_account();
    char *name;
    gboolean ok;

    (void)w; (void)data;
    if(!account)
        return TRUE;
    name = dwp_account_display_name(account);
    ok = dwp_confirm("Delete Account", "Delete the account %s?\n\nIts buddies and logs on this "
                     "computer will no longer be associated with it.", name);
    g_free(name);
    if(ok && dwp_account_exists(account))
        purple_accounts_delete(account);
    schedule_refresh();
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL acc_toggle(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = selected_account();

    (void)w; (void)data;
    if(account)
        set_enabled(account, !purple_account_get_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID));
    schedule_refresh();
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL acc_actions(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = selected_account();

    (void)w; (void)data;
    if(account)
        dwp_account_actions_menu(account, accounts_dlg->window);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL acc_context(HWND w, char *text, int x, int y, void *data, void *itemdata)
{
    (void)w; (void)text; (void)x; (void)y; (void)data;
    if(dwp_account_exists(itemdata))
        dwp_account_actions_menu(itemdata, accounts_dlg->window);
    return TRUE;
}

static void accounts_dlg_free(gpointer p)
{
    g_free(p);
}

static int DWSIGNAL acc_close(HWND w, void *data)
{
    AccountsDlg *d = accounts_dlg;

    (void)w; (void)data;
    if(!d)
        return TRUE;
    accounts_dlg = NULL;
    purple_signals_disconnect_by_handle(&accounts_handle);
    dwp_window_close_later(d->window, accounts_dlg_free, d);
    return TRUE;
}

void dwp_accounts_show(void)
{
    static const char *const sigs[] = {
        "account-added", "account-enabled", "account-disabled", "account-status-changed",
        "account-connecting", "account-signed-on", "account-signed-off",
        "account-error-changed", "account-alias-changed", NULL
    };
    unsigned long flags[4] = {
        DW_CFA_STRING | DW_CFA_CENTER | DW_CFA_HORZSEPARATOR | DW_CFA_SEPARATOR,
        DW_CFA_STRING | DW_CFA_LEFT | DW_CFA_HORZSEPARATOR | DW_CFA_SEPARATOR,
        DW_CFA_STRING | DW_CFA_LEFT | DW_CFA_HORZSEPARATOR | DW_CFA_SEPARATOR,
        DW_CFA_STRING | DW_CFA_LEFT | DW_CFA_HORZSEPARATOR | DW_CFA_SEPARATOR
    };
    char *titles[4] = { "Enabled", "Username", "Protocol", "Status" };
    AccountsDlg *d;
    HWND box, buttons;
    void *h = purple_accounts_get_handle();
    int z;

    if(accounts_dlg)
    {
        dw_window_show(accounts_dlg->window);
        dw_window_raise(accounts_dlg->window);
        return;
    }
    d = accounts_dlg = g_new0(AccountsDlg, 1);
    d->window = dwp_window_new("Accounts", 0, 0);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(d->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    d->list = dw_container_new(0, FALSE);
    dw_container_setup(d->list, flags, titles, 4, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, d->list, 520, 220, TRUE, TRUE, 2);
    dw_signal_connect(d->list, DW_SIGNAL_ITEM_ENTER, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(acc_enter), NULL);
    dw_signal_connect(d->list, DW_SIGNAL_ITEM_CONTEXT, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(acc_context), NULL);

    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Add...", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(acc_add), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Modify...", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(acc_modify), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Delete", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(acc_delete), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Enable/Disable", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(acc_toggle), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Actions...", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(acc_actions), NULL);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(acc_close), NULL);
    dw_signal_connect(d->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(acc_close), NULL);

    for(z = 0; sigs[z]; z++)
        purple_signal_connect(h, sigs[z], &accounts_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(accounts_changed), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(h, "account-removed", &accounts_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(account_removed), NULL);

    accounts_refresh(NULL);
    dwp_window_present(d->window, 600, 320);
}

/* ========================================================= account editor */

typedef struct {
    PurpleAccountUserSplit *split;
    HWND entry;
} SplitWidget;

typedef struct {
    PurpleAccountOption *option;
    HWND widget;
    GPtrArray *values;      /* STRING_LIST: internal values, parallel to labels */
    GPtrArray *labels;
} OptionWidget;

typedef struct {
    PurpleAccount *account;
    HWND window, protocol, notebook;
    HWND basic_holder, basic_box, adv_holder, adv_box;
    HWND username, password, remember, alias, check_mail, reg;
    GPtrArray *prpl_ids, *prpl_names;
    GArray *splits;
    GPtrArray *options;
    char *saved_user, *saved_pass, *saved_alias;
    gboolean saved_remember;
    int last_protocol;
} Editor;

static void option_widget_free(gpointer p)
{
    OptionWidget *o = p;

    if(o->values)
        g_ptr_array_free(o->values, TRUE);
    if(o->labels)
        g_ptr_array_free(o->labels, TRUE);
    g_free(o);
}

static void editor_free(gpointer p)
{
    Editor *e = p;

    g_ptr_array_free(e->prpl_ids, TRUE);
    g_ptr_array_free(e->prpl_names, TRUE);
    g_array_free(e->splits, TRUE);
    g_ptr_array_free(e->options, TRUE);
    g_free(e->saved_user);
    g_free(e->saved_pass);
    g_free(e->saved_alias);
    g_free(e);
}

static PurplePlugin *editor_prpl(Editor *e)
{
    int index;

    if(e->account)
        return purple_find_prpl(purple_account_get_protocol_id(e->account));
    index = dwp_combo_index(e->protocol, (char **)e->prpl_names->pdata, (int)e->prpl_names->len);
    if(index < 0)
        return NULL;
    return purple_find_prpl(g_ptr_array_index(e->prpl_ids, index));
}

/* Remember what the user typed before rebuilding for another protocol. */
static void editor_save_basic(Editor *e)
{
    if(!e->username)
        return;
    g_free(e->saved_user);
    e->saved_user = dwp_get_text(e->username);
    if(e->password)
    {
        g_free(e->saved_pass);
        e->saved_pass = dwp_get_text(e->password);
        e->saved_remember = dw_checkbox_get(e->remember);
    }
    g_free(e->saved_alias);
    e->saved_alias = dwp_get_text(e->alias);
}

static void editor_build(Editor *e)
{
    PurplePlugin *prpl = editor_prpl(e);
    PurplePluginProtocolInfo *info = prpl ? PURPLE_PLUGIN_PROTOCOL_INFO(prpl) : NULL;
    char *username;
    GList *l;
    int z;
    gboolean no_password;

    editor_save_basic(e);
    g_array_set_size(e->splits, 0);
    g_ptr_array_set_size(e->options, 0);
    e->username = e->password = e->remember = e->alias = e->check_mail = e->reg = 0;
    e->basic_box = dwp_box_reset(e->basic_holder, e->basic_box, DW_VERT);
    e->adv_box = dwp_box_reset(e->adv_holder, e->adv_box, DW_VERT);

    /* ---- user splits ("user@server/resource") */
    username = g_strdup(e->account ? purple_account_get_username(e->account)
                                   : (e->saved_user ? e->saved_user : ""));
    e->username = dw_entryfield_new("", 0);
    dwp_form_row(e->basic_box, "Username:", e->username);
    for(l = info ? info->user_splits : NULL; l; l = l->next)
    {
        SplitWidget sw;
        char *label = dwp_strip_mnemonic(purple_account_user_split_get_text(l->data));
        char *withcolon = g_strdup_printf("%s:", label);

        sw.split = l->data;
        sw.entry = dw_entryfield_new("", 0);
        dwp_form_row(e->basic_box, withcolon, sw.entry);
        g_array_append_val(e->splits, sw);
        g_free(withcolon);
        g_free(label);
    }
    /* Peel split values off the end of the username, last split first. */
    for(z = (int)e->splits->len - 1; z >= 0; z--)
    {
        SplitWidget *sw = &g_array_index(e->splits, SplitWidget, z);
        char sep = purple_account_user_split_get_separator(sw->split);
        const char *value = NULL;
        char *pos = NULL;

        if(e->account)
            pos = purple_account_user_split_get_reverse(sw->split) ? strrchr(username, sep)
                                                                   : strchr(username, sep);
        if(pos)
        {
            *pos = '\0';
            value = pos + 1;
        }
        else
            value = purple_account_user_split_get_default_value(sw->split);
        dw_window_set_text(sw->entry, value ? value : "");
    }
    dw_window_set_text(e->username, username);
    g_free(username);

    /* ---- password / alias / mail / register */
    no_password = info && (info->options & OPT_PROTO_NO_PASSWORD);
    if(!no_password)
    {
        const char *pass = e->account ? purple_account_get_password(e->account) : e->saved_pass;

        e->password = dw_entryfield_password_new(pass ? pass : "", 0);
        dwp_form_row(e->basic_box, (info && (info->options & OPT_PROTO_PASSWORD_OPTIONAL))
                                   ? "Password (optional):" : "Password:", e->password);
        e->remember = dw_checkbox_new("Remember password", 0);
        dw_checkbox_set(e->remember, e->account ? purple_account_get_remember_password(e->account)
                                                : e->saved_remember);
        dwp_form_row(e->basic_box, "", e->remember);
    }
    e->alias = dw_entryfield_new(e->account ? (purple_account_get_alias(e->account)
                                               ? purple_account_get_alias(e->account) : "")
                                            : (e->saved_alias ? e->saved_alias : ""), 0);
    dwp_form_row(e->basic_box, "Local alias:", e->alias);
    if(info && (info->options & OPT_PROTO_MAIL_CHECK))
    {
        e->check_mail = dw_checkbox_new("New mail notifications", 0);
        dw_checkbox_set(e->check_mail, e->account ? purple_account_get_check_mail(e->account) : FALSE);
        dwp_form_row(e->basic_box, "", e->check_mail);
    }
    if(!e->account && info && info->register_user)
    {
        e->reg = dw_checkbox_new("Create this new account on the server", 0);
        dwp_form_row(e->basic_box, "", e->reg);
    }

    /* ---- protocol options */
    for(l = info ? info->protocol_options : NULL; l; l = l->next)
    {
        PurpleAccountOption *opt = l->data;
        const char *setting = purple_account_option_get_setting(opt);
        char *label = dwp_strip_mnemonic(purple_account_option_get_text(opt));
        OptionWidget *o = g_new0(OptionWidget, 1);

        o->option = opt;
        switch(purple_account_option_get_type(opt))
        {
            case PURPLE_PREF_BOOLEAN:
            {
                gboolean def = purple_account_option_get_default_bool(opt);

                o->widget = dw_checkbox_new(label, 0);
                dw_checkbox_set(o->widget, e->account ? purple_account_get_bool(e->account, setting, def) : def);
                dwp_form_row(e->adv_box, "", o->widget);
                break;
            }
            case PURPLE_PREF_INT:
            {
                int def = purple_account_option_get_default_int(opt);
                int value = e->account ? purple_account_get_int(e->account, setting, def) : def;
                char buf[32];

                g_snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%d", value);
                o->widget = dw_spinbutton_new(buf, 0);
                dw_spinbutton_set_limits(o->widget, G_MAXINT, G_MININT);
                dw_spinbutton_set_pos(o->widget, value);
                dwp_form_row(e->adv_box, label, o->widget);
                break;
            }
            case PURPLE_PREF_STRING:
            {
                const char *def = purple_account_option_get_default_string(opt);
                const char *value = e->account ? purple_account_get_string(e->account, setting, def) : def;

                o->widget = purple_account_option_get_masked(opt)
                            ? dw_entryfield_password_new(value ? value : "", 0)
                            : dw_entryfield_new(value ? value : "", 0);
                dwp_form_row(e->adv_box, label, o->widget);
                break;
            }
            case PURPLE_PREF_STRING_LIST:
            {
                const char *def = purple_account_option_get_default_list_value(opt);
                const char *current = e->account ? purple_account_get_string(e->account, setting, def) : def;
                GList *i;
                int sel = -1;

                o->values = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(g_free);
                o->labels = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(g_free);
                for(i = purple_account_option_get_list(opt); i; i = i->next)
                {
                    PurpleKeyValuePair *kvp = i->data;

                    if(purple_strequal(kvp->value, current))
                        sel = (int)o->values->len;
                    g_ptr_array_add(o->labels, g_strdup(kvp->key));
                    g_ptr_array_add(o->values, g_strdup(kvp->value));
                }
                o->widget = dw_combobox_new("", 0);
                dwp_combo_fill(o->widget, (char **)o->labels->pdata, (int)o->labels->len, sel >= 0 ? sel : 0);
                dwp_form_row(e->adv_box, label, o->widget);
                break;
            }
            default:
                g_free(o);
                o = NULL;
                break;
        }
        if(o)
            g_ptr_array_add(e->options, o);
        g_free(label);
    }
    if(!e->options->len)
        dw_box_pack_start(e->adv_box, dwp_label("This protocol has no additional options."),
                          DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 4);
    dw_window_redraw(e->window);
}

static int DWSIGNAL editor_protocol_changed(HWND w, int index, void *data)
{
    Editor *e = data;

    (void)w;
    if(index == e->last_protocol)
        return FALSE;
    e->last_protocol = index;
    editor_build(e);
    return FALSE;
}

static char *compose_username(Editor *e)
{
    GString *out = g_string_new(NULL);
    char *base = dwp_get_text_trimmed(e->username);
    guint z;

    g_string_append(out, base);
    g_free(base);
    for(z = 0; z < e->splits->len; z++)
    {
        SplitWidget *sw = &g_array_index(e->splits, SplitWidget, z);
        char *value = dwp_get_text_trimmed(sw->entry);

        if(!*value)
        {
            g_free(value);
            value = g_strdup(purple_account_user_split_get_default_value(sw->split));
        }
        g_string_append_c(out, purple_account_user_split_get_separator(sw->split));
        g_string_append(out, value ? value : "");
        g_free(value);
    }
    return g_string_free(out, FALSE);
}

static int DWSIGNAL editor_cancel(HWND w, void *data)
{
    Editor *e = data;

    (void)w;
    dwp_window_close_later(e->window, editor_free, e);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL editor_save(HWND w, void *data)
{
    Editor *e = data;
    PurplePlugin *prpl;
    PurplePluginProtocolInfo *info;
    PurpleAccount *account, *existing;
    char *base, *full, *alias;
    const char *proto_id;
    gboolean is_new = (e->account == NULL);
    guint z;

    (void)w;
    if(e->account && !dwp_account_exists(e->account))
    {
        dwp_window_close_later(e->window, editor_free, e);
        return TRUE;
    }
    prpl = editor_prpl(e);
    if(!prpl)
    {
        dw_messagebox("Account", DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_WARNING, "The protocol plugin for this account is not available.");
        return TRUE;
    }
    info = PURPLE_PLUGIN_PROTOCOL_INFO(prpl);
    base = dwp_get_text_trimmed(e->username);
    if(!*base)
    {
        g_free(base);
        dw_messagebox("Account", DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_WARNING, "Please enter a username.");
        return TRUE;
    }
    g_free(base);
    full = compose_username(e);
    proto_id = prpl->info->id;
    existing = purple_accounts_find(full, proto_id);
    if(existing && existing != e->account)
    {
        dw_messagebox("Account", DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_WARNING,
                      "An account named %s already exists for this protocol.", full);
        g_free(full);
        return TRUE;
    }

    account = is_new ? purple_account_new(full, proto_id) : e->account;
    if(!is_new && !purple_strequal(full, purple_account_get_username(account)))
        purple_account_set_username(account, full);
    g_free(full);
    alias = dwp_get_text_trimmed(e->alias);
    purple_account_set_alias(account, *alias ? alias : NULL);
    g_free(alias);
    if(!(info->options & OPT_PROTO_NO_PASSWORD) && e->password)
    {
        char *pass = dwp_get_text(e->password);

        purple_account_set_remember_password(account, dw_checkbox_get(e->remember) ? TRUE : FALSE);
        purple_account_set_password(account, *pass ? pass : NULL);
        g_free(pass);
    }
    if((info->options & OPT_PROTO_MAIL_CHECK) && e->check_mail)
        purple_account_set_check_mail(account, dw_checkbox_get(e->check_mail) ? TRUE : FALSE);

    for(z = 0; z < e->options->len; z++)
    {
        OptionWidget *o = g_ptr_array_index(e->options, z);
        const char *setting = purple_account_option_get_setting(o->option);

        switch(purple_account_option_get_type(o->option))
        {
            case PURPLE_PREF_BOOLEAN:
                purple_account_set_bool(account, setting, dw_checkbox_get(o->widget) ? TRUE : FALSE);
                break;
            case PURPLE_PREF_INT:
                purple_account_set_int(account, setting, (int)dw_spinbutton_get_pos(o->widget));
                break;
            case PURPLE_PREF_STRING:
            {
                char *v = dwp_get_text(o->widget);

                purple_account_set_string(account, setting, v);
                g_free(v);
                break;
            }
            case PURPLE_PREF_STRING_LIST:
            {
                int index = dwp_combo_index(o->widget, (char **)o->labels->pdata, (int)o->labels->len);

                if(index >= 0)
                    purple_account_set_string(account, setting, g_ptr_array_index(o->values, index));
                break;
            }
            default:
                break;
        }
    }

    if(is_new)
    {
        purple_accounts_add(account);
        if(e->reg && dw_checkbox_get(e->reg))
            purple_account_register(account);
        else
        {
            PurpleSavedStatus *saved = purple_savedstatus_get_current();

            if(saved)
            {
                purple_savedstatus_activate_for_account(saved, account);
                purple_account_set_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID, TRUE);
            }
        }
    }
    dwp_window_close_later(e->window, editor_free, e);
    return TRUE;
}

static gint prpl_compare(gconstpointer a, gconstpointer b)
{
    const PurplePlugin *pa = *(PurplePlugin *const *)a;
    const PurplePlugin *pb = *(PurplePlugin *const *)b;

    return g_utf8_collate(pa->info->name ? pa->info->name : "", pb->info->name ? pb->info->name : "");
}

void dwp_account_edit(PurpleAccount *account)
{
    Editor *e = g_new0(Editor, 1);
    HWND box, page, buttons, scroll;
    unsigned long pageid;
    GPtrArray *prpls = g_ptr_array_new();
    GList *l;
    guint z;

    e->account = account;
    e->prpl_ids = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(g_free);
    e->prpl_names = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(g_free);
    e->splits = g_array_new(FALSE, TRUE, sizeof(SplitWidget));
    e->options = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(option_widget_free);
    e->last_protocol = -1;

    for(l = purple_plugins_get_protocols(); l; l = l->next)
        g_ptr_array_add(prpls, l->data);
    g_ptr_array_sort(prpls, prpl_compare);
    for(z = 0; z < prpls->len; z++)
    {
        PurplePlugin *p = g_ptr_array_index(prpls, z);

        g_ptr_array_add(e->prpl_ids, g_strdup(p->info->id));
        g_ptr_array_add(e->prpl_names, g_strdup(p->info->name ? p->info->name : p->info->id));
    }
    g_ptr_array_free(prpls, TRUE);

    e->window = dwp_window_new(account ? "Modify Account" : "Add Account", 0, 0);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(e->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    if(account)
        dwp_form_row(box, "Protocol:", dwp_label(purple_account_get_protocol_name(account)));
    else
    {
        int sel = 0;

        for(z = 0; z < e->prpl_ids->len; z++)
            if(purple_strequal(g_ptr_array_index(e->prpl_ids, z), "prpl-jabber"))
                sel = (int)z;
        e->protocol = dw_combobox_new("", 0);
        dwp_combo_fill(e->protocol, (char **)e->prpl_names->pdata, (int)e->prpl_names->len, sel);
        e->last_protocol = sel;
        dwp_form_row(box, "Protocol:", e->protocol);
        dw_signal_connect(e->protocol, DW_SIGNAL_LIST_SELECT, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(editor_protocol_changed), e);
    }

    e->notebook = dw_notebook_new(0, TRUE);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, e->notebook, 480, 320, TRUE, TRUE, 2);

    page = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    pageid = dw_notebook_page_new(e->notebook, 0, FALSE);
    dw_notebook_pack(e->notebook, pageid, page);
    dw_notebook_page_set_text(e->notebook, pageid, "Basic");
    e->basic_holder = page;

    page = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    scroll = dw_scrollbox_new(DW_VERT, 2);
    dw_box_pack_start(page, scroll, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    pageid = dw_notebook_page_new(e->notebook, 0, FALSE);
    dw_notebook_pack(e->notebook, pageid, page);
    dw_notebook_page_set_text(e->notebook, pageid, "Advanced");
    e->adv_holder = scroll;

    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    dwp_button(buttons, account ? "Save" : "Add", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(editor_save), e);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Cancel", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(editor_cancel), e);
    dw_signal_connect(e->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(editor_cancel), e);

    editor_build(e);
    dwp_window_present(e->window, 520, 470);
    if(e->username)
        dw_window_set_focus(e->username);
}

===== END FILE: src/accounts.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/blist.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - buddy list window, blist UI ops and buddy/chat/group dialogs.
 *
 * The list is a Dynamic Windows container (not dw_tree): containers deliver
 * double-click (DW_SIGNAL_ITEM_ENTER) on every platform, trees do not on
 * GTK.  Groups are header rows; double-clicking one collapses/expands it.
 * Each row's data is the PurpleBlistNode it shows.  The container is
 * cleared synchronously whenever a shown node is removed, so a row can
 * never refer to freed memory.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

typedef struct {
    HWND window, menubar_box, list, status_combo, status_msg, status_bar;
    HWND tray_anchor;
    HMENUI menubar, buddies_menu, tools_menu;
    gboolean rebuild_pending, dead, syncing_status;
    GHashTable *shown;            /* nodes currently in the container */
    GHashTable *expanded;         /* contacts showing their buddies */
    guint status_clear;
    gboolean visible;
} BList;

static BList bl;
static int blist_handle;

static const PurpleStatusPrimitive status_prims[] = {
    PURPLE_STATUS_AVAILABLE, PURPLE_STATUS_AWAY, PURPLE_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE,
    PURPLE_STATUS_EXTENDED_AWAY, PURPLE_STATUS_INVISIBLE, PURPLE_STATUS_OFFLINE
};
#define N_STATUS ((int)(sizeof(status_prims) / sizeof(status_prims[0])))
static char *status_labels[N_STATUS];

static void schedule_rebuild(void);

/* ============================================================== helpers */

void dwp_open_im(PurpleAccount *account, const char *name)
{
    PurpleConversation *conv;

    if(!dwp_account_exists(account) || !name || !*name)
        return;
    conv = purple_find_conversation_with_account(PURPLE_CONV_TYPE_IM, name, account);
    if(!conv)
        conv = purple_conversation_new(PURPLE_CONV_TYPE_IM, account, name);
    if(conv)
        purple_conversation_present(conv);
}

static void join_chat_node(PurpleChat *chat)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = purple_chat_get_account(chat);
    PurpleConnection *gc = purple_account_get_connection(account);

    if(!gc)
    {
        purple_notify_error(NULL, "Join Chat", "The account is not connected.", NULL);
        return;
    }
    serv_join_chat(gc, purple_chat_get_components(chat));
}

static int count_shown_buddies(PurpleContact *contact, gboolean show_offline, PurpleBuddy **first)
{
    PurpleBlistNode *cnode = (PurpleBlistNode *)contact, *n;
    gboolean contact_show = purple_blist_node_get_bool(cnode, "show_offline");
    int count = 0;

    if(first)
        *first = NULL;
    for(n = purple_blist_node_get_first_child(cnode); n; n = purple_blist_node_get_sibling_next(n))
    {
        PurpleBuddy *b;

        if(!PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_BUDDY(n) || !PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_VISIBLE(n))
            continue;
        b = (PurpleBuddy *)n;
        if(!purple_account_is_connected(purple_buddy_get_account(b)))
            continue;
        if(!PURPLE_BUDDY_IS_ONLINE(b) && !show_offline && !contact_show &&
           !purple_blist_node_get_bool(n, "show_offline"))
            continue;
        if(first && !*first)
            *first = b;
        count++;
    }
    return count;
}

static gboolean buddy_is_shown(PurpleBuddy *b, gboolean show_offline)
{
    PurpleBlistNode *n = (PurpleBlistNode *)b;
    PurpleBlistNode *cnode = purple_blist_node_get_parent(n);

    if(!PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_VISIBLE(n) || !purple_account_is_connected(purple_buddy_get_account(b)))
        return FALSE;
    return PURPLE_BUDDY_IS_ONLINE(b) || show_offline ||
           purple_blist_node_get_bool(n, "show_offline") ||
           (cnode && purple_blist_node_get_bool(cnode, "show_offline"));
}

static char *buddy_status_text(PurpleBuddy *buddy)
{
    PurplePluginProtocolInfo *info = dwp_account_prpl_info(purple_buddy_get_account(buddy), NULL);
    PurplePresence *presence = purple_buddy_get_presence(buddy);
    PurpleStatusPrimitive prim;
    gboolean idle;
    GString *out = g_string_new(NULL);

    prim = dwp_buddy_primitive(buddy, &idle);
    g_string_append(out, dwp_primitive_label(prim));
    if(info && info->status_text && PURPLE_BUDDY_IS_ONLINE(buddy))
    {
        char *t = info->status_text(buddy);

        if(t && *t)
        {
            char *plain = purple_markup_strip_html(t);
            char *p;

            for(p = plain; *p; p++)
                if(*p == '\n' || *p == '\r' || *p == '\t')
                    *p = ' ';
            g_string_append_printf(out, " - %s", g_strstrip(plain));
            g_free(plain);
        }
        g_free(t);
    }
    if(idle)
    {
        time_t since = purple_presence_get_idle_time(presence);

        if(since > 0)
        {
            long mins = (long)((time(NULL) - since) / 60);

            if(mins > 0)
                g_string_append_printf(out, " (idle %ldm)", mins);
        }
        else
            g_string_append(out, " (idle)");
    }
    return g_string_free(out, FALSE);
}

/* =============================================================== rebuild */

typedef struct {
    char *name, *status;
    HICN icon;
    PurpleBlistNode *node;
} Row;

static void add_row(GPtrArray *rows, const char *name, const char *status, HICN icon, PurpleBlistNode *node)
{
    Row *r = g_new0(Row, 1);

    r->name = g_strdup(name);
    r->status = g_strdup(status ? status : "");
    r->icon = icon;
    r->node = node;
    g_ptr_array_add(rows, r);
}

static void row_free(gpointer p)
{
    Row *r = p;

    g_free(r->name);
    g_free(r->status);
    g_free(r);
}

static void clear_list(void)
{
    if(bl.list)
        dw_container_clear(bl.list, TRUE);
    if(bl.shown)
        g_hash_table_remove_all(bl.shown);
}

static PurpleBlistNode *selected_node(void)
{
    char *data;

    if(!bl.list)
        return NULL;
    data = dw_container_query_start(bl.list, DW_CRA_SELECTED | DW_CR_RETDATA);
    return (PurpleBlistNode *)data;
}

static void rebuild(void)
{
    gboolean show_offline, show_empty;
    PurpleBlistNode *gnode, *selected;
    GPtrArray *rows;
    void *alloc;
    guint z;

    bl.rebuild_pending = FALSE;
    if(bl.dead || !bl.list || !purple_get_blist())
        return;

    show_offline = purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_OFFLINE);
    show_empty = purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_EMPTY);
    selected = selected_node();
    if(selected && !g_hash_table_lookup(bl.shown, selected))
        selected = NULL;

    rows = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(row_free);
    for(gnode = purple_blist_get_root(); gnode; gnode = purple_blist_node_get_sibling_next(gnode))
    {
        PurpleGroup *group;
        GPtrArray *children;
        PurpleBlistNode *n;
        gboolean collapsed;
        char *title;

        if(!PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_GROUP(gnode) || !PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_VISIBLE(gnode))
            continue;
        group = (PurpleGroup *)gnode;
        collapsed = purple_blist_node_get_bool(gnode, "collapsed");
        children = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(row_free);

        for(n = purple_blist_node_get_first_child(gnode); n; n = purple_blist_node_get_sibling_next(n))
        {
            if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CONTACT(n) && PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_VISIBLE(n))
            {
                PurpleContact *contact = (PurpleContact *)n;
                PurpleBuddy *first, *priority;
                int count = count_shown_buddies(contact, show_offline, &first);
                gboolean idle;
                PurpleStatusPrimitive prim;
                char *name, *status;

                if(!count)
                    continue;
                priority = purple_contact_get_priority_buddy(contact);
                if(!priority || !buddy_is_shown(priority, show_offline))
                    priority = first;
                prim = dwp_buddy_primitive(priority, &idle);
                status = buddy_status_text(priority);
                if(count > 1)
                    name = g_strdup_printf("    %s %s", g_hash_table_lookup(bl.expanded, n) ? "-" : "+",
                                           purple_contact_get_alias(contact));
                else
                    name = g_strdup_printf("    %s", purple_contact_get_alias(contact));
                add_row(children, name, status, dwp_status_icon(prim, idle), n);
                g_free(name);
                g_free(status);

                if(count > 1 && g_hash_table_lookup(bl.expanded, n))
                {
                    PurpleBlistNode *b;

                    for(b = purple_blist_node_get_first_child(n); b; b = purple_blist_node_get_sibling_next(b))
                    {
                        PurpleBuddy *buddy;

                        if(!PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_BUDDY(b))
                            continue;
                        buddy = (PurpleBuddy *)b;
                        if(!buddy_is_shown(buddy, show_offline))
                            continue;
                        prim = dwp_buddy_primitive(buddy, &idle);
                        name = g_strdup_printf("          %s [%s]", purple_buddy_get_alias(buddy),
                                               purple_account_get_username(purple_buddy_get_account(buddy)));
                        status = buddy_status_text(buddy);
                        add_row(children, name, status, dwp_status_icon(prim, idle), b);
                        g_free(name);
                        g_free(status);
                    }
                }
            }
            else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CHAT(n) && PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_VISIBLE(n))
            {
                PurpleChat *chat = (PurpleChat *)n;
                char *name;

                if(!purple_account_is_connected(purple_chat_get_account(chat)))
                    continue;
                name = g_strdup_printf("    %s", purple_chat_get_name(chat));
                add_row(children, name, "Chat", dwp_chat_icon(), n);
                g_free(name);
            }
        }
        if(!children->len && !show_empty)
        {
            g_ptr_array_free(children, TRUE);
            continue;
        }
        title = g_strdup_printf("[%s] %s (%d/%d)", collapsed ? "+" : "-",
                                purple_group_get_name(group),
                                purple_blist_get_group_online_count(group),
                                purple_blist_get_group_size(group, FALSE));
        add_row(rows, title, "", 0, gnode);
        g_free(title);
        if(!collapsed)
        {
            for(z = 0; z < children->len; z++)
            {
                Row *r = g_ptr_array_index(children, z);

                add_row(rows, r->name, r->status, r->icon, r->node);
            }
        }
        g_ptr_array_free(children, TRUE);
    }

    clear_list();
    if(rows->len)
    {
        alloc = dw_container_alloc(bl.list, (int)rows->len);
        for(z = 0; z < rows->len; z++)
        {
            Row *r = g_ptr_array_index(rows, z);
            char *status = r->status;

            dw_filesystem_set_file(bl.list, alloc, (int)z, r->name, r->icon);
            dw_filesystem_set_item(bl.list, alloc, 0, (int)z, &status);
            dw_container_set_row_title(alloc, (int)z, r->name);
            dw_container_set_row_data(alloc, (int)z, r->node);
            g_hash_table_insert(bl.shown, r->node, GINT_TO_POINTER(1));
        }
        dw_container_insert(bl.list, alloc, (int)rows->len);
        if(selected && g_hash_table_lookup(bl.shown, selected))
            dw_container_cursor_by_data(bl.list, selected);
    }
    g_ptr_array_free(rows, TRUE);
    dwp_tray_update();
}

static void rebuild_task(gpointer unused)
{
    (void)unused;
    if(bl.rebuild_pending)
        rebuild();
}

static void schedule_rebuild(void)
{
    if(bl.dead || bl.rebuild_pending)
        return;
    bl.rebuild_pending = TRUE;
    dwp_defer(rebuild_task, NULL);
}

/* ====================================================== status selection */

static void sync_status_box(void)
{
    PurpleSavedStatus *saved = purple_savedstatus_get_current();
    PurpleStatusPrimitive prim;
    const char *msg;
    int z;

    if(!saved || !bl.status_combo)
        return;
    bl.syncing_status = TRUE;
    prim = purple_savedstatus_get_type(saved);
    for(z = 0; z < N_STATUS; z++)
    {
        if(status_prims[z] == prim)
        {
            dw_listbox_select(bl.status_combo, z, TRUE);
            dw_window_set_text(bl.status_combo, status_labels[z]);
        }
    }
    msg = purple_savedstatus_get_message(saved);
    if(msg && *msg)
    {
        char *plain = purple_markup_strip_html(msg);

        dw_window_set_text(bl.status_msg, plain);
        g_free(plain);
    }
    else
        dw_window_set_text(bl.status_msg, "");
    bl.syncing_status = FALSE;
}

static void apply_status(void)
{
    int index = dwp_combo_index(bl.status_combo, status_labels, N_STATUS);
    PurpleStatusPrimitive prim = index >= 0 ? status_prims[index] : PURPLE_STATUS_AVAILABLE;
    char *text = dwp_get_text_trimmed(bl.status_msg);
    char *html = *text ? g_markup_escape_text(text, -1) : NULL;
    PurpleSavedStatus *saved;

    saved = purple_savedstatus_find_transient_by_type_and_message(prim, html);
    if(!saved)
    {
        saved = purple_savedstatus_new(NULL, prim);
        purple_savedstatus_set_message(saved, html);
    }
    purple_savedstatus_activate(saved);
    g_free(html);
    g_free(text);
}

void dwp_blist_set_status_primitive(PurpleStatusPrimitive prim)
{
    int z;

    for(z = 0; z < N_STATUS; z++)
    {
        if(status_prims[z] == prim)
        {
            bl.syncing_status = TRUE;
            dw_listbox_select(bl.status_combo, z, TRUE);
            dw_window_set_text(bl.status_combo, status_labels[z]);
            bl.syncing_status = FALSE;
            apply_status();
            return;
        }
    }
}

static int DWSIGNAL status_selected(HWND w, int index, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    (void)index;
    (void)data;
    if(!bl.syncing_status)
        apply_status();
    return FALSE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL status_msg_set(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    if(!bl.syncing_status)
        apply_status();
    return FALSE;
}

static void on_savedstatus_changed(PurpleSavedStatus *now, PurpleSavedStatus *old, gpointer data)
{
    (void)now;
    (void)old;
    (void)data;
    sync_status_box();
    dwp_tray_update();
}

static gboolean status_clear_cb(gpointer data)
{
    (void)data;
    bl.status_clear = 0;
    if(bl.status_bar)
        dw_window_set_text(bl.status_bar, "");
    return FALSE;
}

void dwp_blist_status_message(const char *text)
{
    if(!bl.status_bar)
        return;
    dw_window_set_text(bl.status_bar, text ? text : "");
    if(bl.status_clear)
        purple_timeout_remove(bl.status_clear);
    bl.status_clear = purple_timeout_add_seconds(10, status_clear_cb, NULL);
}

/* ============================================================ menus/node */

typedef struct {
    PurpleCallback cb;
    gpointer data;
} ProtoAct;

static GPtrArray *proto_acts = NULL;

static void proto_action(gpointer node, gpointer act, const char *unused)
{
    ProtoAct *pa = act;
    PurpleCallback cb = pa->cb;
    gpointer data = pa->data;

    (void)unused;
    if(!dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        return;
    ((void (*)(gpointer, gpointer))cb)(node, data);
}

static void add_menu_actions(HMENUI menu, GList *list, PurpleBlistNode *node)
{
    GList *l;

    for(l = list; l; l = l->next)
    {
        PurpleMenuAction *act = l->data;
        char *label;

        if(!act)
        {
            dwp_menu_separator(menu);
            continue;
        }
        label = dwp_mnemonic(act->label);
        if(act->children)
        {
            HMENUI sub = dw_menu_new(0);

            add_menu_actions(sub, act->children, node);
            dw_menu_append_item(menu, label, DW_MENU_POPUP, 0, TRUE, FALSE, sub);
        }
        else if(act->callback)
        {
            ProtoAct *pa = g_new0(ProtoAct, 1);

            pa->cb = act->callback;
            pa->data = act->data;
            g_ptr_array_add(proto_acts, pa);
            dwp_menu_add(menu, label, proto_action, node, pa, NULL);
        }
        else
            dw_menu_append_item(menu, label, DW_MENU_POPUP, 0, TRUE, FALSE, DW_NOMENU);
        g_free(label);
    }
}

static void free_menu_actions(GList *list)
{
    GList *l;

    for(l = list; l; l = l->next)
    {
        PurpleMenuAction *act = l->data;

        if(!act)
            continue;
        free_menu_actions(act->children);
        act->children = NULL;
        purple_menu_action_free(act);
    }
    g_list_free(list);
}

static void reset_popup_state(void)
{
    dwp_actions_reset();
    if(!proto_acts)
        proto_acts = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(g_free);
    g_ptr_array_set_size(proto_acts, 0);
}

static void add_protocol_menus(HMENUI menu, PurpleBlistNode *node, PurpleAccount *account)
{
    PurplePluginProtocolInfo *info = dwp_account_prpl_info(account, NULL);
    GList *ext;

    if(info && info->blist_node_menu)
    {
        GList *actions = info->blist_node_menu(node);

        if(actions)
        {
            dwp_menu_separator(menu);
            add_menu_actions(menu, actions, node);
            free_menu_actions(actions);
        }
    }
    ext = purple_blist_node_get_extended_menu(node);
    if(ext)
    {
        dwp_menu_separator(menu);
        add_menu_actions(menu, ext, node);
        free_menu_actions(ext);
    }
}

static void act_send_im(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    PurpleBuddy *buddy;

    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(!dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        return;
    if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CONTACT((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        buddy = purple_contact_get_priority_buddy((PurpleContact *)node);
    else
        buddy = (PurpleBuddy *)node;
    if(buddy)
        dwp_open_im(purple_buddy_get_account(buddy), purple_buddy_get_name(buddy));
}

static PurpleBuddy *node_buddy(gpointer node)
{
    if(!dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        return NULL;
    if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CONTACT((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        return purple_contact_get_priority_buddy((PurpleContact *)node);
    if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_BUDDY((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        return (PurpleBuddy *)node;
    return NULL;
}

static void act_send_file(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    PurpleBuddy *buddy = node_buddy(node);
    PurpleConnection *gc;

    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(!buddy || !(gc = purple_account_get_connection(purple_buddy_get_account(buddy))))
        return;
    serv_send_file(gc, purple_buddy_get_name(buddy), NULL);
}

static void act_get_info(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    PurpleBuddy *buddy = node_buddy(node);
    PurpleConnection *gc;

    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(!buddy || !(gc = purple_account_get_connection(purple_buddy_get_account(buddy))))
        return;
    serv_get_info(gc, purple_buddy_get_name(buddy));
}

static void act_pounce(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    PurpleBuddy *buddy = node_buddy(node);

    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(buddy)
        dwp_pounce_edit(NULL, purple_buddy_get_account(buddy), purple_buddy_get_name(buddy));
}

static void act_block(gpointer node, gpointer block, const char *s)
{
    PurpleBuddy *buddy = node_buddy(node);

    (void)s;
    if(buddy)
        dwp_privacy_set_blocked(purple_buddy_get_account(buddy), purple_buddy_get_name(buddy),
                                GPOINTER_TO_INT(block));
}

static void alias_done(gpointer node, const char *text)
{
    char *alias;

    if(!dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        return;
    alias = g_strstrip(g_strdup(text ? text : ""));
    if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CONTACT((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        purple_blist_alias_contact((PurpleContact *)node, *alias ? alias : NULL);
    else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_BUDDY((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
    {
        PurpleBuddy *buddy = node;

        purple_blist_alias_buddy(buddy, *alias ? alias : NULL);
        serv_alias_buddy(buddy);
    }
    else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CHAT((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        purple_blist_alias_chat((PurpleChat *)node, *alias ? alias : NULL);
    g_free(alias);
}

static void act_alias(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    const char *current = "";

    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(!dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        return;
    if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CONTACT((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        current = purple_contact_get_alias((PurpleContact *)node);
    else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_BUDDY((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        current = purple_buddy_get_local_buddy_alias((PurpleBuddy *)node);
    else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CHAT((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        current = purple_chat_get_name((PurpleChat *)node);
    dwp_prompt("Alias", "Enter an alias:", current, FALSE, alias_done, node);
}

static void act_show_offline(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(!dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        return;
    purple_blist_node_set_bool(node, "show_offline", !purple_blist_node_get_bool(node, "show_offline"));
    schedule_rebuild();
}

static void act_expand(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(!dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        return;
    if(g_hash_table_lookup(bl.expanded, node))
        g_hash_table_remove(bl.expanded, node);
    else
        g_hash_table_insert(bl.expanded, node, GINT_TO_POINTER(1));
    schedule_rebuild();
}

static void act_move(gpointer node, gpointer group, const char *s)
{
    (void)s;
    if(!dwp_blist_node_exists(node) || !dwp_blist_node_exists(group))
        return;
    if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CONTACT((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        purple_blist_add_contact((PurpleContact *)node, (PurpleGroup *)group, NULL);
    else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_BUDDY((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        purple_blist_add_buddy((PurpleBuddy *)node, NULL, (PurpleGroup *)group, NULL);
    else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CHAT((PurpleBlistNode *)node))
        purple_blist_add_chat((PurpleChat *)node, (PurpleGroup *)group, NULL);
}

static void remove_buddy_from_server(PurpleBuddy *buddy)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = purple_buddy_get_account(buddy);

    if(purple_account_is_connected(account))
        purple_account_remove_buddy(account, buddy, purple_buddy_get_group(buddy));
}

static void act_remove(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    PurpleBlistNode *n = node;

    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(!dwp_blist_node_exists(n))
        return;
    if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CONTACT(n))
    {
        char *name = g_strdup(purple_contact_get_alias((PurpleContact *)n));
        gboolean ok = dwp_confirm("Remove Contact", "Remove %s from your buddy list?", name);
        PurpleBlistNode *c;

        g_free(name);
        if(!ok || !dwp_blist_node_exists(n))
            return;
        for(c = purple_blist_node_get_first_child(n); c; c = purple_blist_node_get_sibling_next(c))
            if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_BUDDY(c))
                remove_buddy_from_server((PurpleBuddy *)c);
        purple_blist_remove_contact((PurpleContact *)n);
    }
    else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_BUDDY(n))
    {
        char *name = g_strdup(purple_buddy_get_alias((PurpleBuddy *)n));
        gboolean ok = dwp_confirm("Remove Buddy", "Remove %s from your buddy list?", name);

        g_free(name);
        if(!ok || !dwp_blist_node_exists(n))
            return;
        remove_buddy_from_server((PurpleBuddy *)n);
        purple_blist_remove_buddy((PurpleBuddy *)n);
    }
    else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CHAT(n))
    {
        char *name = g_strdup(purple_chat_get_name((PurpleChat *)n));
        gboolean ok = dwp_confirm("Remove Chat", "Remove the chat %s from your buddy list?", name);

        g_free(name);
        if(ok && dwp_blist_node_exists(n))
            purple_blist_remove_chat((PurpleChat *)n);
    }
    else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_GROUP(n))
    {
        char *name = g_strdup(purple_group_get_name((PurpleGroup *)n));
        gboolean ok = dwp_confirm("Remove Group", "Remove the group %s and all its buddies and chats?", name);
        PurpleBlistNode *c, *next;
        GList *l;

        g_free(name);
        if(!ok || !dwp_blist_node_exists(n))
            return;
        for(c = purple_blist_node_get_first_child(n); c; c = next)
        {
            next = purple_blist_node_get_sibling_next(c);
            if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CONTACT(c))
            {
                PurpleBlistNode *bn;

                for(bn = purple_blist_node_get_first_child(c); bn; bn = purple_blist_node_get_sibling_next(bn))
                    if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_BUDDY(bn))
                        remove_buddy_from_server((PurpleBuddy *)bn);
                purple_blist_remove_contact((PurpleContact *)c);
            }
            else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CHAT(c))
                purple_blist_remove_chat((PurpleChat *)c);
        }
        for(l = purple_connections_get_all(); l; l = l->next)
        {
            PurpleAccount *account = purple_connection_get_account(l->data);

            if(purple_account_is_connected(account))
                purple_account_remove_group(account, (PurpleGroup *)n);
        }
        purple_blist_remove_group((PurpleGroup *)n);
    }
}

static void act_join(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        join_chat_node(node);
}

static void act_add_buddy_to(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    dwp_show_add_buddy(NULL, NULL, dwp_blist_node_exists(node) ? purple_group_get_name(node) : NULL, NULL);
}

static void act_add_chat_to(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    dwp_show_add_chat(NULL, dwp_blist_node_exists(node) ? purple_group_get_name(node) : NULL, NULL, NULL);
}

static void rename_group_done(gpointer node, const char *text)
{
    char *name;

    if(!dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        return;
    name = g_strstrip(g_strdup(text ? text : ""));
    if(*name)
        purple_blist_rename_group(node, name);
    g_free(name);
}

static void act_rename_group(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        dwp_prompt("Rename Group", "New group name:", purple_group_get_name(node), FALSE,
                   rename_group_done, node);
}

static void add_move_menu(HMENUI menu, PurpleBlistNode *node)
{
    HMENUI move = dw_menu_new(0);
    PurpleBlistNode *g;
    PurpleBlistNode *current = PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_BUDDY(node)
        ? purple_blist_node_get_parent(purple_blist_node_get_parent(node))
        : purple_blist_node_get_parent(node);

    for(g = purple_blist_get_root(); g; g = purple_blist_node_get_sibling_next(g))
    {
        if(!PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_GROUP(g) || g == current)
            continue;
        dwp_menu_add(move, purple_group_get_name((PurpleGroup *)g), act_move, node, g, NULL);
    }
    dw_menu_append_item(menu, "~Move to", DW_MENU_POPUP, 0, TRUE, FALSE, move);
}

static void buddy_menu(HMENUI menu, PurpleBlistNode *node)
{
    PurpleBuddy *buddy = node_buddy(node);
    PurpleAccount *account;
    PurpleConnection *gc;
    PurplePluginProtocolInfo *info;
    gboolean blocked;
    int count;

    if(!buddy)
        return;
    account = purple_buddy_get_account(buddy);
    info = dwp_account_prpl_info(account, &gc);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "Send ~IM", act_send_im, node, NULL, NULL);
    if(info && info->send_file &&
       (!info->can_receive_file || info->can_receive_file(gc, purple_buddy_get_name(buddy))))
        dwp_menu_add(menu, "Send ~File...", act_send_file, node, NULL, NULL);
    if(info && info->get_info)
        dwp_menu_add(menu, "Get ~Info", act_get_info, node, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "Add Buddy ~Pounce...", act_pounce, node, NULL, NULL);
    blocked = dwp_privacy_is_blocked(account, purple_buddy_get_name(buddy));
    dwp_menu_add(menu, blocked ? "Un~block" : "~Block", act_block, node, GINT_TO_POINTER(!blocked), NULL);
    dwp_menu_separator(menu);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "~Alias...", act_alias, node, NULL, NULL);
    if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CONTACT(node))
    {
        count = count_shown_buddies((PurpleContact *)node, TRUE, NULL);
        if(count > 1)
            dwp_menu_add(menu, g_hash_table_lookup(bl.expanded, node) ? "~Collapse" : "~Expand",
                         act_expand, node, NULL, NULL);
    }
    dwp_menu_add(menu, purple_blist_node_get_bool(node, "show_offline")
                       ? "Don't Show When ~Offline" : "Show When ~Offline",
                 act_show_offline, node, NULL, NULL);
    add_move_menu(menu, node);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "~Remove", act_remove, node, NULL, NULL);
    add_protocol_menus(menu, (PurpleBlistNode *)buddy, account);
}

static void group_menu(HMENUI menu, PurpleBlistNode *node)
{
    GList *ext;

    dwp_menu_add(menu, "Add ~Buddy...", act_add_buddy_to, node, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "Add C~hat...", act_add_chat_to, node, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "Re~name...", act_rename_group, node, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "~Remove...", act_remove, node, NULL, NULL);
    ext = purple_blist_node_get_extended_menu(node);
    if(ext)
    {
        dwp_menu_separator(menu);
        add_menu_actions(menu, ext, node);
        free_menu_actions(ext);
    }
}

static void act_autojoin(gpointer node, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b;
    (void)s;
    if(dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        purple_blist_node_set_bool(node, "gtk-autojoin", !purple_blist_node_get_bool(node, "gtk-autojoin"));
}

static void chat_menu(HMENUI menu, PurpleBlistNode *node)
{
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "~Join", act_join, node, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "~Alias...", act_alias, node, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, purple_blist_node_get_bool(node, "gtk-autojoin") ? "Don't Auto-~Join" : "Auto-~Join",
                 act_autojoin, node, NULL, NULL);
    add_move_menu(menu, node);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "~Remove", act_remove, node, NULL, NULL);
    add_protocol_menus(menu, node, purple_chat_get_account((PurpleChat *)node));
}

static void generic_add_menu(HMENUI menu)
{
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "Add ~Buddy...", act_add_buddy_to, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "Add C~hat...", act_add_chat_to, NULL, NULL, NULL);
}

static int DWSIGNAL list_context(HWND w, char *text, int x, int y, void *data, void *itemdata)
{
    PurpleBlistNode *node = itemdata;
    HMENUI menu;

    (void)w;
    (void)text;
    (void)data;
    dwp_note_activity();
    reset_popup_state();
    menu = dw_menu_new(0);
    if(node && g_hash_table_lookup(bl.shown, node) && dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
    {
        if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_GROUP(node))
            group_menu(menu, node);
        else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CHAT(node))
            chat_menu(menu, node);
        else
            buddy_menu(menu, node);
    }
    else
        generic_add_menu(menu);
    dw_menu_popup(&menu, bl.window, x, y);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL list_enter(HWND w, char *text, void *data, void *itemdata)
{
    PurpleBlistNode *node = itemdata;

    (void)w;
    (void)text;
    (void)data;
    dwp_note_activity();
    if(!node || !g_hash_table_lookup(bl.shown, node) || !dwp_blist_node_exists(node))
        return FALSE;
    if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_GROUP(node))
    {
        purple_blist_node_set_bool(node, "collapsed", !purple_blist_node_get_bool(node, "collapsed"));
        schedule_rebuild();
    }
    else if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_CHAT(node))
        join_chat_node((PurpleChat *)node);
    else
        act_send_im(node, NULL, NULL);
    return TRUE;
}

/* =============================================================== menubar */

static void menu_new_im(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_show_new_im(); }
static void menu_join_chat(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_show_join_chat(); }
static void menu_get_info(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_show_get_info(); }
static void menu_roomlist(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_roomlist_show(NULL, FALSE); }
static void menu_add_buddy(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_show_add_buddy(NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL); }
static void menu_add_chat(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_show_add_chat(NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL); }
static void menu_add_group(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_show_add_group(); }
static void menu_quit(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_core_request_quit(); }
static void menu_accounts(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_accounts_show(); }
static void menu_account_actions(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_accounts_popup(bl.window); }
static void menu_pounces(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_pounces_show(); }
static void menu_privacy(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_privacy_show(NULL); }
static void menu_plugins(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_plugins_show(); }
static void menu_prefs(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_prefs_show(); }
static void menu_xfers(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_xfers_show(); }
static void menu_debug(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_debug_show(); }

static void menu_show_offline(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    gboolean on = !purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_OFFLINE);

    (void)a; (void)b; (void)s;
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_OFFLINE, on);
    dwp_menu_set_state(bl.buddies_menu, DWP_MID_SHOW_OFFLINE, on ? DW_MIS_CHECKED : DW_MIS_UNCHECKED);
    schedule_rebuild();
}

static void menu_show_empty(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    gboolean on = !purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_EMPTY);

    (void)a; (void)b; (void)s;
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_EMPTY, on);
    dwp_menu_set_state(bl.buddies_menu, DWP_MID_SHOW_EMPTY, on ? DW_MIS_CHECKED : DW_MIS_UNCHECKED);
    schedule_rebuild();
}

static void menu_mute(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)a; (void)b; (void)s;
    dwp_sound_set_muted(!dwp_sound_muted());
    dwp_blist_sync_mute();
}

void dwp_blist_sync_mute(void)
{
    if(bl.tools_menu)
        dwp_menu_set_state(bl.tools_menu, DWP_MID_MUTE,
                           dwp_sound_muted() ? DW_MIS_CHECKED : DW_MIS_UNCHECKED);
}

static void menu_about(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    DWEnv env;
    char *text;

    (void)a; (void)b; (void)s;
    dw_environment_query(&env);
    text = g_strdup_printf("%s %s\n\nAn instant messenger built on libpurple %s and "
                           "Dynamic Windows %d.%d.%d (%s).\n\nLicensed under the GNU GPL v2 or later.",
                           DWP_NAME, DWP_VERSION, purple_core_get_version(),
                           env.DWMajorVersion, env.DWMinorVersion, env.DWSubVersion, env.osName);
    dwp_message("About " DWP_NAME, text, NULL);
    g_free(text);
}

static void build_menubar(void)
{
    HMENUI menu;

    bl.menubar = dw_menubar_new(bl.window);

    menu = dw_menu_new(0);
    bl.buddies_menu = menu;
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "New ~Instant Message...", 0, FALSE, menu_new_im, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Join a ~Chat...", 0, FALSE, menu_join_chat, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Get User ~Info...", 0, FALSE, menu_get_info, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "~Room List...", 0, FALSE, menu_roomlist, NULL);
    dwp_menu_separator(menu);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Show ~Offline Buddies", DWP_MID_SHOW_OFFLINE, TRUE, menu_show_offline, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Show ~Empty Groups", DWP_MID_SHOW_EMPTY, TRUE, menu_show_empty, NULL);
    dwp_menu_separator(menu);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "~Add Buddy...", 0, FALSE, menu_add_buddy, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Add C~hat...", 0, FALSE, menu_add_chat, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Add ~Group...", 0, FALSE, menu_add_group, NULL);
    dwp_menu_separator(menu);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "~Quit", 0, FALSE, menu_quit, NULL);
    dw_menu_append_item(bl.menubar, "~Buddies", DW_MENU_AUTO, 0, TRUE, FALSE, menu);

    menu = dw_menu_new(0);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "~Manage Accounts", 0, FALSE, menu_accounts, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Account ~Actions...", 0, FALSE, menu_account_actions, NULL);
    dw_menu_append_item(bl.menubar, "~Accounts", DW_MENU_AUTO, 0, TRUE, FALSE, menu);

    menu = dw_menu_new(0);
    bl.tools_menu = menu;
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Buddy ~Pounces", 0, FALSE, menu_pounces, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Pri~vacy", 0, FALSE, menu_privacy, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Plu~gins", 0, FALSE, menu_plugins, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "Pr~eferences", 0, FALSE, menu_prefs, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "File ~Transfers", 0, FALSE, menu_xfers, NULL);
    dwp_menu_separator(menu);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "~Mute Sounds", DWP_MID_MUTE, TRUE, menu_mute, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "~Debug Window", 0, FALSE, menu_debug, NULL);
    dw_menu_append_item(bl.menubar, "~Tools", DW_MENU_AUTO, 0, TRUE, FALSE, menu);

    menu = dw_menu_new(0);
    dwp_menu_add_static(menu, "~About", 0, FALSE, menu_about, NULL);
    dw_menu_append_item(bl.menubar, "~Help", DW_MENU_AUTO, 0, TRUE, FALSE, menu);
}

/* ================================================================ window */

static int DWSIGNAL blist_delete(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    dwp_blist_save_geometry();
    /* With a tray icon the buddy list only hides; Quit exits. */
    if(dwp_tray_shown())
    {
        dw_window_hide(bl.window);
        bl.visible = FALSE;
    }
    else
        dwp_core_request_quit();
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL tray_button(HWND w, int x, int y, int button, void *data);

void dwp_blist_create(void)
{
    HWND box, row;
    int z;
    unsigned long flags[1] = { DW_CFA_STRING | DW_CFA_LEFT | DW_CFA_HORZSEPARATOR | DW_CFA_SEPARATOR };
    char *titles[1] = { "Status" };
    char *title;

    if(bl.window)
        return;
    bl.shown = g_hash_table_new(g_direct_hash, g_direct_equal);
    bl.expanded = g_hash_table_new(g_direct_hash, g_direct_equal);
    for(z = 0; z < N_STATUS; z++)
        status_labels[z] = (char *)dwp_primitive_label(status_prims[z]);

    title = g_strdup_printf("Buddy List - %s", DWP_NAME);
    bl.window = dwp_window_new(title, 300, 560);
    g_free(title);
    build_menubar();

    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 2);
    dw_box_pack_start(bl.window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);

    bl.list = dw_container_new(0, FALSE);
    dw_filesystem_set_column_title(bl.list, "Buddy");
    dw_filesystem_setup(bl.list, flags, titles, 1);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, bl.list, 250, 400, TRUE, TRUE, 2);
    dw_signal_connect(bl.list, DW_SIGNAL_ITEM_ENTER, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(list_enter), NULL);
    dw_signal_connect(bl.list, DW_SIGNAL_ITEM_CONTEXT, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(list_context), NULL);

    row = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, row, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    bl.status_msg = dw_entryfield_new("", 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(row, bl.status_msg, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    {
        HWND set = dwp_button(row, "Set", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(status_msg_set), NULL);

        dw_window_set_tooltip(set, "Set the status message");
        dw_window_set_tooltip(bl.status_msg, "Status message (press Enter to set)");
        dw_window_click_default(bl.status_msg, set);
    }

    bl.status_combo = dw_combobox_new(status_labels[0], 0);
    for(z = 0; z < N_STATUS; z++)
        dw_listbox_append(bl.status_combo, status_labels[z]);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, bl.status_combo, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    dw_signal_connect(bl.status_combo, DW_SIGNAL_LIST_SELECT, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(status_selected), NULL);

    bl.status_bar = dw_status_text_new("", 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, bl.status_bar, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);

    /* Invisible anchor that receives tray icon clicks. */
    bl.tray_anchor = dw_render_new(0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, bl.tray_anchor, 1, 1, FALSE, FALSE, 0);
    dw_signal_connect(bl.tray_anchor, DW_SIGNAL_BUTTON_PRESS, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(tray_button), NULL);

    dw_signal_connect(bl.window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(blist_delete), NULL);
    /* Application-level close (macOS); ignored elsewhere. */
    dw_signal_connect(DW_DESKTOP, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(blist_delete), NULL);

    purple_signal_connect(purple_savedstatuses_get_handle(), "savedstatus-changed", &blist_handle,
                          PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_savedstatus_changed), NULL);
}

void dwp_blist_apply_prefs(void)
{
    int w = purple_prefs_get_int(DWP_PREF_BLIST_W), h = purple_prefs_get_int(DWP_PREF_BLIST_H);

    if(w > 100 && h > 100)
        dw_window_set_size(bl.window, w, h);
    dwp_menu_set_state(bl.buddies_menu, DWP_MID_SHOW_OFFLINE,
                       purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_OFFLINE) ? DW_MIS_CHECKED : DW_MIS_UNCHECKED);
    dwp_menu_set_state(bl.buddies_menu, DWP_MID_SHOW_EMPTY,
                       purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_EMPTY) ? DW_MIS_CHECKED : DW_MIS_UNCHECKED);
    dwp_blist_sync_mute();
    sync_status_box();
    schedule_rebuild();
}

void dwp_blist_save_geometry(void)
{
    long x = 0, y = 0;
    unsigned long w = 0, h = 0;

    if(!bl.window || !bl.visible)
        return;
    dw_window_get_pos_size(bl.window, &x, &y, &w, &h);
    if(w > 100 && h > 100)
    {
        purple_prefs_set_int(DWP_PREF_BLIST_W, (int)w);
        purple_prefs_set_int(DWP_PREF_BLIST_H, (int)h);
    }
}

void dwp_blist_present(void)
{
    if(!bl.window)
        return;
    dw_window_show(bl.window);
    dw_window_raise(bl.window);
    bl.visible = TRUE;
}

void dwp_blist_toggle(void)
{
    if(bl.visible)
    {
        dwp_blist_save_geometry();
        dw_window_hide(bl.window);
        bl.visible = FALSE;
    }
    else
        dwp_blist_present();
}

gboolean dwp_blist_visible(void)
{
    return bl.visible;
}

/* Tray anchor clicks are forwarded to tray.c. */
static int DWSIGNAL tray_button(HWND w, int x, int y, int button, void *data)
{
