    char *user, *alias;
    PurpleAccountRequestAuthorizationCb authorize_cb, deny_cb;
    void *user_data;
    gboolean done;
} AuthBox;

static GList *auth_boxes = NULL;

static void auth_box_free(gpointer p)
{
    AuthBox *box = p;

    g_free(box->user);
    g_free(box->alias);
    g_free(box);
}

static void auth_finish(AuthBox *box, gboolean authorized)
{
    gboolean want_add;
    PurpleAccount *account = box->account;
    char *user, *alias;

    if(box->done || !g_list_find(auth_boxes, box))
        return;
    box->done = TRUE;
    auth_boxes = g_list_remove(auth_boxes, box);
    want_add = authorized && box->addcheck && dw_checkbox_get(box->addcheck);
    user = g_strdup(box->user);
    alias = g_strdup(box->alias);
    /* libpurple frees its request record inside these callbacks. */
    if(authorized)
    {
        if(box->authorize_cb)
            box->authorize_cb(box->user_data);
    }
    else if(box->deny_cb)
        box->deny_cb(box->user_data);
    dwp_window_close_later(box->window, auth_box_free, box);
    if(want_add && dwp_account_exists(account))
        dwp_show_add_buddy(account, user, NULL, alias);
    g_free(user);
    g_free(alias);
}

static int DWSIGNAL auth_authorize(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    auth_finish(data, TRUE);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL auth_deny(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    auth_finish(data, FALSE);
    return TRUE;
}

static void *request_authorize(PurpleAccount *account, const char *remote_user, const char *id,
                               const char *alias, const char *message, gboolean on_list,
                               PurpleAccountRequestAuthorizationCb authorize_cb,
                               PurpleAccountRequestAuthorizationCb deny_cb, void *user_data)
{
    AuthBox *box = g_new0(AuthBox, 1);
    char *who = who_string(remote_user, alias);
    char *text = g_strdup_printf("%s wants to add %s to their buddy list.", who,
                                 id && *id ? id : purple_account_get_username(account));
    HWND vbox, buttons, authb;

    box->account = account;
    box->user = g_strdup(remote_user);
    box->alias = g_strdup(alias);
    box->authorize_cb = authorize_cb;
    box->deny_cb = deny_cb;
    box->user_data = user_data;
    box->window = dwp_window_new("Authorize buddy?", 0, 0);
    vbox = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(box->window, vbox, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(vbox, dwp_label(text), 420, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 4);
    if(message && *message)
    {
        char *m = g_strdup_printf("Message: %s", message);

        dw_box_pack_start(vbox, dwp_label(m), 420, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 4);
        g_free(m);
    }
    if(!on_list)
    {
        box->addcheck = dw_checkbox_new("Add to my buddy list", 0);
        dw_checkbox_set(box->addcheck, TRUE);
        dw_box_pack_start(vbox, box->addcheck, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 4);
    }
    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(vbox, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    authb = dwp_button(buttons, "Authorize", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(auth_authorize), box);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Deny", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(auth_deny), box);
    dw_signal_connect(box->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(auth_deny), box);
    auth_boxes = g_list_append(auth_boxes, box);
    dwp_window_present(box->window, 460, 170);
    dw_window_set_focus(authb);
    g_free(text);
    g_free(who);
    return box;
}

static void close_account_request(void *ui_handle)
{
    AuthBox *box = ui_handle;

    if(!g_list_find(auth_boxes, box))
        return;
    auth_boxes = g_list_remove(auth_boxes, box);
    box->done = TRUE;
    dwp_window_close_later(box->window, auth_box_free, box);
}

static PurpleAccountUiOps *account_ui_ops(void)
{
    static PurpleAccountUiOps ops;
    static gboolean ready = FALSE;

    if(!ready)
    {
        memset(&ops, 0, sizeof(ops));
        ops.notify_added = notify_added;
        ops.request_add = request_add;
        ops.request_authorize = request_authorize;
        ops.close_account_request = close_account_request;
        ready = TRUE;
    }
    return &ops;
}

/* ======================================================== connection ops */

static void connect_progress(PurpleConnection *gc, const char *text, size_t step, size_t step_count)
{
    char *name = dwp_account_display_name(purple_connection_get_account(gc));
    char *msg;

    if(step_count > 0)
        msg = g_strdup_printf("%s: %s (%lu/%lu)", name, text ? text : "",
                              (unsigned long)step + 1, (unsigned long)step_count);
    else
        msg = g_strdup_printf("%s: %s", name, text ? text : "");
    dwp_blist_status_message(msg);
    g_free(msg);
    g_free(name);
}

static void connected(PurpleConnection *gc)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = purple_connection_get_account(gc);
    char *name = dwp_account_display_name(account);
    char *msg = g_strdup_printf("%s: connected", name);

    cancel_reconnect(account);
    error_box_close(account);
    dwp_blist_status_message(msg);
    g_free(msg);
    g_free(name);
}

static void disconnected(PurpleConnection *gc)
{
    (void)gc;
}

static int DWSIGNAL error_reconnect(HWND w, void *data)
{
    ErrorBox *box = data;
    PurpleAccount *account = box->account;

    (void)w;
    error_box_close(account);
    if(dwp_account_exists(account))
    {
        if(!purple_account_get_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID))
            purple_account_set_enabled(account, DWP_UI_ID, TRUE);
        dwp_core_reconnect_now(account);
    }
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL error_modify(HWND w, void *data)
{
    ErrorBox *box = data;
    PurpleAccount *account = box->account;

    (void)w;
    error_box_close(account);
    if(dwp_account_exists(account))
        dwp_account_edit(account);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL error_close(HWND w, void *data)
{
    ErrorBox *box = data;

    (void)w;
    error_box_close(box->account);
    return TRUE;
}

static void show_fatal_error(PurpleAccount *account, const char *text)
{
    ErrorBox *box;
    HWND vbox, buttons;
    char *name, *primary;

    if(!error_boxes)
        error_boxes = g_hash_table_new(g_direct_hash, g_direct_equal);
    error_box_close(account);
    box = g_new0(ErrorBox, 1);
    box->account = account;
    name = dwp_account_display_name(account);
    primary = g_strdup_printf("%s disconnected", name);
    box->window = dwp_window_new("Connection error", 0, 0);
    vbox = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(box->window, vbox, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(vbox, dwp_label(primary), 440, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(vbox, dwp_label(text), 440, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(vbox, dwp_label("The account will not reconnect automatically."),
                      440, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 4);
    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(vbox, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Reconnect", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(error_reconnect), box);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Modify Account", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(error_modify), box);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(error_close), box);
    dw_signal_connect(box->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(error_close), box);
    g_hash_table_insert(error_boxes, account, box);
    dwp_window_present(box->window, 480, 170);
    g_free(primary);
    g_free(name);
}

static void report_disconnect_reason(PurpleConnection *gc, PurpleConnectionError reason, const char *text)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = purple_connection_get_account(gc);
    char *name = dwp_account_display_name(account);
    char *msg = g_strdup_printf("%s: %s", name, text ? text : "");

    dwp_blist_status_message(msg);
    g_free(msg);
    g_free(name);
    if(shutting_down)
        return;
    if(!purple_connection_error_is_fatal(reason))
        schedule_reconnect(account);
    else
        show_fatal_error(account, text ? text : "");
}

static void network_connected(void)
{
    GList *pending, *l;

    if(!reconnects)
        return;
    pending = g_hash_table_get_keys(reconnects);
    for(l = pending; l; l = l->next)
        do_reconnect(l->data);
    g_list_free(pending);
}

static void network_disconnected(void)
{
    GList *active = purple_accounts_get_all_active(), *l;

    for(l = active; l; l = l->next)
    {
        PurpleAccount *account = l->data;
        char *password;

        if(purple_account_is_disconnected(account))
            continue;
        /* Keep the password across the forced disconnect. */
        password = g_strdup(purple_account_get_password(account));
        purple_account_disconnect(account);
        purple_account_set_password(account, password);
        g_free(password);
        schedule_reconnect(account);
    }
    g_list_free(active);
}

static PurpleConnectionUiOps *connection_ui_ops(void)
{
    static PurpleConnectionUiOps ops;
    static gboolean ready = FALSE;

    if(!ready)
    {
        memset(&ops, 0, sizeof(ops));
        ops.connect_progress = connect_progress;
        ops.connected = connected;
        ops.disconnected = disconnected;
        ops.network_connected = network_connected;
        ops.network_disconnected = network_disconnected;
        ops.report_disconnect_reason = report_disconnect_reason;
        ready = TRUE;
    }
    return &ops;
}

/* ================================================================ public */

gboolean dwp_core_init(const DwpOptions *options, char **error)
{
    char *dir, *plugins;

    if(options->config_dir && *options->config_dir)
        dir = g_strdup(options->config_dir);
    else
        dir = g_build_filename(g_get_home_dir(), ".dwpurple", NULL);
    g_mkdir_with_parents(dir, 0700);
    purple_util_set_user_dir(dir);
    g_free(dir);

    purple_debug_set_enabled(options->debug ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_core_set_ui_ops(core_ui_ops());
    purple_eventloop_set_ui_ops(dwp_eventloop_ui_ops());
    dwp_eventloop_start();
    dwp_tasks_start();

    plugins = g_build_filename(purple_user_dir(), "plugins", NULL);
    purple_plugins_add_search_path(plugins);
    g_free(plugins);

    if(!purple_core_init(DWP_UI_ID))
    {
        if(error)
            *error = g_strdup("libpurple initialization failed.");
        return FALSE;
    }
    if(!purple_core_ensure_single_instance())
    {
        if(error)
            *error = g_strdup("Another libpurple client is already running with this configuration directory.");
        purple_core_quit();
        return FALSE;
    }

    purple_set_blist(purple_blist_new());
    purple_blist_load();
    purple_prefs_load();
    purple_prefs_update_old();
    purple_plugins_load_saved(DWP_PREF_LOADED);
    purple_pounces_load();

    dwp_blist_apply_prefs();
    purple_blist_show();
    dwp_tray_apply_prefs();

    if(options->no_login)
    {
        PurpleSavedStatus *off =
            purple_savedstatus_find_transient_by_type_and_message(PURPLE_STATUS_OFFLINE, NULL);

        if(!off)
            off = purple_savedstatus_new(NULL, PURPLE_STATUS_OFFLINE);
        purple_savedstatus_activate(off);
    }
    else
    {
        purple_savedstatus_activate(purple_savedstatus_get_startup());
        purple_accounts_restore_current_statuses();
    }

    if(!purple_accounts_get_all())
        dwp_accounts_show();
    return TRUE;
}

void dwp_core_request_quit(void)
{
    dw_main_quit();
}

gboolean dwp_core_shutting_down(void)
{
    return shutting_down;
}

void dwp_core_shutdown(void)
{
    GList *pending, *l;

    if(shutting_down)
        return;
    shutting_down = TRUE;
    if(reconnects)
    {
        pending = g_hash_table_get_keys(reconnects);
        for(l = pending; l; l = l->next)
            cancel_reconnect(l->data);
        g_list_free(pending);
    }
    purple_signals_disconnect_by_handle(&signal_handle);
    dwp_blist_save_geometry();
    dwp_conv_save_geometry();
    dwp_tray_shutdown();
    purple_core_quit();
    dwp_idle_shutdown();
    dwp_tasks_stop();
    dwp_eventloop_shutdown();
}

===== END FILE: src/core.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/debugwin.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - debug window (libpurple debug UI ops).
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

#define MAX_DEBUG_CHARS 120000

typedef struct {
    HWND window, mle, level, pause;
    gboolean visible;
    unsigned int pos;
    unsigned long chars;
} DebugWin;

static DebugWin dbg;
static GThread *main_thread = NULL;
static char *level_labels[] = { "All", "Misc", "Info", "Warning", "Error", "Fatal" };

static const char *level_name(PurpleDebugLevel level)
{
    switch(level)
    {
        case PURPLE_DEBUG_MISC:    return "misc";
        case PURPLE_DEBUG_INFO:    return "info";
        case PURPLE_DEBUG_WARNING: return "warning";
        case PURPLE_DEBUG_ERROR:   return "error";
        case PURPLE_DEBUG_FATAL:   return "fatal";
        default:                   return "all";
    }
}

static PurpleDebugLevel min_level(void)
{
    int index = dwp_combo_index(dbg.level, level_labels, 6);

    return index > 0 ? (PurpleDebugLevel)index : PURPLE_DEBUG_ALL;
}

static void debug_print(PurpleDebugLevel level, const char *category, const char *arg_s)
{
    char ts[32], *line, **parts, *crlf;
    time_t now;
    struct tm *tm;

    /* Widgets may only be touched from the GUI thread. */
    if(!dbg.window || !dbg.visible || g_thread_self() != main_thread)
        return;
    if(dw_checkbox_get(dbg.pause) || level < min_level())
        return;
    now = time(NULL);
    tm = localtime(&now);
    if(!tm || !strftime(ts, sizeof(ts), "%H:%M:%S", tm))
        strcpy(ts, "--:--:--");
    line = g_strdup_printf("(%s) %s%s%s: %s", ts, category ? category : "",
                           category ? " " : "", level_name(level), arg_s ? arg_s : "");
    if(!g_utf8_validate(line, -1, NULL))
    {
        char *fixed = purple_utf8_salvage(line);

        g_free(line);
        line = fixed;
    }
    g_strchomp(line);
    parts = g_strsplit(line, "\n", -1);
    crlf = g_strjoinv("\r\n", parts);
    g_strfreev(parts);
    g_free(line);
    if(dbg.chars > MAX_DEBUG_CHARS)
    {
        dw_mle_clear(dbg.mle);
        dbg.pos = 0;
        dbg.chars = 0;
    }
    dbg.pos = dw_mle_import(dbg.mle, crlf, dbg.pos);
    dbg.pos = dw_mle_import(dbg.mle, "\r\n", dbg.pos);
    dbg.chars += strlen(crlf) + 2;
    dw_mle_set_cursor(dbg.mle, dbg.pos);
    g_free(crlf);
}

static gboolean debug_is_enabled(PurpleDebugLevel level, const char *category)
{
    (void)level;
    (void)category;
    return dbg.window && dbg.visible;
}

PurpleDebugUiOps *dwp_debug_ui_ops(void)
{
    static PurpleDebugUiOps ops;
    static gboolean ready = FALSE;

    if(!ready)
    {
        main_thread = g_thread_self();
        memset(&ops, 0, sizeof(ops));
        ops.print = debug_print;
        ops.is_enabled = debug_is_enabled;
        ready = TRUE;
    }
    return &ops;
}

static int DWSIGNAL debug_clear(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    dw_mle_clear(dbg.mle);
    dbg.pos = 0;
    dbg.chars = 0;
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL debug_copy(HWND w, void *data)
{
    char *text = dwp_mle_get_text(dbg.mle);

    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    dw_clipboard_set_text(text, (int)strlen(text));
    g_free(text);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL debug_close(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    dw_window_hide(dbg.window);
    dbg.visible = FALSE;
    return TRUE;
}

void dwp_debug_show(void)
{
    HWND box, row;

    if(!dbg.window)
    {
        dbg.window = dwp_window_new("Debug Window", 0, 0);
        box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 2);
        dw_box_pack_start(dbg.window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
        row = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
        dw_box_pack_start(box, row, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
        dw_box_pack_start(row, dwp_label("Level:"), DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, FALSE, TRUE, 2);
        dbg.level = dw_combobox_new("", 0);
        dwp_combo_fill(dbg.level, level_labels, 6, 0);
        dw_box_pack_start(row, dbg.level, 120, DW_SIZE_AUTO, FALSE, FALSE, 2);
        dbg.pause = dw_checkbox_new("Pause", 0);
        dw_box_pack_start(row, dbg.pause, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, FALSE, FALSE, 2);
        dwp_spacer(row);
        dwp_button(row, "Copy", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(debug_copy), NULL);
        dwp_button(row, "Clear", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(debug_clear), NULL);
        dwp_button(row, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(debug_close), NULL);
        dbg.mle = dw_mle_new(0);
        dw_mle_set_editable(dbg.mle, FALSE);
        dw_mle_set_word_wrap(dbg.mle, TRUE);
        dw_box_pack_start(box, dbg.mle, 600, 360, TRUE, TRUE, 2);
        dw_signal_connect(dbg.window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(debug_close), NULL);
        dwp_window_present(dbg.window, 720, 460);
    }
    else
    {
        dw_window_show(dbg.window);
        dw_window_raise(dbg.window);
    }
    dbg.visible = TRUE;
}

===== END FILE: src/debugwin.c =====

/*
 * DWPurple - a Dynamic Windows front end for libpurple
 *
 * This program is free software; you can redistribute it and/or modify
 * it under the terms of the GNU General Public License as published by
 * the Free Software Foundation; either version 2 of the License, or
 * (at your option) any later version.
 *
 * Common definitions shared by all modules.
 *
 * Portability notes:
 *  - HWND/HMENUI/HICN are integers on OS/2 and pointers elsewhere, so they
 *    are only ever compared with 0 / DW_NOHWND and never stored as void *.
 *  - Every Dynamic Windows callback is declared with DWSIGNAL (the OS/2
 *    calling convention differs from the C default).
 */
#ifndef DWPURPLE_H
#define DWPURPLE_H

/* dw.h first: some ports define TRUE/FALSE unconditionally, glib only
 * when they are not yet defined. */
#include <dw.h>

#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <time.h>

#define DWP_NAME            "DWPurple"
#define DWP_VERSION         "0.1.0"
#define DWP_WEBSITE         "https://github.com/"
#define DWP_APP_ID          "org.dwpurple.dwpurple"

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ preferences */

 * DWPurple - message display helpers: HTML -> text, ":code:" emoji and
 * reaction-notice detection (bridged networks send "Reacted with :x:").
 */

#include <glib/gstdio.h>

    const char *code;

static GHashTable *builtin_map = NULL;
static GHashTable *user_map = NULL;
static char *user_path = NULL;
static time_t user_mtime = 0;
static long user_size = -1;

void dwp_emoji_add_prefs(void)
{
    purple_prefs_add_bool(DWP_PREF_SHOW_REACTIONS, TRUE);
#ifdef __OS2__
    /* Most OS/2 code pages cannot show emoji. */
    purple_prefs_add_bool(DWP_PREF_EMOJI_CODES, FALSE);
#else
    purple_prefs_add_bool(DWP_PREF_EMOJI_CODES, TRUE);
#endif
}

char *dwp_emoji_user_map_path(void)
{
    return g_build_filename(purple_user_dir(), "emoji.txt", NULL);
}

static void ensure_builtin(void)
{
    size_t z;

    if(builtin_map)
        return;
    builtin_map = g_hash_table_new(g_str_hash, g_str_equal);
    for(z = 0; z < sizeof(builtin) / sizeof(builtin[0]); z++)
        g_hash_table_insert(builtin_map, (gpointer)builtin[z].code, (gpointer)builtin[z].glyph);
}

/* Reload <config dir>/emoji.txt ("code = glyph" lines) when it changes. */
static void refresh_user_map(void)
{
    char *path = dwp_emoji_user_map_path();
    GStatBuf st;
    gboolean exists = g_stat(path, &st) == 0;
    time_t mtime = exists ? st.st_mtime : 0;
    long size = exists ? (long)st.st_size : -1;
    char *contents = NULL;
    char **lines;
    int z;

    if(user_map && purple_strequal(path, user_path) && mtime == user_mtime && size == user_size)
    {
        g_free(path);
        return;
    }
    g_free(user_path);
    user_path = path;
    user_mtime = mtime;
    user_size = size;
    if(user_map)
        g_hash_table_destroy(user_map);
    user_map = g_hash_table_new_full(g_str_hash, g_str_equal, g_free, g_free);
    if(!exists || !g_file_get_contents(path, &contents, NULL, NULL))
        return;
    lines = g_strsplit(contents, "\n", -1);
    for(z = 0; lines[z]; z++)
    {
        char *line = g_strstrip(lines[z]), *eq, *code, *value;
        size_t len;

        if(!*line || *line == '#' || !(eq = strchr(line, '=')) || eq == line)
            continue;
        *eq = '\0';
        code = g_strstrip(line);
        value = g_strstrip(eq + 1);
        len = strlen(code);
        if(len > 2 && code[0] == ':' && code[len - 1] == ':')
        {
            code[len - 1] = '\0';
            code++;
        }
        if(*code && *value && g_utf8_validate(value, -1, NULL))
            g_hash_table_replace(user_map, g_ascii_strdown(code, -1), g_strdup(value));
    }
    g_strfreev(lines);
    g_free(contents);
}

const char *dwp_emoji_lookup(const char *code)
{
    char *key;
    const char *ret;

    ensure_builtin();
    refresh_user_map();
    key = g_ascii_strdown(code, -1);
    ret = user_map ? g_hash_table_lookup(user_map, key) : NULL;
    if(!ret)
        ret = g_hash_table_lookup(builtin_map, key);
    g_free(key);
    return ret;
}

const char *const *dwp_emoji_picker_codes(void)
{
    return picker_codes;
}

/* Value of attribute name in an HTML tag, newly allocated or NULL. */
static char *tag_attribute(const char *tag, const char *name)
{
    char *pattern = g_strdup_printf("\\b%s\\s*=\\s*(?:\"([^\"]*)\"|'([^']*)'|([^\\s>]+))", name);
    GRegex *re = g_regex_new(pattern, G_REGEX_CASELESS, 0, NULL);
    GMatchInfo *mi = NULL;
    char *ret = NULL;
    int z;

    g_free(pattern);
    if(!re)
        return NULL;
    if(g_regex_match(re, tag, 0, &mi))
    {
        for(z = 1; z <= 3 && !ret; z++)
        {
            int start = -1, end = -1;

            if(g_match_info_fetch_pos(mi, z, &start, &end) && start >= 0)
                ret = g_strndup(tag + start, end - start);
        }
    }
    g_match_info_free(mi);
    g_regex_unref(re);
    return ret;
}

/* <img> tags: libpurple stored images become "[image]", others (blocked
 * remote images, custom emoticons) show their alt/title text. */
static char *replace_images(const char *html)
{
    static GRegex *img_re = NULL;
    GMatchInfo *mi = NULL;
    GString *out = g_string_new(NULL);
    const char *last = html;

    if(!img_re)
        img_re = g_regex_new("<img\\b[^>]*>", G_REGEX_CASELESS, 0, NULL);
    if(g_regex_match(img_re, html, 0, &mi))
    {
        do
        {
            int start, end;
            char *tag, *text = NULL;

            g_match_info_fetch_pos(mi, 0, &start, &end);
            g_string_append_len(out, last, (html + start) - last);
            last = html + end;
            tag = g_strndup(html + start, end - start);
            if(!(text = tag_attribute(tag, "id")))
            {
                text = tag_attribute(tag, "alt");
                if(!text || !*g_strstrip(text))
                {
                    g_free(text);
                    text = tag_attribute(tag, "title");
                }
                if(text)
                {
                    char *p;

                    /* Attribute values are HTML text; drop anything tag-like. */
                    for(p = text; *p; p++)
                        if(*p == '<' || *p == '>')
                            *p = ' ';
                }
            }
            else
            {
                PurpleStoredImage *img = purple_imgstore_find_by_id(atoi(text));
                const char *fname = img ? purple_imgstore_get_filename(img) : NULL;

                g_free(text);
                text = fname ? g_markup_printf_escaped("[image: %s]", fname) : g_strdup("[image]");
            }
            g_string_append(out, text && *g_strstrip(text) ? text : "[image]");
            g_free(text);
            g_free(tag);
        } while(g_match_info_next(mi, NULL));
    }
    g_match_info_free(mi);
    g_string_append(out, last);
    return g_string_free(out, FALSE);
}

static char *convert_codes(const char *text)
{
    static GRegex *code_re = NULL;
    GString *out = g_string_new(NULL);
    const char *last = text;
    int from = 0, len = (int)strlen(text);

    if(!code_re)
        code_re = g_regex_new(":([A-Za-z0-9_+\\-]{1,48}):", 0, 0, NULL);
    while(from < len)
    {
        GMatchInfo *mi = NULL;
        int start, end, cstart, cend;
        char *code;
        const char *glyph;

        if(!g_regex_match_full(code_re, text, -1, from, 0, &mi, NULL))
        {
            g_match_info_free(mi);
            break;
        }
        g_match_info_fetch_pos(mi, 0, &start, &end);
        g_match_info_fetch_pos(mi, 1, &cstart, &cend);
        g_match_info_free(mi);
        code = g_strndup(text + cstart, cend - cstart);
        glyph = dwp_emoji_lookup(code);
        g_free(code);
        if(!glyph)
        {
            /* Unknown: the closing ':' may open the next code ("a :x:y:"). */
            from = end - 1;
            continue;
        }
        g_string_append_len(out, last, (text + start) - last);
        g_string_append(out, glyph);
        last = text + end;
        from = end;
    }
    g_string_append(out, last);
    return g_string_free(out, FALSE);
}

char *dwp_html_to_text(const char *html, gboolean emoji_codes)
{
    char *noimg = replace_images(html ? html : "");
    char *plain = purple_markup_strip_html(noimg);
    char *ret;

    g_free(noimg);
    if(!emoji_codes)
        return plain;
    ret = convert_codes(plain);
    g_free(plain);
    return ret;
}

gboolean dwp_is_reaction(const char *plain)
{
    static GRegex *direct = NULL, *named = NULL, *code = NULL;
    char *text;
    gboolean ret = FALSE;
    const char *p;

    if(!direct)
    {
        /* "Reacted with a :rainbowsmile:" / "Reacted to ..." */
        direct = g_regex_new("^\\s*reacted\\s+(?:with|to)\\b", G_REGEX_CASELESS, 0, NULL);
        /* "Andy Smith reacted to your message with :like:" */
        named = g_regex_new("^\\s*(?:\\S+\\s+){1,3}reacted\\s+(?:with|to)\\b", G_REGEX_CASELESS, 0, NULL);
        code = g_regex_new(":[A-Za-z0-9_+\\-]{1,48}:", 0, 0, NULL);
    }
    if(!plain || !g_utf8_validate(plain, -1, NULL))
        return FALSE;
    text = g_strstrip(g_strdup(plain));
    /* Reaction notices are short; sentences that merely mention them are not. */
    if(!*text || g_utf8_strlen(text, -1) > 160)
        goto out;
    if(g_regex_match(direct, text, 0, NULL))
    {
        ret = TRUE;
        goto out;
    }
    if(!g_regex_match(named, text, 0, NULL))
        goto out;
    if(g_regex_match(code, text, 0, NULL))
    {
        ret = TRUE;
        goto out;
    }
    /* With a name in front, also require an emoji. */
    for(p = text; *p; p = g_utf8_next_char(p))
    {
        gunichar c = g_utf8_get_char(p);

        if(c > 0xFFFF || g_unichar_type(c) == G_UNICODE_OTHER_SYMBOL)
        {
            ret = TRUE;
            break;
        }
    }
out:
    g_free(text);
    return ret;
}

===== END FILE: src/emoji.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/eventloop.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - libpurple event loop on top of Dynamic Windows.
 *
 * Dynamic Windows has timers but no file descriptor watches, so a single
 * periodic "pump" timer services both:
 *   - libpurple timeouts, kept in our own table with monotonic deadlines;
 *   - libpurple input watches, polled with a zero-timeout select().
 * This works the same on OS/2 (kLIBC select handles sockets and pipes),
 * Windows (sockets) and Unix.
 *
 * Re-entrancy: a callback may start a nested message loop (OS/2 runs the
 * pump timer inside it), so each source carries a "running" flag and is
 * looked up again by id after every callback.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

#include <errno.h>
#ifndef _WIN32
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <sys/time.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#ifdef __OS2__
#include <sys/socket.h>
#endif
#include <sys/select.h>
#endif

#define PUMP_INTERVAL_MS 20

typedef struct {
    guint id;
    guint interval;
    gint64 due;          /* milliseconds, monotonic */
    GSourceFunc func;
    gpointer data;
    gboolean running;
} Timeout;

typedef struct {
    guint id;
    int fd;
    PurpleInputCondition cond;
    PurpleInputFunction func;
    gpointer data;
    gboolean running;
} Input;

static GMutex lock;
static GHashTable *timeouts = NULL;   /* id -> Timeout */
static GHashTable *inputs = NULL;     /* id -> Input */
static guint next_id = 1;
static HTIMER pump_timer = 0;
static gboolean shutting_down = FALSE;

static gint64 now_ms(void)
{
    return g_get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
}

static void ensure_tables(void)
{
    if(!timeouts)
    {
        timeouts = g_hash_table_new_full(g_direct_hash, g_direct_equal, NULL, g_free);
        inputs = g_hash_table_new_full(g_direct_hash, g_direct_equal, NULL, g_free);
    }
}

/* Caller holds the lock. */
static guint new_id(void)
{
    guint id;

    do
    {
        id = next_id++;
        if(next_id == 0)
            next_id = 1;
    } while(id == 0 ||
            g_hash_table_lookup(timeouts, GUINT_TO_POINTER(id)) ||
            g_hash_table_lookup(inputs, GUINT_TO_POINTER(id)));
    return id;
}

static guint add_timeout_ms(guint interval, GSourceFunc func, gpointer data)
{
    Timeout *t;
    guint id;

    g_mutex_lock(&lock);
    ensure_tables();
    t = g_new0(Timeout, 1);
    t->id = id = new_id();
    t->interval = interval;
    t->due = now_ms() + interval;
    t->func = func;
    t->data = data;
    g_hash_table_insert(timeouts, GUINT_TO_POINTER(id), t);
    g_mutex_unlock(&lock);
    return id;
}

static guint ui_timeout_add(guint interval, GSourceFunc function, gpointer data)
{
    return add_timeout_ms(interval, function, data);
}

static guint ui_timeout_add_seconds(guint interval, GSourceFunc function, gpointer data)
{
    return add_timeout_ms(interval * 1000, function, data);
}

static gboolean ui_timeout_remove(guint handle)
{
    gboolean ret;

    g_mutex_lock(&lock);
    ensure_tables();
    ret = g_hash_table_remove(timeouts, GUINT_TO_POINTER(handle));
    g_mutex_unlock(&lock);
    return ret;
}

static guint ui_input_add(int fd, PurpleInputCondition cond, PurpleInputFunction func, gpointer data)
{
    Input *in;
    guint id;

    if(fd < 0)
    {
        purple_debug_warning("eventloop", "input_add called with invalid fd %d\n", fd);
        return 0;
    }
#ifndef _WIN32
    if(fd >= FD_SETSIZE)
    {
        purple_debug_error("eventloop", "fd %d exceeds FD_SETSIZE; not watched\n", fd);
        return 0;
    }
#endif
    g_mutex_lock(&lock);
    ensure_tables();
    in = g_new0(Input, 1);
    in->id = id = new_id();
    in->fd = fd;
    in->cond = cond;
    in->func = func;
    in->data = data;
    g_hash_table_insert(inputs, GUINT_TO_POINTER(id), in);
    g_mutex_unlock(&lock);
    return id;
}

static gboolean ui_input_remove(guint handle)
{
    gboolean ret;

    g_mutex_lock(&lock);
    ensure_tables();
    ret = g_hash_table_remove(inputs, GUINT_TO_POINTER(handle));
    g_mutex_unlock(&lock);
    return ret;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ pump */

static void run_timeouts(void)
{
    GArray *due;
    GHashTableIter iter;
    gpointer key, value;
    gint64 now = now_ms();
    guint z;

    due = g_array_new(FALSE, FALSE, sizeof(guint));
    g_mutex_lock(&lock);
    g_hash_table_iter_init(&iter, timeouts);
    while(g_hash_table_iter_next(&iter, &key, &value))
    {
        Timeout *t = value;

        if(!t->running && t->due <= now)
            g_array_append_val(due, t->id);
    }
    g_mutex_unlock(&lock);

    for(z = 0; z < due->len; z++)
    {
        guint id = g_array_index(due, guint, z);
        GSourceFunc func;
        gpointer data;
        gboolean again;
        Timeout *t;

        g_mutex_lock(&lock);
        t = g_hash_table_lookup(timeouts, GUINT_TO_POINTER(id));
        if(!t || t->running)
        {
            g_mutex_unlock(&lock);
            continue;
        }
        t->running = TRUE;
        t->due = now_ms() + t->interval;
        func = t->func;
        data = t->data;
        g_mutex_unlock(&lock);

        again = func(data);

        g_mutex_lock(&lock);
        t = g_hash_table_lookup(timeouts, GUINT_TO_POINTER(id));
        if(t)
        {
            t->running = FALSE;
            if(!again)
                g_hash_table_remove(timeouts, GUINT_TO_POINTER(id));
        }
        g_mutex_unlock(&lock);
    }
    g_array_free(due, TRUE);
}

typedef struct {
    guint id;
    int fd;
    PurpleInputCondition cond;
} Watch;

static PurpleInputCondition poll_one(int fd, PurpleInputCondition want, gboolean *bad)
{
    fd_set rfds, wfds;
    struct timeval tv = { 0, 0 };
    PurpleInputCondition got = 0;
    int rc;

    FD_ZERO(&rfds);
    FD_ZERO(&wfds);
    if(want & PURPLE_INPUT_READ)
        FD_SET(fd, &rfds);
    if(want & PURPLE_INPUT_WRITE)
        FD_SET(fd, &wfds);
    rc = select(fd + 1, &rfds, &wfds, NULL, &tv);
    if(rc < 0)
    {
        *bad = TRUE;
        return 0;
    }
    if(rc > 0)
    {
        if(FD_ISSET(fd, &rfds))
            got |= PURPLE_INPUT_READ;
        if(FD_ISSET(fd, &wfds))
            got |= PURPLE_INPUT_WRITE;
    }
    return got;
}

static void run_inputs(void)
{
    GArray *watches;
    GHashTableIter iter;
    gpointer key, value;
    fd_set rfds, wfds;
    struct timeval tv = { 0, 0 };
    int maxfd = -1, rc;
    guint z;
    gboolean fallback = FALSE;

    watches = g_array_new(FALSE, FALSE, sizeof(Watch));
    g_mutex_lock(&lock);
    g_hash_table_iter_init(&iter, inputs);
    while(g_hash_table_iter_next(&iter, &key, &value))
    {
        Input *in = value;
        Watch w;

        if(in->running)
            continue;
        w.id = in->id;
        w.fd = in->fd;
        w.cond = in->cond;
        g_array_append_val(watches, w);
    }
    g_mutex_unlock(&lock);

    if(!watches->len)
    {
        g_array_free(watches, TRUE);
        return;
    }

    FD_ZERO(&rfds);
    FD_ZERO(&wfds);
    for(z = 0; z < watches->len; z++)
    {
        Watch *w = &g_array_index(watches, Watch, z);

        if(w->cond & PURPLE_INPUT_READ)
            FD_SET(w->fd, &rfds);
        if(w->cond & PURPLE_INPUT_WRITE)
            FD_SET(w->fd, &wfds);
        if(w->fd > maxfd)
            maxfd = w->fd;
    }
    rc = select(maxfd + 1, &rfds, &wfds, NULL, &tv);
    if(rc < 0)
    {
        /* Usually EBADF: a protocol closed a descriptor before removing its
         * watch.  Poll each descriptor separately and skip the bad ones. */
        if(errno == EINTR)
        {
            g_array_free(watches, TRUE);
            return;
        }
        fallback = TRUE;
    }
    else if(rc == 0)
    {
        g_array_free(watches, TRUE);
        return;
    }

    for(z = 0; z < watches->len; z++)
    {
        Watch *w = &g_array_index(watches, Watch, z);
        PurpleInputCondition got = 0;
        PurpleInputFunction func;
        gpointer data;
        Input *in;

        if(fallback)
        {
            gboolean bad = FALSE;

            got = poll_one(w->fd, w->cond, &bad);
            if(bad)
                continue;
        }
        else
        {
            if((w->cond & PURPLE_INPUT_READ) && FD_ISSET(w->fd, &rfds))
                got |= PURPLE_INPUT_READ;
            if((w->cond & PURPLE_INPUT_WRITE) && FD_ISSET(w->fd, &wfds))
                got |= PURPLE_INPUT_WRITE;
        }
        if(!got)
            continue;

        g_mutex_lock(&lock);
        in = g_hash_table_lookup(inputs, GUINT_TO_POINTER(w->id));
        if(!in || in->running || in->fd != w->fd)
        {
            g_mutex_unlock(&lock);
            continue;
        }
        in->running = TRUE;
        func = in->func;
        data = in->data;
        g_mutex_unlock(&lock);

        func(data, w->fd, got);

        g_mutex_lock(&lock);
        in = g_hash_table_lookup(inputs, GUINT_TO_POINTER(w->id));
        if(in)
            in->running = FALSE;
        g_mutex_unlock(&lock);
    }
    g_array_free(watches, TRUE);
}

static int DWSIGNAL pump_cb(void *unused)
{
    (void)unused;
    if(shutting_down || !timeouts)
        return TRUE;
    /* libpurple callbacks open windows; GTK needs the GDK lock held. */
    dwp_gui_lock();
    run_inputs();
    run_timeouts();
    dwp_gui_unlock();
    return TRUE;
}

void dwp_eventloop_start(void)
{
    g_mutex_lock(&lock);
    ensure_tables();
    g_mutex_unlock(&lock);
    if(!pump_timer)
        pump_timer = dw_timer_connect(PUMP_INTERVAL_MS, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(pump_cb), NULL);
}

void dwp_eventloop_shutdown(void)
{
    shutting_down = TRUE;
    if(pump_timer)
    {
        dw_timer_disconnect(pump_timer);
        pump_timer = 0;
    }
    g_mutex_lock(&lock);
    if(timeouts)
    {
        g_hash_table_destroy(timeouts);
        g_hash_table_destroy(inputs);
        timeouts = inputs = NULL;
    }
    g_mutex_unlock(&lock);
}

PurpleEventLoopUiOps *dwp_eventloop_ui_ops(void)
{
    static PurpleEventLoopUiOps ops;
    static gboolean ready = FALSE;

    if(!ready)
    {
        memset(&ops, 0, sizeof(ops));
        ops.timeout_add = ui_timeout_add;
        ops.timeout_remove = ui_timeout_remove;
        ops.input_add = ui_input_add;
        ops.input_remove = ui_input_remove;
        ops.input_get_error = NULL;       /* libpurple falls back to getsockopt */
        ops.timeout_add_seconds = ui_timeout_add_seconds;
        ready = TRUE;
    }
    return &ops;
}

===== END FILE: src/eventloop.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/gdklock.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - GDK 3 thread lock watchdog (GTK builds only).
 *
 * The GTK 3 port of Dynamic Windows calls gdk_threads_init(), so GDK
 * guards its event and timer dispatch with a non-recursive global lock.
 * A hang was observed with the main thread blocked inside a GDK dispatch
 * waiting for that lock while no other thread was doing anything, i.e.
 * the main thread itself already held it.
 *
 * This replaces GDK's lock functions (before dw_init() calls
 * gdk_threads_init()) with an owner-tracking version that:
 *   - lets the owning thread re-enter instead of deadlocking;
 *   - ignores a release by a thread that does not hold the lock instead
 *     of aborting;
 *   - prints both stacks to stderr when either happens: where the lock was
 *     taken and where it was taken again, so the unbalanced call can be
 *     found.
 *
 * Set DWP_GDK_DEFAULT_LOCK=1 to keep GDK's own lock.  On other platforms
 * (or Dynamic Windows ports without GDK 3) this does nothing.
 */
#if defined(__linux__) || defined(__FreeBSD__)
#ifndef _GNU_SOURCE
#define _GNU_SOURCE
#endif
#endif

#include "dwpurple.h"

#include <stdio.h>

#if defined(__GLIBC__) && !defined(_WIN32) && !defined(__OS2__) && !defined(__APPLE__)
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <execinfo.h>

#define STACK_DEPTH 48
#define MAX_REPORTS 8

static GMutex state;
static GCond released;
static GThread *owner = NULL;
static int depth = 0;
static void *owner_stack[STACK_DEPTH];
static int owner_frames = 0;
static int reports = 0;

/* Caller holds "state". */
static void report(const char *what)
{
    void *here[STACK_DEPTH];
    int n;

    if(reports >= MAX_REPORTS)
        return;
    reports++;
    fprintf(stderr, "\n*** dwpurple gdk-lock: %s\n*** current stack:\n", what);
    n = backtrace(here, STACK_DEPTH);
    backtrace_symbols_fd(here, n, 2);
    if(owner_frames)
    {
        fprintf(stderr, "*** the lock was taken here:\n");
        backtrace_symbols_fd(owner_stack, owner_frames, 2);
    }
    fflush(stderr);
}

static void lock_enter(void)
{
    GThread *self = g_thread_self();

    g_mutex_lock(&state);
    if(owner == self)
    {
        depth++;
        report("taken again by the thread that already holds it (GDK would deadlock here)");
        g_mutex_unlock(&state);
        return;
    }
    while(owner)
    {
        gint64 until = g_get_monotonic_time() + 3 * G_TIME_SPAN_SECOND;

        if(!g_cond_wait_until(&released, &state, until) && owner)
            report("waited 3 seconds for the lock held by another thread");
    }
    owner = self;
    depth = 1;
    owner_frames = backtrace(owner_stack, STACK_DEPTH);
    g_mutex_unlock(&state);
}

static void lock_leave(void)
{
    g_mutex_lock(&state);
    if(owner != g_thread_self())
    {
        report("released by a thread that does not hold it (ignored; GLib would abort)");
        g_mutex_unlock(&state);
        return;
    }
    if(--depth == 0)
    {
        owner = NULL;
        owner_frames = 0;
        g_cond_signal(&released);
    }
    g_mutex_unlock(&state);
}

void dwp_gdk_lock_install(void)
{
    void (*set_functions)(GCallback, GCallback);
    void *warm[2];

    if(g_getenv("DWP_GDK_DEFAULT_LOCK"))
        return;
    *(void **)(&set_functions) = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "gdk_threads_set_lock_functions");
    if(!set_functions)
        return;
    /* The first backtrace() loads the unwinder; do it outside the lock. */
    backtrace(warm, 2);
    set_functions(G_CALLBACK(lock_enter), G_CALLBACK(lock_leave));
}

static void (*gui_enter)(void) = NULL;
static void (*gui_leave)(void) = NULL;
static gboolean gui_resolved = FALSE;

static void gui_resolve(void)
{
    if(gui_resolved)
        return;
    gui_resolved = TRUE;
    *(void **)(&gui_enter) = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "gdk_threads_enter");
    *(void **)(&gui_leave) = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "gdk_threads_leave");
    if(!gui_enter || !gui_leave)
        gui_enter = gui_leave = NULL;
}

/* Dynamic Windows timers are plain GLib timeouts, so their callbacks run
 * without the GDK lock, while GTK 3's gtk_events_pending()/
 * gtk_main_iteration() (used by Dynamic Windows' nested loops, for example
 * when sizing a window) release and retake it.  Unheld, that leaves the
 * lock owned by the main thread when the callback returns, and the next GDK
 * dispatch deadlocks. */
void dwp_gui_lock(void)
{
    gui_resolve();
    if(gui_enter)
        gui_enter();
}

void dwp_gui_unlock(void)
{
    if(gui_leave)
        gui_leave();
}

#else

void dwp_gdk_lock_install(void)
{
}

void dwp_gui_lock(void)
{
}

void dwp_gui_unlock(void)
{
}

#endif

===== END FILE: src/gdklock.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/idle.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - system idle detection (PurpleIdleUiOps).
 *
 * Sources, in order:
 *   Windows        GetLastInputInfo()
 *   X11            XScreenSaver extension (build with HAVE_XSS=1)
 *   everywhere     keyboard/mouse activity in DWPurple's own windows
 * libpurple polls get_time_idle and handles auto-away itself, using the
 * /purple/away/idle_reporting and /purple/away/mins_before_away prefs.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

#ifdef _WIN32
#include <windows.h>
#endif

#ifdef HAVE_XSS
#include <X11/Xlib.h>
#include <X11/extensions/scrnsaver.h>

static Display *xss_display = NULL;
static XScreenSaverInfo *xss_info = NULL;
static gboolean xss_failed = FALSE;

static gboolean xss_query(time_t *secs)
{
    int event_base, error_base;

    if(xss_failed)
        return FALSE;
    if(!xss_display)
    {
        const char *disp = g_getenv("DISPLAY");

        /* Pure Wayland sessions have no X display to ask. */
        if(!disp || !*disp || !(xss_display = XOpenDisplay(NULL)))
        {
            xss_failed = TRUE;
            return FALSE;
        }
        if(!XScreenSaverQueryExtension(xss_display, &event_base, &error_base) ||
           !(xss_info = XScreenSaverAllocInfo()))
        {
            XCloseDisplay(xss_display);
            xss_display = NULL;
            xss_failed = TRUE;
            return FALSE;
        }
    }
    if(!XScreenSaverQueryInfo(xss_display, DefaultRootWindow(xss_display), xss_info))
        return FALSE;
    *secs = (time_t)(xss_info->idle / 1000);
    return TRUE;
}

static void xss_close(void)
{
    if(xss_info)
        XFree(xss_info);
    if(xss_display)
        XCloseDisplay(xss_display);
    xss_info = NULL;
    xss_display = NULL;
}
#endif

static const char *backend = "Activity in DWPurple windows only";

static time_t app_idle(void)
{
    time_t now = time(NULL), last = dwp_last_activity();

    return now > last ? now - last : 0;
}

static time_t get_time_idle(void)
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    LASTINPUTINFO lii;

    memset(&lii, 0, sizeof(lii));
    lii.cbSize = sizeof(lii);
    if(GetLastInputInfo(&lii))
    {
        backend = "Windows input activity";
        return (time_t)((GetTickCount() - lii.dwTime) / 1000);
    }
#endif
#ifdef HAVE_XSS
    {
        time_t secs;

        if(xss_query(&secs))
        {
            backend = "X11 screen saver extension";
            return secs;
        }
    }
#endif
    backend = "Activity in DWPurple windows only";
    return app_idle();
}

PurpleIdleUiOps *dwp_idle_ui_ops(void)
{
    static PurpleIdleUiOps ops;
    static gboolean ready = FALSE;

    if(!ready)
    {
        memset(&ops, 0, sizeof(ops));
        ops.get_time_idle = get_time_idle;
        ready = TRUE;
    }
    return &ops;
}

const char *dwp_idle_backend_name(void)
{
    get_time_idle();
    return backend;
}

void dwp_idle_shutdown(void)
{
#ifdef HAVE_XSS
    xss_close();
#endif
}

===== END FILE: src/idle.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/ircconsole.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - IRC server console.
 *
 * libpurple's IRC protocol has no server window: lines that are not aimed
 * at an open channel or query (MOTD, notices, numeric replies, errors)
 * only go to the debug log, and Pidgin behaves the same way.  This window
 * shows them by listening to the IRC plugin's "irc-receiving-text" signal
 * and sends typed commands with the protocol's send_raw.  It opens when an
 * IRC account starts signing on (so the welcome and MOTD are captured) and
 * can be reopened from the account's menu.
 *
 * Input:
 *   /join #chan, /nick x, /whois x, /list, /motd ...  sent as JOIN #chan ...
 *   /msg nick text, /query nick                       open an IM window
 *   /quote LINE, /raw LINE                            LINE sent unchanged
 *   anything without a leading /                      sent unchanged
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

#include <ctype.h>

#define IRC_PRPL_ID        "prpl-irc"
#define CONSOLE_MAX_CHARS  100000

typedef struct {
    PurpleAccount *account;
    HWND window, history, entry;
    unsigned int pos;
    unsigned long chars;
} Console;

static GHashTable *consoles = NULL;      /* account -> Console */
static int console_handle;
static gboolean irc_hooked = FALSE;

static gboolean is_irc(PurpleAccount *account)
{
    return account && purple_strequal(purple_account_get_protocol_id(account), IRC_PRPL_ID);
}

static char *server_name(PurpleAccount *account)
{
    const char *user = purple_account_get_username(account);
    const char *at = user ? strrchr(user, '@') : NULL;

    return g_strdup(at && at[1] ? at + 1 : (user ? user : "IRC"));
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- output */

static void console_write(Console *c, const char *line)
{
    char ts[16];
    time_t now = time(NULL);
    struct tm *tm = localtime(&now);
    char *text;

    if(!c->history)
        return;
    if(!tm || !strftime(ts, sizeof(ts), "%H:%M:%S", tm))
        strcpy(ts, "--:--:--");
    if(c->chars > CONSOLE_MAX_CHARS)
    {
        dw_mle_clear(c->history);
        c->pos = 0;
        c->chars = 0;
    }
    text = g_strdup_printf("(%s) %s\r\n", ts, line);
    if(!g_utf8_validate(text, -1, NULL))
    {
        char *fixed = purple_utf8_salvage(text);

        g_free(text);
        text = fixed;
    }
    c->pos = dw_mle_import(c->history, text, c->pos);
    c->chars += strlen(text);
    dw_mle_set_cursor(c->history, c->pos);
    g_free(text);
}

static gboolean is_channel(const char *s)
{
    return s && (*s == '#' || *s == '&' || *s == '+' || *s == '!');
}

/* Turn a raw server line into console text; NULL for lines that already
 * show up elsewhere (channel and query traffic, keep-alives, replies that
 * libpurple consumes for the buddy list and room list). */
static char *format_line(const char *raw)
{
    static const char *const hidden[] = {
        "PING", "PONG", "JOIN", "PART", "QUIT", "NICK", "KICK", "TOPIC", "CAP",
        "AUTHENTICATE", "303", "315", "321", "322", "323", "332", "333", "352",
        "353", "366", NULL
    };
    char *line = g_strdup(raw), *p = line, *prefix = NULL, *cmd, *trailing = NULL, *sp;
    GPtrArray *params = g_ptr_array_new();
    GString *out;
    char *ret = NULL;
    guint z;

    g_strchomp(line);
    if(*p == ':')
    {
        prefix = ++p;
        if(!(sp = strchr(p, ' ')))
            goto done;
        *sp = '\0';
        p = sp + 1;
    }
    while(*p == ' ')
        p++;
    cmd = p;
    if(!*cmd)
        goto done;
    if((sp = strchr(p, ' ')))
    {
        *sp = '\0';
        p = sp + 1;
        while(*p)
        {
            while(*p == ' ')
                p++;
            if(*p == ':')
            {
                trailing = p + 1;
                break;
            }
            if(!*p)
                break;
            g_ptr_array_add(params, p);
            if(!(sp = strchr(p, ' ')))
                break;
            *sp = '\0';
            p = sp + 1;
        }
    }

    for(z = 0; hidden[z]; z++)
        if(g_ascii_strcasecmp(cmd, hidden[z]) == 0)
            goto done;
    /* MODE and PRIVMSG/NOTICE to a channel belong to the channel window;
     * PRIVMSG to us opens a query window. */
    if((g_ascii_strcasecmp(cmd, "MODE") == 0 || g_ascii_strcasecmp(cmd, "PRIVMSG") == 0 ||
        g_ascii_strcasecmp(cmd, "NOTICE") == 0) &&
       params->len && is_channel(g_ptr_array_index(params, 0)))
        goto done;
    if(g_ascii_strcasecmp(cmd, "PRIVMSG") == 0)
        goto done;

    out = g_string_new(NULL);
    if(g_ascii_strcasecmp(cmd, "NOTICE") == 0)
    {
        char *bang = prefix ? strchr(prefix, '!') : NULL;

        if(bang)
            *bang = '\0';
        g_string_append_printf(out, "-%s- %s", prefix ? prefix : "server", trailing ? trailing : "");
    }
    else if(strlen(cmd) == 3 && isdigit((unsigned char)cmd[0]) &&
            isdigit((unsigned char)cmd[1]) && isdigit((unsigned char)cmd[2]))
    {
        /* Numeric: drop the first parameter (our own nick). */
        for(z = 1; z < params->len; z++)
            g_string_append_printf(out, "%s%s", out->len ? " " : "", (char *)g_ptr_array_index(params, z));
        if(trailing)
            g_string_append_printf(out, "%s%s", out->len ? " " : "", trailing);
        if(!out->len)
            g_string_append(out, cmd);
    }
    else
    {
        g_string_append(out, cmd);
        for(z = 0; z < params->len; z++)
            g_string_append_printf(out, " %s", (char *)g_ptr_array_index(params, z));
        if(trailing)
            g_string_append_printf(out, " %s", trailing);
    }
    ret = g_string_free(out, FALSE);

done:
    g_ptr_array_free(params, TRUE);
    g_free(line);
    return ret;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- window */

static void console_free(gpointer p)
{
    g_free(p);
}

static int DWSIGNAL console_close(HWND w, void *data)
{
    Console *c = data;

    (void)w;
    if(consoles && g_hash_table_lookup(consoles, c->account) == c)
        g_hash_table_steal(consoles, c->account);
    c->history = c->entry = 0;
    dwp_window_close_later(c->window, console_free, c);
    return TRUE;
}

static gboolean send_line(Console *c, const char *line)
{
    PurpleConnection *gc = purple_account_get_connection(c->account);
    PurplePluginProtocolInfo *info = dwp_prpl_info(gc);
    char *wire;

    if(!gc || purple_connection_get_state(gc) != PURPLE_CONNECTED)
    {
        console_write(c, "!! Not connected.");
        return FALSE;
    }
    if(!info || !info->send_raw)
    {
        console_write(c, "!! This protocol cannot send raw commands.");
        return FALSE;
    }
    wire = g_strdup_printf("%s\r\n", line);
    info->send_raw(gc, wire, -1);
    g_free(wire);
    return TRUE;
}

static void handle_input(Console *c, const char *typed)
{
    char *text = g_strstrip(g_strdup(typed)), *p, *verb, *rest, *line = NULL, *echo;

    /* One command per entry: never let typed newlines start a second one. */
    for(p = text; *p; p++)
        if(*p == '\r' || *p == '\n')
            *p = ' ';
    if(!*text)
    {
        g_free(text);
        return;
    }
    if(text[0] != '/')
        line = g_strdup(text);
    else
    {
        verb = text + 1;
        rest = strchr(verb, ' ');
        if(rest)
        {
            *rest++ = '\0';
            while(*rest == ' ')
                rest++;
        }
        else
            rest = verb + strlen(verb);

        if(!g_ascii_strcasecmp(verb, "quote") || !g_ascii_strcasecmp(verb, "raw"))
            line = g_strdup(rest);
        else if(!g_ascii_strcasecmp(verb, "msg") || !g_ascii_strcasecmp(verb, "query"))
        {
            char *msg = strchr(rest, ' ');

            if(msg)
            {
                *msg++ = '\0';
                while(*msg == ' ')
                    msg++;
            }
            if(!*rest)
                console_write(c, "!! Usage: /msg <nick> [message]");
            else if(!purple_account_is_connected(c->account))
                console_write(c, "!! Not connected.");
            else
            {
                PurpleConversation *conv;

                dwp_open_im(c->account, rest);
                conv = purple_find_conversation_with_account(PURPLE_CONV_TYPE_IM, rest, c->account);
                if(conv && msg && *msg)
                {
                    char *html = g_markup_escape_text(msg, -1);

                    purple_conv_im_send(PURPLE_CONV_IM(conv), html);
                    g_free(html);
                }
            }
            g_free(text);
            return;
        }
        else if(*verb)
        {
            char *up = g_ascii_strup(verb, -1);

            line = *rest ? g_strdup_printf("%s %s", up, rest) : g_strdup(up);
            g_free(up);
        }
    }
    if(line && *line && send_line(c, line))
    {
        echo = g_strdup_printf("> %s", line);
        console_write(c, echo);
        g_free(echo);
    }
    g_free(line);
    g_free(text);
}

static int DWSIGNAL console_send(HWND w, void *data)
{
    Console *c = data;
    char *text;

    (void)w;
    if(!c->entry)
        return TRUE;
    text = dwp_get_text(c->entry);
    dw_window_set_text(c->entry, "");
    handle_input(c, text);
    g_free(text);
    dw_window_set_focus(c->entry);
    return TRUE;
}

static Console *console_open(PurpleAccount *account, gboolean raise)
{
    Console *c;
    HWND box, row, sendb;
    char *server, *title;

    if(!consoles)
        consoles = g_hash_table_new(g_direct_hash, g_direct_equal);
    if((c = g_hash_table_lookup(consoles, account)))
    {
        dw_window_show(c->window);
        if(raise)
            dw_window_raise(c->window);
        return c;
    }
    c = g_new0(Console, 1);
    c->account = account;
    server = server_name(account);
    title = g_strdup_printf("%s - %s", server, purple_account_get_username(account));
    c->window = dwp_window_new(title, 0, 0);
    g_free(title);
    g_free(server);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 2);
    dw_box_pack_start(c->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    c->history = dw_mle_new(0);
    dw_mle_set_editable(c->history, FALSE);
    dw_mle_set_word_wrap(c->history, TRUE);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, c->history, 560, 320, TRUE, TRUE, 2);
    row = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, row, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    c->entry = dw_entryfield_new("", 0);
    dw_window_set_tooltip(c->entry, "/join #channel, /msg nick text, /whois nick, /list, /quote RAW");
    dw_box_pack_start(row, c->entry, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    sendb = dwp_button(row, "Send", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(console_send), c);
    dw_window_click_default(c->entry, sendb);
    dw_signal_connect(c->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(console_close), c);
    g_hash_table_insert(consoles, account, c);
    dwp_window_present(c->window, 640, 440);
    if(raise)
        dw_window_set_focus(c->entry);
    return c;
}

void dwp_irc_console_show(PurpleAccount *account)
{
    if(dwp_account_exists(account) && is_irc(account))
        console_open(account, TRUE);
}

gboolean dwp_irc_console_supported(PurpleAccount *account)
{
    return is_irc(account);
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- signals */

static void on_receiving_text(PurpleConnection *gc, char **text, gpointer data)
{
    PurpleAccount *account;
    Console *c;
    char *shown;

    (void)data;
    if(!consoles || !gc || !text || !*text)
        return;
    account = purple_connection_get_account(gc);
    if(!(c = g_hash_table_lookup(consoles, account)))
        return;
    if((shown = format_line(*text)))
    {
        console_write(c, shown);
        g_free(shown);
    }
}

static void hook_irc(void)
{
    PurplePlugin *irc;

    if(irc_hooked || !(irc = purple_plugins_find_with_id(IRC_PRPL_ID)))
        return;
    purple_signal_connect(irc, "irc-receiving-text", &console_handle,
                          PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_receiving_text), NULL);
    irc_hooked = TRUE;
}

static void on_signing_on(PurpleConnection *gc, gpointer data)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = purple_connection_get_account(gc);
    Console *c;
    char *server, *msg;

    (void)data;
    if(!is_irc(account))
        return;
    hook_irc();
    c = console_open(account, FALSE);
    server = server_name(account);
    msg = g_strdup_printf("Connecting to %s...", server);
    console_write(c, msg);
    g_free(msg);
    g_free(server);
}

static void on_signed_on(PurpleConnection *gc, gpointer data)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = purple_connection_get_account(gc);
    Console *c;
    char *server, *msg;

    (void)data;
    if(!is_irc(account))
        return;
    hook_irc();
    c = console_open(account, FALSE);
    server = server_name(account);
    msg = g_strdup_printf("Connected to %s as %s.", server, purple_connection_get_display_name(gc));
    console_write(c, msg);
    g_free(msg);
    g_free(server);
}

static void on_signed_off(PurpleConnection *gc, gpointer data)
{
    Console *c;

    (void)data;
    if(consoles && (c = g_hash_table_lookup(consoles, purple_connection_get_account(gc))))
        console_write(c, "Disconnected.");
}

static void on_account_removed(PurpleAccount *account, gpointer data)
{
    Console *c;

    (void)data;
    if(consoles && (c = g_hash_table_lookup(consoles, account)))
        console_close(0, c);
}

void dwp_irc_console_init(void)
{
    void *conns = purple_connections_get_handle();

    purple_signal_connect(conns, "signing-on", &console_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_signing_on), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(conns, "signed-on", &console_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_signed_on), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(conns, "signed-off", &console_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_signed_off), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(purple_accounts_get_handle(), "account-removed", &console_handle,
                          PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_account_removed), NULL);
    hook_irc();
}

===== END FILE: src/ircconsole.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/main.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - entry point.
 *
 *   dwpurple [-c DIR] [-d] [-n] [-h] [-v]
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#ifndef _WIN32
#include <signal.h>
#endif

static void usage(const char *name)
{
    printf("%s %s - instant messenger using libpurple and Dynamic Windows\n\n"
           "Usage: %s [options]\n\n"
           "  -c, --config DIR   use DIR for configuration files (default ~/.dwpurple)\n"
           "  -d, --debug        print libpurple debug messages to the terminal\n"
           "  -n, --nologin      start with all accounts offline\n"
           "  -h, --help         show this help\n"
           "  -v, --version      show the version\n",
           DWP_NAME, DWP_VERSION, name);
}

int dwmain(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    DwpOptions options;
    char *error = NULL;
    int z;

    memset(&options, 0, sizeof(options));
    for(z = 1; z < argc; z++)
    {
        const char *a = argv[z];

        if((!strcmp(a, "-c") || !strcmp(a, "--config")) && z + 1 < argc)
            options.config_dir = argv[++z];
        else if(!strncmp(a, "--config=", 9))
            options.config_dir = (char *)a + 9;
        else if(!strcmp(a, "-d") || !strcmp(a, "--debug"))
            options.debug = TRUE;
        else if(!strcmp(a, "-n") || !strcmp(a, "--nologin"))
            options.no_login = TRUE;
        else if(!strcmp(a, "-v") || !strcmp(a, "--version"))
        {
            printf("%s %s\n", DWP_NAME, DWP_VERSION);
            return 0;
        }
        else if(!strcmp(a, "-h") || !strcmp(a, "--help"))
        {
            usage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        }
        else
        {
            fprintf(stderr, "Unknown option: %s\n\n", a);
            usage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

#ifndef _WIN32
    /* A peer closing a socket must not kill the client. */
    signal(SIGPIPE, SIG_IGN);
#endif

    dwp_set_exe_path(argc > 0 ? argv[0] : NULL);
    /* Must run before dw_init() calls gdk_threads_init(). */
    dwp_gdk_lock_install();
    dw_app_id_set(DWP_APP_ID, DWP_NAME);
    if(dw_init(TRUE, argc, argv) != DW_ERROR_NONE)
    {
        fprintf(stderr, "Could not initialize Dynamic Windows.\n");
        return 1;
    }

    if(!dwp_core_init(&options, &error))
    {
        dw_messagebox(DWP_NAME, DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_ERROR, "%s", error ? error : "Initialization failed.");
        g_free(error);
        dw_exit(1);
        return 1;
    }

    dw_main();

    dwp_core_shutdown();
    dw_exit(0);
    return 0;
}

===== END FILE: src/main.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/notify.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - libpurple notify UI: messages, mail, formatted text, user
 * info and search results.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

typedef struct _Note Note;

typedef struct {
    Note *note;
    PurpleNotifySearchButton *button;
} SearchButton;

struct _Note {
    PurpleNotifyType type;
    HWND window, list;
    gboolean closing, deleted;
    char *url;
    /* search results */
    PurpleAccount *account;
    PurpleNotifySearchResults *results;
    gpointer user_data;
    GPtrArray *buttons;
    int columns;
};

static GList *live = NULL;

static void note_free(gpointer p)
{
    Note *n = p;

    if(n->results)
        purple_notify_searchresults_free(n->results);
    if(n->buttons)
        g_ptr_array_free(n->buttons, TRUE);
    g_free(n->url);
    g_free(n);
}

static void close_from_core(Note *n)
{
    if(n->deleted)
        return;
    n->deleted = TRUE;
    n->closing = TRUE;
    live = g_list_remove(live, n);
    dwp_window_close_later(n->window, note_free, n);
}

/* User closed the window: tell libpurple, which runs its close callback. */
static void user_close(Note *n)
{
    if(n->closing)
        return;
    n->closing = TRUE;
    if(n->window)
        dw_window_hide(n->window);
    purple_notify_close(n->type, n);
    if(!n->deleted)
        close_from_core(n);
}

static int DWSIGNAL note_close(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    user_close(data);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL note_open_url(HWND w, void *data)
{
    Note *n = data;

    (void)w;
    if(n->url)
        dwp_open_link(n->url);
    return TRUE;
}

static Note *note_new(PurpleNotifyType type, const char *title)
{
    Note *n = g_new0(Note, 1);

    n->type = type;
    n->window = dwp_window_new(title && *title ? title : DWP_NAME, 0, 0);
    live = g_list_append(live, n);
    return n;
}

static void add_text(HWND box, const char *text)
{
    /* Read-only text block: selectable, and never collapses the layout. */
    dwp_text_block(box, text, 400, 160);
}

/* body may contain HTML (formatted/user info) and is shown as text. */
static Note *simple_window(PurpleNotifyType type, const char *title, const char *primary,
                           const char *secondary, const char *body_html, const char *url)
{
    Note *n = note_new(type, title);
    HWND box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4), buttons, closeb;
    gboolean big = FALSE;

    dw_box_pack_start(n->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    if(primary && *primary)
        add_text(box, primary);
    if(secondary && *secondary)
    {
        if(strlen(secondary) > 200 || strchr(secondary, '\n'))
        {
            HWND mle = dw_mle_new(0);
            char **parts = g_strsplit(secondary, "\n", -1);
            char *crlf = g_strjoinv("\r\n", parts);

            dw_mle_set_editable(mle, FALSE);
            dw_mle_set_word_wrap(mle, TRUE);
            dw_mle_import(mle, crlf, 0);
            dw_mle_set_cursor(mle, 0);
            dw_box_pack_start(box, mle, 420, 120, TRUE, TRUE, 3);
            g_free(crlf);
            g_strfreev(parts);
            big = TRUE;
        }
        else
            add_text(box, secondary);
    }
    if(body_html)
    {
        HWND mle = dw_mle_new(0);
        char *text = dwp_html_to_text(body_html, FALSE);
        char **parts = g_strsplit(text, "\n", -1);
        char *crlf = g_strjoinv("\r\n", parts);

        dw_mle_set_editable(mle, FALSE);
        dw_mle_set_word_wrap(mle, TRUE);
        dw_mle_import(mle, crlf, 0);
        dw_mle_set_cursor(mle, 0);
        dw_box_pack_start(box, mle, 460, 240, TRUE, TRUE, 3);
        g_free(crlf);
        g_strfreev(parts);
        g_free(text);
        big = TRUE;
    }
    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    if(url && *url)
    {
        n->url = g_strdup(url);
        dwp_button(buttons, "Open", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(note_open_url), n);
    }
    closeb = dwp_button(buttons, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(note_close), n);
    dw_signal_connect(n->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(note_close), n);
    dwp_window_present(n->window, big ? 520 : 440, big ? 400 : 240);
    dw_window_set_focus(closeb);
    return n;
}

static void *notify_message(PurpleNotifyMsgType type, const char *title,
                            const char *primary, const char *secondary)
{
    const char *kind = type == PURPLE_NOTIFY_MSG_ERROR ? "Error"
                     : type == PURPLE_NOTIFY_MSG_WARNING ? "Warning" : "Information";

    return simple_window(PURPLE_NOTIFY_MESSAGE, title && *title ? title : kind, primary, secondary, NULL, NULL);
}

static void *notify_email(PurpleConnection *gc, const char *subject, const char *from,
                          const char *to, const char *url)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = purple_connection_get_account(gc);
    char *text = g_strdup_printf("From: %s\nTo: %s\nSubject: %s", from ? from : "",
                                 to && *to ? to : purple_account_get_username(account),
                                 subject ? subject : "");
    Note *n = simple_window(PURPLE_NOTIFY_EMAIL, "New Mail", "You have new mail!", text, NULL, url);

    g_free(text);
    return n;
}

static void *notify_emails(PurpleConnection *gc, size_t count, gboolean detailed,
                           const char **subjects, const char **froms, const char **tos,
                           const char **urls)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = purple_connection_get_account(gc);
    char *name = dwp_account_display_name(account);
    char *primary = g_strdup_printf("%s has %lu new message%s.", name, (unsigned long)count, count == 1 ? "" : "s");
    GString *secondary = g_string_new(NULL);
    Note *n;
    size_t z;

    (void)tos;
    if(detailed && subjects)
        for(z = 0; z < count; z++)
            g_string_append_printf(secondary, "%s - %s\n", froms && froms[z] ? froms[z] : "",
                                   subjects[z] ? subjects[z] : "");
    n = simple_window(PURPLE_NOTIFY_EMAILS, "New Mail", primary,
                      secondary->len ? g_strstrip(secondary->str) : NULL, NULL,
                      urls && urls[0] ? urls[0] : NULL);
    g_string_free(secondary, TRUE);
    g_free(primary);
    g_free(name);
    return n;
}

static void *notify_formatted(const char *title, const char *primary, const char *secondary, const char *text)
{
    return simple_window(PURPLE_NOTIFY_FORMATTED, title, primary, secondary, text ? text : "", NULL);
}

static void *notify_userinfo(PurpleConnection *gc, const char *who, PurpleNotifyUserInfo *user_info)
{
    char *text = purple_notify_user_info_get_text_with_newline(user_info, "<br />");
    char *acct = dwp_account_display_name(purple_connection_get_account(gc));
    char *primary = g_strdup_printf("Info for %s", who ? who : "");
    Note *n = simple_window(PURPLE_NOTIFY_USERINFO, "User Info", primary, acct,
                            text && *text ? text : "(no information)", NULL);

    g_free(primary);
    g_free(acct);
    g_free(text);
    return n;
}

static void *notify_uri(const char *uri)
{
    if(!dwp_open_link(uri))
        purple_notify_error(NULL, "Open Link", "Refusing to open this link.", uri);
    return NULL;
}

/* ========================================================= search results */

static char *button_label(PurpleNotifySearchButton *button)
{
    switch(button->type)
    {
        case PURPLE_NOTIFY_BUTTON_LABELED:  return dwp_strip_mnemonic(button->label);
        case PURPLE_NOTIFY_BUTTON_CONTINUE: return g_strdup("Continue");
        case PURPLE_NOTIFY_BUTTON_ADD:      return g_strdup("Add");
        case PURPLE_NOTIFY_BUTTON_INFO:     return g_strdup("Get Info");
        case PURPLE_NOTIFY_BUTTON_IM:       return g_strdup("Send Message");
        case PURPLE_NOTIFY_BUTTON_JOIN:     return g_strdup("Join");
        case PURPLE_NOTIFY_BUTTON_INVITE:   return g_strdup("Invite");
    }
    return g_strdup("");
}

static void search_set_rows(Note *n, PurpleNotifySearchResults *results)
{
    GList *r;
    int count = 0, row = 0;
    void *alloc;

    dw_container_clear(n->list, TRUE);
    count = (int)g_list_length(results->rows);
    if(!count)
        return;
    alloc = dw_container_alloc(n->list, count);
    for(r = results->rows; r; r = r->next, row++)
    {
        GList *c;
        int col = 0;

        for(c = r->data; c && col < n->columns; c = c->next, col++)
        {
            char *text = c->data ? (char *)c->data : "";

            dw_container_set_item(n->list, alloc, col, row, &text);
        }
        for(; col < n->columns; col++)
        {
            char *empty = "";

            dw_container_set_item(n->list, alloc, col, row, &empty);
        }
        dw_container_set_row_title(alloc, row, ((GList *)r->data && ((GList *)r->data)->data)
                                               ? (char *)((GList *)r->data)->data : "");
        dw_container_set_row_data(alloc, row, GINT_TO_POINTER(row + 1));
    }
    dw_container_insert(n->list, alloc, count);
    dw_container_optimize(n->list);
}

static int DWSIGNAL search_button_clicked(HWND w, void *data)
{
    SearchButton *sb = data;
    Note *n = sb->note;
    PurpleConnection *conn;
    GList *row = NULL;
    int index;

    (void)w;
    if(n->closing || !dwp_account_exists(n->account))
        return TRUE;
    conn = purple_account_get_connection(n->account);
    if(!conn || !sb->button->callback)
        return TRUE;
    index = GPOINTER_TO_INT(dw_container_query_start(n->list, DW_CRA_SELECTED | DW_CR_RETDATA)) - 1;
    if(index >= 0)
    {
        GList *src = g_list_nth_data(n->results->rows, index), *c;

        /* Like Pidgin: pass a freshly allocated copy of the selected row. */
        for(c = src; c; c = c->next)
            row = g_list_append(row, g_strdup(c->data ? c->data : ""));
    }
    sb->button->callback(conn, row, n->user_data);
    g_list_free_full(row, g_free);
    return TRUE;
}

static void *notify_searchresults(PurpleConnection *gc, const char *title, const char *primary,
                                  const char *secondary, PurpleNotifySearchResults *results,
                                  gpointer user_data)
{
    Note *n = note_new(PURPLE_NOTIFY_SEARCHRESULTS, title);
    HWND box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4), buttons;
    GList *c, *b;
    unsigned long *flags;
    char **titles;
    int z;

    n->account = purple_connection_get_account(gc);
    n->results = results;
    n->user_data = user_data;
    n->buttons = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(g_free);
    n->columns = (int)g_list_length(results->columns);
    dw_box_pack_start(n->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    if(primary && *primary)
        add_text(box, primary);
    if(secondary && *secondary)
        add_text(box, secondary);

    n->list = dw_container_new(0, FALSE);
    if(n->columns > 0)
    {
        flags = g_new0(unsigned long, n->columns);
        titles = g_new0(char *, n->columns);
        for(c = results->columns, z = 0; c; c = c->next, z++)
        {
            flags[z] = DW_CFA_STRING | DW_CFA_LEFT | DW_CFA_HORZSEPARATOR | DW_CFA_SEPARATOR;
            titles[z] = ((PurpleNotifySearchColumn *)c->data)->title;
        }
        dw_container_setup(n->list, flags, titles, n->columns, 0);
        g_free(flags);
        g_free(titles);
    }
    dw_box_pack_start(box, n->list, 560, 300, TRUE, TRUE, 2);

    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    for(b = results->buttons; b; b = b->next)
    {
        SearchButton *sb = g_new0(SearchButton, 1);
        char *label;

        sb->note = n;
        sb->button = b->data;
        g_ptr_array_add(n->buttons, sb);
        label = button_label(sb->button);
        dwp_button(buttons, label, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(search_button_clicked), sb);
        g_free(label);
    }
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(note_close), n);
    dw_signal_connect(n->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(note_close), n);
    if(n->columns > 0)
        search_set_rows(n, results);
    dwp_window_present(n->window, 640, 440);
    return n;
}

static void notify_searchresults_new_rows(PurpleConnection *gc, PurpleNotifySearchResults *results, void *data)
{
    Note *n = data;

    (void)gc;
    if(!g_list_find(live, n) || n->closing || n->columns <= 0)
        return;
    search_set_rows(n, results);
}

static void close_notify(PurpleNotifyType type, void *ui_handle)
{
    Note *n = ui_handle;

    (void)type;
    if(g_list_find(live, n))
        close_from_core(n);
}

PurpleNotifyUiOps *dwp_notify_ui_ops(void)
{
    static PurpleNotifyUiOps ops;
    static gboolean ready = FALSE;

    if(!ready)
    {
        memset(&ops, 0, sizeof(ops));
        ops.notify_message = notify_message;
        ops.notify_email = notify_email;
        ops.notify_emails = notify_emails;
        ops.notify_formatted = notify_formatted;
        ops.notify_searchresults = notify_searchresults;
        ops.notify_searchresults_new_rows = notify_searchresults_new_rows;
        ops.notify_userinfo = notify_userinfo;
        ops.notify_uri = notify_uri;
        ops.close_notify = close_notify;
        ready = TRUE;
    }
    return &ops;
}

===== END FILE: src/notify.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/plugins.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - plugin manager, plugin actions and plugin preference frames.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

typedef struct {
    HWND window, list, details;
} PluginsDlg;

static PluginsDlg *pd = NULL;
static GPtrArray *plugin_actions = NULL;

static gboolean plugin_listed(PurplePlugin *plugin)
{
    const char *ui;

    if(!plugin || !plugin->info || plugin->info->type != PURPLE_PLUGIN_STANDARD)
        return FALSE;
    if(plugin->info->flags & PURPLE_PLUGIN_FLAG_INVISIBLE)
        return FALSE;
    ui = plugin->info->ui_requirement;
    return !ui || strcmp(ui, DWP_UI_ID) == 0;
}

static gboolean plugin_exists(PurplePlugin *plugin)
{
    return plugin && g_list_find(purple_plugins_get_all(), plugin) != NULL;
}

static PurplePlugin *selected_plugin(void)
{
    PurplePlugin *p;

    if(!pd)
        return NULL;
    p = (PurplePlugin *)dw_container_query_start(pd->list, DW_CRA_SELECTED | DW_CR_RETDATA);
    return plugin_exists(p) ? p : NULL;
}

static void show_details(PurplePlugin *p)
{
    GString *s;
    PurplePluginInfo *info;

    if(!pd)
        return;
    dw_mle_clear(pd->details);
    if(!p)
        return;
    info = p->info;
    s = g_string_new(NULL);
    g_string_append_printf(s, "%s %s\r\n%s\r\n", info->name ? info->name : "",
                           info->version ? info->version : "", info->description ? info->description : "");
    if(info->author)
        g_string_append_printf(s, "\r\nWritten by: %s", info->author);
    if(info->homepage)
        g_string_append_printf(s, "\r\nWeb site: %s", info->homepage);
    if(p->path)
    {
        char *path = dwp_filename_to_utf8(p->path);

        g_string_append_printf(s, "\r\nFilename: %s", path);
        g_free(path);
    }
    if(p->error)
        g_string_append_printf(s, "\r\n\r\nError: %s", p->error);
    dw_mle_import(pd->details, s->str, 0);
    dw_mle_set_cursor(pd->details, 0);
    g_string_free(s, TRUE);
}

static gint plugin_compare(gconstpointer a, gconstpointer b)
{
    const PurplePlugin *x = *(PurplePlugin *const *)a, *y = *(PurplePlugin *const *)b;

    return g_utf8_collate(x->info->name ? x->info->name : "", y->info->name ? y->info->name : "");
}

static void refresh(void)
{
    GPtrArray *list = g_ptr_array_new();
    PurplePlugin *selected = selected_plugin();
    GList *l;
    void *alloc;
    guint z;

    if(!pd)
        return;
    for(l = purple_plugins_get_all(); l; l = l->next)
        if(plugin_listed(l->data))
            g_ptr_array_add(list, l->data);
    g_ptr_array_sort(list, plugin_compare);
    dw_container_clear(pd->list, TRUE);
    if(list->len)
    {
        alloc = dw_container_alloc(pd->list, (int)list->len);
        for(z = 0; z < list->len; z++)
        {
            PurplePlugin *p = g_ptr_array_index(list, z);
            char *loaded = purple_plugin_is_loaded(p) ? "Yes" : (p->error ? "Error" : "No");
            char *name = p->info->name ? p->info->name : p->info->id;
            char *version = p->info->version ? p->info->version : "";
            char *summary = p->info->summary ? p->info->summary : "";

            dw_container_set_item(pd->list, alloc, 0, (int)z, &loaded);
            dw_container_set_item(pd->list, alloc, 1, (int)z, &name);
            dw_container_set_item(pd->list, alloc, 2, (int)z, &version);
            dw_container_set_item(pd->list, alloc, 3, (int)z, &summary);
            dw_container_set_row_title(alloc, (int)z, name);
            dw_container_set_row_data(alloc, (int)z, p);
        }
        dw_container_insert(pd->list, alloc, (int)list->len);
        dw_container_optimize(pd->list);
    }
    if(selected)
        dw_container_cursor_by_data(pd->list, selected);
    g_ptr_array_free(list, TRUE);
}

static int DWSIGNAL plugin_select(HWND w, HTREEITEM item, char *text, void *data, void *itemdata)
{
    (void)w;
    (void)item;
    (void)text;
    (void)data;
    show_details(plugin_exists(itemdata) ? itemdata : NULL);
    return FALSE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL plugin_toggle(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurplePlugin *p = selected_plugin();
    gboolean want, ok;

    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    if(!p)
        return TRUE;
    want = !purple_plugin_is_loaded(p);
    ok = want ? purple_plugin_load(p) : purple_plugin_unload(p);
    if(!ok)
        dw_messagebox("Plugins", DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_WARNING, "%s could not be %s.%s%s",
                      p->info->name ? p->info->name : "The plugin", want ? "loaded" : "unloaded",
                      p->error ? "\n\n" : "", p->error ? p->error : "");
    purple_plugins_save_loaded(DWP_PREF_LOADED);
    refresh();
    show_details(plugin_exists(p) ? p : NULL);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL plugin_enter(HWND w, char *text, void *data, void *itemdata)
{
    (void)text;
    (void)itemdata;
    return plugin_toggle(w, data);
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------- pref frames */

typedef struct {
    PurplePluginPref *pref;
    char *name;
    HWND widget;
    PurplePrefType ptype;
    gboolean multiline;
    GPtrArray *labels;
    GPtrArray *values;      /* gpointer values for choices */
} PrefWidget;

typedef struct {
