    HWND window;
    PurplePluginPrefFrame *frame;
    GPtrArray *widgets;
} PrefsDlg;

static void pref_widget_free(gpointer p)
{
    PrefWidget *w = p;

    g_free(w->name);
    if(w->labels)
        g_ptr_array_free(w->labels, TRUE);
    if(w->values)
        g_ptr_array_free(w->values, TRUE);
    g_free(w);
}

static void prefs_dlg_free(gpointer p)
{
    PrefsDlg *d = p;

    g_ptr_array_free(d->widgets, TRUE);
    purple_plugin_pref_frame_destroy(d->frame);
    g_free(d);
}

static int DWSIGNAL prefs_close(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PrefsDlg *d = data;

    (void)w;
    dwp_window_close_later(d->window, prefs_dlg_free, d);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL prefs_save(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PrefsDlg *d = data;
    guint z;

    for(z = 0; z < d->widgets->len; z++)
    {
        PrefWidget *pw = g_ptr_array_index(d->widgets, z);

        if(!purple_prefs_exists(pw->name))
            continue;
        if(pw->values)
        {
            int index = dwp_combo_index(pw->widget, (char **)pw->labels->pdata, (int)pw->labels->len);
            gpointer value;

            if(index < 0)
                continue;
            value = g_ptr_array_index(pw->values, index);
            if(pw->ptype == PURPLE_PREF_INT)
                purple_prefs_set_int(pw->name, GPOINTER_TO_INT(value));
            else if(pw->ptype == PURPLE_PREF_BOOLEAN)
                purple_prefs_set_bool(pw->name, GPOINTER_TO_INT(value) ? TRUE : FALSE);
            else
                purple_prefs_set_string(pw->name, value);
            continue;
        }
        switch(pw->ptype)
        {
            case PURPLE_PREF_BOOLEAN:
                purple_prefs_set_bool(pw->name, dw_checkbox_get(pw->widget) ? TRUE : FALSE);
                break;
            case PURPLE_PREF_INT:
                purple_prefs_set_int(pw->name, (int)dw_spinbutton_get_pos(pw->widget));
                break;
            case PURPLE_PREF_STRING:
            case PURPLE_PREF_PATH:
            {
                char *v = pw->multiline ? dwp_mle_get_text(pw->widget) : dwp_get_text(pw->widget);

                if(pw->ptype == PURPLE_PREF_PATH)
                    purple_prefs_set_path(pw->name, v);
                else
                    purple_prefs_set_string(pw->name, v);
                g_free(v);
                break;
            }
            default:
                break;
        }
    }
    return prefs_close(w, data);
}

static void show_pref_frame(PurplePlugin *plugin, PurplePluginPrefFrame *frame)
{
    PrefsDlg *d = g_new0(PrefsDlg, 1);
    HWND box, scroll, buttons;
    GList *l;
    char *title = g_strdup_printf("%s Preferences", plugin->info->name ? plugin->info->name : "Plugin");

    d->frame = frame;
    d->widgets = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(pref_widget_free);
    d->window = dwp_window_new(title, 0, 0);
    g_free(title);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(d->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    scroll = dw_scrollbox_new(DW_VERT, 2);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, scroll, 460, 300, TRUE, TRUE, 2);

    for(l = purple_plugin_pref_frame_get_prefs(frame); l; l = l->next)
    {
        PurplePluginPref *pref = l->data;
        const char *name = purple_plugin_pref_get_name(pref);
        char *label = dwp_strip_mnemonic(purple_plugin_pref_get_label(pref));
        PrefWidget *pw;

        if(!name || purple_plugin_pref_get_type(pref) == PURPLE_PLUGIN_PREF_INFO || !purple_prefs_exists(name))
        {
            dwp_text_block(scroll, label, 420, 160);
            g_free(label);
            continue;
        }
        pw = g_new0(PrefWidget, 1);
        pw->pref = pref;
        pw->name = g_strdup(name);
        pw->ptype = purple_prefs_get_type(name);
        if(purple_plugin_pref_get_type(pref) == PURPLE_PLUGIN_PREF_CHOICE)
        {
            GList *c = purple_plugin_pref_get_choices(pref);
            int sel = 0;

            pw->labels = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(g_free);
            pw->values = g_ptr_array_new();
            /* choices: label, value, label, value, ... */
            for(; c && c->next; c = c->next->next)
            {
                gpointer value = c->next->data;
                gboolean current = FALSE;

                if(pw->ptype == PURPLE_PREF_INT)
                    current = purple_prefs_get_int(name) == GPOINTER_TO_INT(value);
                else if(pw->ptype == PURPLE_PREF_BOOLEAN)
                    current = !!purple_prefs_get_bool(name) == !!GPOINTER_TO_INT(value);
                else
                    current = purple_strequal(purple_prefs_get_string(name), value);
                if(current)
                    sel = (int)pw->labels->len;
                g_ptr_array_add(pw->labels, dwp_strip_mnemonic(c->data));
                g_ptr_array_add(pw->values, value);
            }
            pw->widget = dw_combobox_new("", 0);
            dwp_combo_fill(pw->widget, (char **)pw->labels->pdata, (int)pw->labels->len, sel);
            dwp_form_row(scroll, label, pw->widget);
        }
        else switch(pw->ptype)
        {
            case PURPLE_PREF_BOOLEAN:
                pw->widget = dw_checkbox_new(label, 0);
                dw_checkbox_set(pw->widget, purple_prefs_get_bool(name));
                dwp_form_row(scroll, "", pw->widget);
                break;
            case PURPLE_PREF_INT:
            {
                int min = 0, max = 0;
                char buf[32];

                purple_plugin_pref_get_bounds(pref, &min, &max);
                if(max <= min)
                {
                    min = G_MININT;
                    max = G_MAXINT;
                }
                g_snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%d", purple_prefs_get_int(name));
                pw->widget = dw_spinbutton_new(buf, 0);
                dw_spinbutton_set_limits(pw->widget, max, min);
                dw_spinbutton_set_pos(pw->widget, purple_prefs_get_int(name));
                dwp_form_row(scroll, label, pw->widget);
                break;
            }
            case PURPLE_PREF_STRING:
            case PURPLE_PREF_PATH:
            {
                const char *value = pw->ptype == PURPLE_PREF_PATH ? purple_prefs_get_path(name)
                                                                  : purple_prefs_get_string(name);

                pw->multiline = purple_plugin_pref_get_type(pref) == PURPLE_PLUGIN_PREF_STRING_FORMAT &&
                                (purple_plugin_pref_get_format_type(pref) & PURPLE_STRING_FORMAT_TYPE_MULTILINE);
                if(pw->multiline)
                {
                    pw->widget = dw_mle_new(0);
                    dw_mle_set_word_wrap(pw->widget, TRUE);
                    if(value)
                        dw_mle_import(pw->widget, value, 0);
                    dw_box_pack_start(scroll, dwp_label(label), DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
                    dw_box_pack_start(scroll, pw->widget, 420, 90, TRUE, TRUE, 2);
                }
                else
                {
                    pw->widget = purple_plugin_pref_get_masked(pref)
                                 ? dw_entryfield_password_new(value ? value : "", 0)
                                 : dw_entryfield_new(value ? value : "", 0);
                    if(purple_plugin_pref_get_max_length(pref))
                        dw_entryfield_set_limit(pw->widget, purple_plugin_pref_get_max_length(pref));
                    dwp_form_row(scroll, label, pw->widget);
                }
                break;
            }
            default:
                g_free(pw->name);
                g_free(pw);
                pw = NULL;
                break;
        }
        if(pw)
            g_ptr_array_add(d->widgets, pw);
        g_free(label);
    }

    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Save", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(prefs_save), d);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(prefs_close), d);
    dw_signal_connect(d->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(prefs_close), d);
    dwp_window_present(d->window, 500, 400);
}

static int DWSIGNAL plugin_configure(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurplePlugin *p = selected_plugin();
    PurplePluginPrefFrame *frame;

    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    if(!p || !purple_plugin_is_loaded(p))
    {
        dw_messagebox("Plugins", DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_INFORMATION, "Load the plugin first.");
        return TRUE;
    }
    if(!p->info->prefs_info || !p->info->prefs_info->get_plugin_pref_frame)
    {
        dw_messagebox("Plugins", DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_INFORMATION, "This plugin has no preferences.");
        return TRUE;
    }
    frame = p->info->prefs_info->get_plugin_pref_frame(p);
    if(frame)
        show_pref_frame(p, frame);
    return TRUE;
}

static void run_plugin_action(gpointer act, gpointer plugin, const char *s)
{
    PurplePluginAction *pa = act;

    (void)s;
    if(plugin_exists(plugin) && purple_plugin_is_loaded(plugin) && pa->callback)
        pa->callback(pa);
}

static int DWSIGNAL plugin_actions_popup(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurplePlugin *p = selected_plugin();
    HMENUI menu;
    GList *actions, *a;
    gboolean any = FALSE;

    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    if(!p || !purple_plugin_is_loaded(p) || !PURPLE_PLUGIN_HAS_ACTIONS(p))
    {
        dw_messagebox("Plugins", DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_INFORMATION, "This plugin has no actions (or is not loaded).");
        return TRUE;
    }
    dwp_actions_reset();
    if(!plugin_actions)
        plugin_actions = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func((GDestroyNotify)purple_plugin_action_free);
    g_ptr_array_set_size(plugin_actions, 0);
    menu = dw_menu_new(0);
    actions = PURPLE_PLUGIN_ACTIONS(p, NULL);
    for(a = actions; a; a = a->next)
    {
        PurplePluginAction *pa = a->data;
        char *label;

        if(!pa)
        {
            dwp_menu_separator(menu);
            continue;
        }
        pa->plugin = p;
        g_ptr_array_add(plugin_actions, pa);
        label = dwp_mnemonic(pa->label);
        dwp_menu_add(menu, label, run_plugin_action, pa, p, NULL);
        g_free(label);
        any = TRUE;
    }
    g_list_free(actions);
    if(any)
        dwp_menu_popup_here(&menu, pd->window);
    else
        dw_menu_destroy(&menu);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL plugin_rescan(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    purple_plugins_probe(G_MODULE_SUFFIX);
    refresh();
    return TRUE;
}

static void plugins_free(gpointer p)
{
    g_free(p);
}

static int DWSIGNAL plugins_close(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PluginsDlg *d = pd;

    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    if(!d)
        return TRUE;
    pd = NULL;
    dwp_window_close_later(d->window, plugins_free, d);
    return TRUE;
}

void dwp_plugins_show(void)
{
    unsigned long flags[4];
    char *titles[4] = { "Loaded", "Name", "Version", "Summary" };
    HWND box, buttons;
    int z;

    if(pd)
    {
        dw_window_show(pd->window);
        dw_window_raise(pd->window);
        return;
    }
    for(z = 0; z < 4; z++)
        flags[z] = DW_CFA_STRING | DW_CFA_LEFT | DW_CFA_HORZSEPARATOR | DW_CFA_SEPARATOR;
    pd = g_new0(PluginsDlg, 1);
    pd->window = dwp_window_new("Plugins", 0, 0);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(pd->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    pd->list = dw_container_new(0, FALSE);
    dw_container_setup(pd->list, flags, titles, 4, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, pd->list, 560, 240, TRUE, TRUE, 2);
    dw_signal_connect(pd->list, DW_SIGNAL_ITEM_SELECT, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(plugin_select), NULL);
    dw_signal_connect(pd->list, DW_SIGNAL_ITEM_ENTER, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(plugin_enter), NULL);
    pd->details = dw_mle_new(0);
    dw_mle_set_editable(pd->details, FALSE);
    dw_mle_set_word_wrap(pd->details, TRUE);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, pd->details, 560, 110, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Load/Unload", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(plugin_toggle), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Configure...", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(plugin_configure), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Actions...", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(plugin_actions_popup), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Rescan", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(plugin_rescan), NULL);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(plugins_close), NULL);
    dw_signal_connect(pd->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(plugins_close), NULL);
    refresh();
    dwp_window_present(pd->window, 640, 470);
}

===== END FILE: src/plugins.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/pounces.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - buddy pounces: handler, manager window and editor.
 * Action names and attributes match Pidgin, so pounces.xml is shared.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

#define ACTION_OPEN_WINDOW   "open-window"
#define ACTION_POPUP         "popup-notify"
#define ACTION_SEND_MESSAGE  "send-message"
#define ACTION_EXECUTE       "execute-command"
#define ACTION_PLAY_SOUND    "play-sound"

static const struct {
    PurplePounceEvent event;
    const char *label;
    const char *sentence;
} event_labels[] = {
    { PURPLE_POUNCE_SIGNON,           "Signs on",              "%s has signed on" },
    { PURPLE_POUNCE_SIGNOFF,          "Signs off",             "%s has signed off" },
    { PURPLE_POUNCE_AWAY,             "Goes away",             "%s has gone away" },
    { PURPLE_POUNCE_AWAY_RETURN,      "Returns from away",     "%s has returned from away" },
    { PURPLE_POUNCE_IDLE,             "Becomes idle",          "%s has become idle" },
    { PURPLE_POUNCE_IDLE_RETURN,      "Is no longer idle",     "%s is no longer idle" },
    { PURPLE_POUNCE_TYPING,           "Starts typing",         "%s has started typing to you" },
    { PURPLE_POUNCE_TYPED,            "Pauses while typing",   "%s has paused while typing to you" },
    { PURPLE_POUNCE_TYPING_STOPPED,   "Stops typing",          "%s has stopped typing to you" },
    { PURPLE_POUNCE_MESSAGE_RECEIVED, "Sends a message",       "%s has sent you a message" },
};
#define N_EVENTS ((int)(sizeof(event_labels) / sizeof(event_labels[0])))

typedef struct {
    HWND window, list;
    gboolean refresh_pending;
} Manager;

typedef struct {
    PurplePounce *pounce;           /* NULL for a new pounce */
    HWND window, buddy, events[N_EVENTS], away_only;
    HWND open_window, popup, send_message, message, execute, command, play_sound, sound_file, save;
    DwpAccountCombo *account;
} Editor;

static Manager *mgr = NULL;
static GList *editors = NULL;

static void manager_schedule_refresh(void);

/* ================================================================ handler */

static PurpleConversation *im_conversation(PurpleAccount *account, const char *who)
{
    PurpleConversation *conv = purple_find_conversation_with_account(PURPLE_CONV_TYPE_IM, who, account);

    if(!conv)
        conv = purple_conversation_new(PURPLE_CONV_TYPE_IM, account, who);
    return conv;
}

static char *event_sentence(PurplePounceEvent events, const char *who)
{
    int z;

    for(z = 0; z < N_EVENTS; z++)
        if(events & event_labels[z].event)
            return g_strdup_printf(event_labels[z].sentence, who);
    return g_strdup_printf("Unknown pounce event for %s", who);
}

static void pounce_cb(PurplePounce *pounce, PurplePounceEvent events, void *data)
{
    PurpleAccount *account = purple_pounce_get_pouncer(pounce);
    char *pouncee = g_strdup(purple_pounce_get_pouncee(pounce));
    PurpleBuddy *buddy = purple_find_buddy(account, pouncee);
    const char *alias = buddy ? purple_buddy_get_alias(buddy) : pouncee;
    gboolean connected = purple_account_is_connected(account);

    (void)data;
    if(purple_pounce_action_is_enabled(pounce, ACTION_OPEN_WINDOW) && connected)
    {
        PurpleConversation *conv = im_conversation(account, pouncee);

        if(conv)
            purple_conversation_present(conv);
    }
    if(purple_pounce_action_is_enabled(pounce, ACTION_POPUP))
    {
        char *primary = event_sentence(events, alias);
        GString *secondary = g_string_new(NULL);
        char *acct = dwp_account_display_name(account);

        g_string_append(secondary, acct);
        if(buddy && (events & PURPLE_POUNCE_AWAY))
        {
            PurpleStatus *status = purple_presence_get_active_status(purple_buddy_get_presence(buddy));
            const char *msg = status ? purple_status_get_attr_string(status, "message") : NULL;

            if(msg && *msg)
            {
                char *plain = purple_markup_strip_html(msg);

                g_string_append_printf(secondary, "\n%s", plain);
                g_free(plain);
            }
        }
        purple_notify_info(NULL, "Buddy Pounce", primary, secondary->str);
        g_string_free(secondary, TRUE);
        g_free(acct);
        g_free(primary);
    }
    if(purple_pounce_action_is_enabled(pounce, ACTION_SEND_MESSAGE) && connected)
    {
        const char *message = purple_pounce_action_get_attribute(pounce, ACTION_SEND_MESSAGE, "message");
        char *trimmed = g_strstrip(g_strdup(message ? message : ""));

        if(*trimmed)
        {
            PurpleConversation *conv = im_conversation(account, pouncee);

            if(conv)
            {
                char *escaped = g_markup_escape_text(message, -1);
                char **parts = g_strsplit(escaped, "\n", -1);
                char *html = g_strjoinv("<br>", parts);

                purple_conv_im_send(PURPLE_CONV_IM(conv), html);
                g_free(html);
                g_strfreev(parts);
                g_free(escaped);
            }
        }
        g_free(trimmed);
    }
    if(purple_pounce_action_is_enabled(pounce, ACTION_EXECUTE))
    {
        const char *command = purple_pounce_action_get_attribute(pounce, ACTION_EXECUTE, "command");

        if(command && *command)
        {
            GError *error = NULL;

            /* The user's own command line from the pounce editor. */
            if(!g_spawn_command_line_async(command, &error))
            {
                purple_debug_error("pounces", "Could not run pounce command: %s\n",
                                   error ? error->message : "unknown error");
                if(error)
                    g_error_free(error);
            }
        }
    }
    if(purple_pounce_action_is_enabled(pounce, ACTION_PLAY_SOUND))
    {
        const char *file = purple_pounce_action_get_attribute(pounce, ACTION_PLAY_SOUND, "filename");

        if(file && *file)
            purple_sound_play_file(file, NULL);
        else
            purple_sound_play_event(PURPLE_SOUND_POUNCE_DEFAULT, NULL);
    }
    g_free(pouncee);
    manager_schedule_refresh();
}

static void new_pounce(PurplePounce *pounce)
{
    purple_pounce_action_register(pounce, ACTION_OPEN_WINDOW);
    purple_pounce_action_register(pounce, ACTION_POPUP);
    purple_pounce_action_register(pounce, ACTION_SEND_MESSAGE);
    purple_pounce_action_register(pounce, ACTION_EXECUTE);
    purple_pounce_action_register(pounce, ACTION_PLAY_SOUND);
    manager_schedule_refresh();
}

static void editor_free(gpointer p)
{
    Editor *e = p;

    dwp_account_combo_free(e->account);
    g_free(e);
}

static void editor_close(Editor *e)
{
    editors = g_list_remove(editors, e);
    dwp_window_close_later(e->window, editor_free, e);
}

static void free_pounce(PurplePounce *pounce)
{
    GList *l, *next;

    /* Close editors for this pounce; it is freed right after this. */
    for(l = editors; l; l = next)
    {
        Editor *e = l->data;

        next = l->next;
        if(e->pounce == pounce)
            editor_close(e);
    }
    if(mgr)
        dw_container_clear(mgr->list, TRUE);
    manager_schedule_refresh();
}

void dwp_pounces_init(void)
{
    purple_pounces_register_handler(DWP_UI_ID, pounce_cb, new_pounce, free_pounce);
}

static gboolean pounce_exists(PurplePounce *pounce)
{
    return pounce && g_list_find(purple_pounces_get_all(), pounce) != NULL;
}

/* ================================================================= editor */

static int DWSIGNAL editor_cancel(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    editor_close(data);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL editor_browse(HWND w, void *data)
{
    Editor *e = data;
    char *path;

    (void)w;
    path = dw_file_browse("Pounce Sound", NULL, "wav", DW_FILE_OPEN);
    if(path)
    {
        if(g_list_find(editors, e))
            dw_window_set_text(e->sound_file, path);
        dw_free(path);
    }
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL editor_save(HWND w, void *data)
{
    Editor *e = data;
    PurpleAccount *account = dwp_account_combo_get(e->account);
    char *buddy = dwp_get_text_trimmed(e->buddy);
    char *message, *command, *sound;
    PurplePounceEvent events = PURPLE_POUNCE_NONE;
    PurplePounce *p;
    int z;

    (void)w;
    for(z = 0; z < N_EVENTS; z++)
        if(dw_checkbox_get(e->events[z]))
            events |= event_labels[z].event;
    if(!account || !*buddy)
    {
        dw_messagebox("Buddy Pounce", DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_WARNING, "Please choose an account and enter a buddy name.");
        g_free(buddy);
        return TRUE;
    }
    if(events == PURPLE_POUNCE_NONE)
    {
        dw_messagebox("Buddy Pounce", DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_WARNING, "Please choose at least one event.");
        g_free(buddy);
        return TRUE;
    }
    if(e->pounce && !pounce_exists(e->pounce))
        e->pounce = NULL;
    p = e->pounce;
    if(!p)
    {
        p = purple_pounce_new(DWP_UI_ID, account, buddy, events, PURPLE_POUNCE_OPTION_NONE);
        if(!p)
        {
            dw_messagebox("Buddy Pounce", DW_MB_OK | DW_MB_WARNING, "The pounce could not be created.");
            g_free(buddy);
            return TRUE;
        }
    }
    else
    {
        purple_pounce_set_pouncer(p, account);
        purple_pounce_set_pouncee(p, buddy);
        purple_pounce_set_events(p, events);
    }
    purple_pounce_set_options(p, dw_checkbox_get(e->away_only) ? PURPLE_POUNCE_OPTION_AWAY
                                                               : PURPLE_POUNCE_OPTION_NONE);
    purple_pounce_action_set_enabled(p, ACTION_OPEN_WINDOW, dw_checkbox_get(e->open_window) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_pounce_action_set_enabled(p, ACTION_POPUP, dw_checkbox_get(e->popup) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_pounce_action_set_enabled(p, ACTION_SEND_MESSAGE, dw_checkbox_get(e->send_message) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_pounce_action_set_enabled(p, ACTION_EXECUTE, dw_checkbox_get(e->execute) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_pounce_action_set_enabled(p, ACTION_PLAY_SOUND, dw_checkbox_get(e->play_sound) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    message = dwp_mle_get_text(e->message);
    command = dwp_get_text_trimmed(e->command);
    sound = dwp_get_text_trimmed(e->sound_file);
    purple_pounce_action_set_attribute(p, ACTION_SEND_MESSAGE, "message", *message ? message : NULL);
    purple_pounce_action_set_attribute(p, ACTION_EXECUTE, "command", *command ? command : NULL);
    purple_pounce_action_set_attribute(p, ACTION_PLAY_SOUND, "filename", *sound ? sound : NULL);
    /* "save" means keep the pounce after it fires. */
    purple_pounce_set_save(p, dw_checkbox_get(e->save) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    g_free(message);
    g_free(command);
    g_free(sound);
    g_free(buddy);
    editor_close(e);
    manager_schedule_refresh();
    return TRUE;
}

void dwp_pounce_edit(PurplePounce *pounce, PurpleAccount *account, const char *buddy)
{
    Editor *e;
    HWND box, group, grid, row, buttons;
    GList *l;
    int z;

    for(l = editors; l && pounce; l = l->next)
    {
        Editor *open = l->data;

        if(open->pounce == pounce)
        {
            dw_window_show(open->window);
            dw_window_raise(open->window);
            return;
        }
    }
    e = g_new0(Editor, 1);
    e->pounce = pounce;
    e->window = dwp_window_new(pounce ? "Edit Buddy Pounce" : "New Buddy Pounce", 0, 0);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(e->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);

    group = dw_groupbox_new(DW_VERT, 2, "Pounce Who");
    dw_box_pack_start(box, group, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    e->account = dwp_account_combo_new(FALSE, NULL);
    if(pounce)
        dwp_account_combo_set(e->account, purple_pounce_get_pouncer(pounce));
    else if(account)
        dwp_account_combo_set(e->account, account);
    dwp_form_row(group, "Account:", dwp_account_combo_widget(e->account));
    e->buddy = dw_entryfield_new(pounce ? purple_pounce_get_pouncee(pounce) : (buddy ? buddy : ""), 0);
    dwp_form_row(group, "Buddy name:", e->buddy);

    group = dw_groupbox_new(DW_VERT, 2, "Pounce When Buddy...");
    dw_box_pack_start(box, group, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    grid = 0;
    for(z = 0; z < N_EVENTS; z++)
    {
        if(z % 2 == 0)
        {
            grid = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
            dw_box_pack_start(group, grid, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 0);
        }
        e->events[z] = dw_checkbox_new(event_labels[z].label, 0);
        dw_checkbox_set(e->events[z], pounce ? (purple_pounce_get_events(pounce) & event_labels[z].event) != 0
                                             : event_labels[z].event == PURPLE_POUNCE_SIGNON);
        dw_box_pack_start(grid, e->events[z], 200, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    }

    group = dw_groupbox_new(DW_VERT, 2, "Action");
    dw_box_pack_start(box, group, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, TRUE, 2);
    e->open_window = dw_checkbox_new("Open an IM window", 0);
    dw_checkbox_set(e->open_window, pounce ? purple_pounce_action_is_enabled(pounce, ACTION_OPEN_WINDOW) : FALSE);
    dw_box_pack_start(group, e->open_window, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    e->popup = dw_checkbox_new("Pop up a notification", 0);
    dw_checkbox_set(e->popup, pounce ? purple_pounce_action_is_enabled(pounce, ACTION_POPUP) : TRUE);
    dw_box_pack_start(group, e->popup, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    e->send_message = dw_checkbox_new("Send a message:", 0);
    dw_checkbox_set(e->send_message, pounce ? purple_pounce_action_is_enabled(pounce, ACTION_SEND_MESSAGE) : FALSE);
    dw_box_pack_start(group, e->send_message, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    e->message = dw_mle_new(0);
    dw_mle_set_word_wrap(e->message, TRUE);
    if(pounce)
    {
        const char *m = purple_pounce_action_get_attribute(pounce, ACTION_SEND_MESSAGE, "message");

        if(m)
            dwp_mle_set_text(e->message, m);
    }
    dw_box_pack_start(group, e->message, 380, 50, TRUE, TRUE, 1);
    e->execute = dw_checkbox_new("Execute a command:", 0);
    dw_checkbox_set(e->execute, pounce ? purple_pounce_action_is_enabled(pounce, ACTION_EXECUTE) : FALSE);
    e->command = dw_entryfield_new(pounce && purple_pounce_action_get_attribute(pounce, ACTION_EXECUTE, "command")
                                   ? purple_pounce_action_get_attribute(pounce, ACTION_EXECUTE, "command") : "", 0);
    row = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(group, row, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    dw_box_pack_start(row, e->execute, 160, DW_SIZE_AUTO, FALSE, FALSE, 1);
    dw_box_pack_start(row, e->command, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    e->play_sound = dw_checkbox_new("Play a sound:", 0);
    dw_checkbox_set(e->play_sound, pounce ? purple_pounce_action_is_enabled(pounce, ACTION_PLAY_SOUND) : FALSE);
    e->sound_file = dw_entryfield_new(pounce && purple_pounce_action_get_attribute(pounce, ACTION_PLAY_SOUND, "filename")
                                      ? purple_pounce_action_get_attribute(pounce, ACTION_PLAY_SOUND, "filename") : "", 0);
    dw_window_set_tooltip(e->sound_file, "Empty = default pounce sound");
    row = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(group, row, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    dw_box_pack_start(row, e->play_sound, 160, DW_SIZE_AUTO, FALSE, FALSE, 1);
    dw_box_pack_start(row, e->sound_file, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    dwp_button(row, "Browse...", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(editor_browse), e);

    group = dw_groupbox_new(DW_VERT, 2, "Options");
    dw_box_pack_start(box, group, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    e->away_only = dw_checkbox_new("Pounce only when my status is not Available", 0);
    dw_checkbox_set(e->away_only, pounce ? (purple_pounce_get_options(pounce) & PURPLE_POUNCE_OPTION_AWAY) != 0 : FALSE);
    dw_box_pack_start(group, e->away_only, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    e->save = dw_checkbox_new("Recurring (keep this pounce after it fires)", 0);
    dw_checkbox_set(e->save, pounce ? purple_pounce_get_save(pounce) : FALSE);
    dw_box_pack_start(group, e->save, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);

    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Save", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(editor_save), e);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Cancel", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(editor_cancel), e);
    dw_signal_connect(e->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(editor_cancel), e);
    editors = g_list_append(editors, e);
    dwp_window_present(e->window, 480, 600);
}

/* ================================================================ manager */

static char *events_text(PurplePounceEvent events)
{
    GString *s = g_string_new(NULL);
    int z;

    for(z = 0; z < N_EVENTS; z++)
        if(events & event_labels[z].event)
            g_string_append_printf(s, "%s%s", s->len ? ", " : "", event_labels[z].label);
    return g_string_free(s, FALSE);
}

static void manager_refresh(gpointer unused)
{
    GList *all, *l;
    int count, row = 0;
    void *alloc;

    (void)unused;
    if(!mgr)
        return;
    mgr->refresh_pending = FALSE;
    dw_container_clear(mgr->list, TRUE);
    all = purple_pounces_get_all_for_ui(DWP_UI_ID);
    count = (int)g_list_length(all);
    if(count)
    {
        alloc = dw_container_alloc(mgr->list, count);
        for(l = all; l; l = l->next, row++)
        {
            PurplePounce *p = l->data;
            char *who = (char *)purple_pounce_get_pouncee(p);
            char *acct = dwp_account_display_name(purple_pounce_get_pouncer(p));
            char *ev = events_text(purple_pounce_get_events(p));
            char *rec = purple_pounce_get_save(p) ? "Yes" : "No";

            dw_container_set_item(mgr->list, alloc, 0, row, &who);
            dw_container_set_item(mgr->list, alloc, 1, row, &acct);
            dw_container_set_item(mgr->list, alloc, 2, row, &ev);
            dw_container_set_item(mgr->list, alloc, 3, row, &rec);
            dw_container_set_row_title(alloc, row, who);
            dw_container_set_row_data(alloc, row, p);
            g_free(ev);
            g_free(acct);
        }
        dw_container_insert(mgr->list, alloc, count);
        dw_container_optimize(mgr->list);
    }
    g_list_free(all);
}

static void manager_schedule_refresh(void)
{
    if(!mgr || mgr->refresh_pending)
        return;
    mgr->refresh_pending = TRUE;
    dwp_defer(manager_refresh, NULL);
}

static PurplePounce *selected_pounce(void)
{
    PurplePounce *p;

    if(!mgr)
        return NULL;
    p = (PurplePounce *)dw_container_query_start(mgr->list, DW_CRA_SELECTED | DW_CR_RETDATA);
    return pounce_exists(p) ? p : NULL;
}

static int DWSIGNAL mgr_add(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w; (void)data;
    dwp_pounce_edit(NULL, NULL, NULL);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL mgr_modify(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurplePounce *p = selected_pounce();

    (void)w; (void)data;
    if(p)
        dwp_pounce_edit(p, NULL, NULL);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL mgr_enter(HWND w, char *text, void *data, void *itemdata)
{
    (void)w; (void)text; (void)data;
    if(pounce_exists(itemdata))
        dwp_pounce_edit(itemdata, NULL, NULL);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL mgr_delete(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurplePounce *p = selected_pounce();
    char *who;
    gboolean ok;

    (void)w; (void)data;
    if(!p)
        return TRUE;
    who = g_strdup(purple_pounce_get_pouncee(p));
    ok = dwp_confirm("Delete Pounce", "Delete the buddy pounce on %s?", who);
    g_free(who);
    if(ok && pounce_exists(p))
        purple_pounce_destroy(p);
    manager_schedule_refresh();
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL mgr_close(HWND w, void *data)
{
    Manager *m = mgr;

    (void)w; (void)data;
    if(!m)
        return TRUE;
    mgr = NULL;
    dwp_window_close_later(m->window, g_free, m);
    return TRUE;
}

void dwp_pounces_show(void)
{
    unsigned long flags[4];
    char *titles[4] = { "Pounce Target", "Account", "Events", "Recurring" };
    HWND box, buttons;
    int z;

    if(mgr)
    {
        dw_window_show(mgr->window);
        dw_window_raise(mgr->window);
        return;
    }
    for(z = 0; z < 4; z++)
        flags[z] = DW_CFA_STRING | DW_CFA_LEFT | DW_CFA_HORZSEPARATOR | DW_CFA_SEPARATOR;
    mgr = g_new0(Manager, 1);
    mgr->window = dwp_window_new("Buddy Pounces", 0, 0);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(mgr->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    mgr->list = dw_container_new(0, FALSE);
    dw_container_setup(mgr->list, flags, titles, 4, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, mgr->list, 560, 240, TRUE, TRUE, 2);
    dw_signal_connect(mgr->list, DW_SIGNAL_ITEM_ENTER, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(mgr_enter), NULL);
    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Add...", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(mgr_add), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Modify...", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(mgr_modify), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Delete", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(mgr_delete), NULL);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(mgr_close), NULL);
    dw_signal_connect(mgr->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(mgr_close), NULL);
    manager_refresh(NULL);
    dwp_window_present(mgr->window, 620, 330);
}

===== END FILE: src/pounces.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/prefs.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - preferences dialog.
 * Changes are applied with Save; Close discards them.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

typedef struct {
    HWND enabled, file;
    const DwpSoundEvent *event;
} SoundRow;

typedef struct {
    HWND window, notebook;
    /* interface */
    HWND show_offline, show_empty, tray, raise_new, notify_msgs;
    /* conversations */
    HWND timestamps, reactions, emoji, send_typing, emoji_file;
    /* sounds */
    HWND sound_method, sound_command, sound_mute, sound_focus, sound_while;
    SoundRow *sounds;
    int nsounds;
    /* status / idle */
    HWND idle_reporting, away_when_idle, mins_before_away, auto_reply;
    /* logging */
    HWND log_ims, log_chats, log_system;
    /* network */
    HWND proxy_type, proxy_host, proxy_port, proxy_user, proxy_pass, stun;
} Prefs;

static Prefs *pd = NULL;

static char *method_labels[] = { "Automatic", "Command", "Beep only", "No sounds" };
static const char *const method_values[] = { "automatic", "command", "beep", "none" };
static char *while_labels[] = { "Only when available", "Only when not available", "Always" };
static char *idle_labels[] = { "Never", "From last sent message", "Based on keyboard or mouse use" };
static const char *const idle_values[] = { "none", "purple", "system" };
static char *reply_labels[] = { "Never", "When away", "When both away and idle" };
static const char *const reply_values[] = { "never", "away", "awayidle" };
static char *proxy_labels[] = { "No proxy", "Use environment settings", "HTTP", "SOCKS 4", "SOCKS 5", "Tor/Privacy (SOCKS5)" };
static const char *const proxy_values[] = { "none", "envvar", "http", "socks4", "socks5", "tor" };

#define COUNT(a) ((int)(sizeof(a) / sizeof((a)[0])))

static int string_index(const char *const *values, int count, const char *value)
{
    int z;

    for(z = 0; z < count; z++)
        if(purple_strequal(values[z], value))
            return z;
    return 0;
}

static HWND check(HWND box, const char *label, gboolean on)
{
    HWND c = dw_checkbox_new(label, 0);

    dw_checkbox_set(c, on);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, c, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    return c;
}

static HWND combo(HWND box, const char *label, char **items, int count, int sel)
{
    HWND c = dw_combobox_new("", 0);

    dwp_combo_fill(c, items, count, sel);
    dwp_form_row(box, label, c);
    return c;
}

static HWND entry(HWND box, const char *label, const char *value, gboolean masked)
{
    HWND e = masked ? dw_entryfield_password_new(value ? value : "", 0) : dw_entryfield_new(value ? value : "", 0);

    dwp_form_row(box, label, e);
    return e;
}

static HWND spin(HWND box, const char *label, int value, int min, int max)
{
    char buf[32];
    HWND s;

    g_snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%d", value);
    s = dw_spinbutton_new(buf, 0);
    dw_spinbutton_set_limits(s, max, min);
    dw_spinbutton_set_pos(s, value);
    dwp_form_row(box, label, s);
    return s;
}

static HWND new_page(HWND notebook, const char *title, gboolean scroll)
{
    HWND page = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4), inner = page;
    unsigned long id = dw_notebook_page_new(notebook, 0, FALSE);

    dw_notebook_pack(notebook, id, page);
    dw_notebook_page_set_text(notebook, id, title);
    if(scroll)
    {
        inner = dw_scrollbox_new(DW_VERT, 2);
        dw_box_pack_start(page, inner, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    }
    return inner;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ sound rows */

static int DWSIGNAL sound_test(HWND w, void *data)
{
    SoundRow *row = data;
    char *file;

    (void)w;
    if(!pd)
        return TRUE;
    file = dwp_get_text_trimmed(row->file);
    if(!*file)
    {
        g_free(file);
        file = dwp_sound_default_file(row->event);
    }
    dwp_sound_test(file);
    g_free(file);
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL sound_browse(HWND w, void *data)
{
    SoundRow *row = data;
    Prefs *owner = pd;
    char *path;

    (void)w;
    path = dw_file_browse("Choose Sound", NULL, "wav", DW_FILE_OPEN);
    if(path)
    {
        /* The dialog may have been closed during the nested loop. */
        if(pd && pd == owner)
            dw_window_set_text(row->file, path);
        dw_free(path);
    }
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL sound_reset(HWND w, void *data)
{
    SoundRow *row = data;

    (void)w;
    if(pd)
        dw_window_set_text(row->file, "");
    return TRUE;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ save */

static void prefs_free(gpointer p)
{
    Prefs *d = p;

    g_free(d->sounds);
    g_free(d);
}

static int DWSIGNAL prefs_close(HWND w, void *data)
{
    Prefs *d = pd;

    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    if(!d)
        return TRUE;
    pd = NULL;
    dwp_window_close_later(d->window, prefs_free, d);
    return TRUE;
}

static void set_combo_string(HWND c, char **labels, const char *const *values, int count, const char *pref)
{
    int index = dwp_combo_index(c, labels, count);

    if(index >= 0)
        purple_prefs_set_string(pref, values[index]);
}

static int DWSIGNAL prefs_save(HWND w, void *data)
{
    Prefs *d = pd;
    int z, index;
    char *s;

    (void)data;
    if(!d)
        return TRUE;
    /* Interface */
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_OFFLINE, dw_checkbox_get(d->show_offline) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_EMPTY, dw_checkbox_get(d->show_empty) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_TRAY_ENABLED, dw_checkbox_get(d->tray) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_RAISE_NEW, dw_checkbox_get(d->raise_new) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_NOTIFY_MSGS, dw_checkbox_get(d->notify_msgs) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    /* Conversations */
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_TIMESTAMPS, dw_checkbox_get(d->timestamps) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_SHOW_REACTIONS, dw_checkbox_get(d->reactions) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_EMOJI_CODES, dw_checkbox_get(d->emoji) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_bool("/purple/conversations/im/send_typing", dw_checkbox_get(d->send_typing) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    /* Sounds */
    set_combo_string(d->sound_method, method_labels, method_values, COUNT(method_labels), DWP_PREF_SOUND_METHOD);
    s = dwp_get_text_trimmed(d->sound_command);
    purple_prefs_set_string(DWP_PREF_SOUND_COMMAND, s);
    g_free(s);
    dwp_sound_set_muted(dw_checkbox_get(d->sound_mute) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_SOUND_FOCUS, dw_checkbox_get(d->sound_focus) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    index = dwp_combo_index(d->sound_while, while_labels, COUNT(while_labels));
    if(index >= 0)
        purple_prefs_set_int("/purple/sound/while_status", index + 1);
    for(z = 0; z < d->nsounds; z++)
    {
        char *en = dwp_sound_enabled_pref(d->sounds[z].event);
        char *fp = dwp_sound_file_pref(d->sounds[z].event);
        char *file = dwp_get_text_trimmed(d->sounds[z].file);

        purple_prefs_set_bool(en, dw_checkbox_get(d->sounds[z].enabled) ? TRUE : FALSE);
        purple_prefs_set_path(fp, file);
        g_free(file);
        g_free(fp);
        g_free(en);
    }
    /* Status / idle */
    set_combo_string(d->idle_reporting, idle_labels, idle_values, COUNT(idle_labels), "/purple/away/idle_reporting");
    purple_prefs_set_bool("/purple/away/away_when_idle", dw_checkbox_get(d->away_when_idle) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_int("/purple/away/mins_before_away", (int)dw_spinbutton_get_pos(d->mins_before_away));
    set_combo_string(d->auto_reply, reply_labels, reply_values, COUNT(reply_labels), "/purple/away/auto_reply");
    /* Logging */
    purple_prefs_set_bool("/purple/logging/log_ims", dw_checkbox_get(d->log_ims) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_bool("/purple/logging/log_chats", dw_checkbox_get(d->log_chats) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    purple_prefs_set_bool("/purple/logging/log_system", dw_checkbox_get(d->log_system) ? TRUE : FALSE);
    /* Network */
    set_combo_string(d->proxy_type, proxy_labels, proxy_values, COUNT(proxy_labels), "/purple/proxy/type");
    s = dwp_get_text_trimmed(d->proxy_host);
    purple_prefs_set_string("/purple/proxy/host", s);
    g_free(s);
    purple_prefs_set_int("/purple/proxy/port", (int)dw_spinbutton_get_pos(d->proxy_port));
    s = dwp_get_text_trimmed(d->proxy_user);
    purple_prefs_set_string("/purple/proxy/username", s);
    g_free(s);
    s = dwp_get_text(d->proxy_pass);
    purple_prefs_set_string("/purple/proxy/password", s);
    g_free(s);
    s = dwp_get_text_trimmed(d->stun);
    purple_prefs_set_string("/purple/network/stun_server", s);
    g_free(s);

    dwp_blist_apply_prefs();
    dwp_tray_apply_prefs();
    return prefs_close(w, NULL);
}

void dwp_prefs_show(void)
{
    HWND box, page, buttons, row, group;
    const DwpSoundEvent *events;
    char *mins;
    int z, count;

    if(pd)
    {
        dw_window_show(pd->window);
        dw_window_raise(pd->window);
        return;
    }
    pd = g_new0(Prefs, 1);
    pd->window = dwp_window_new("Preferences", 0, 0);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(pd->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    pd->notebook = dw_notebook_new(0, TRUE);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, pd->notebook, 560, 420, TRUE, TRUE, 2);

    /* ---- Interface */
    page = new_page(pd->notebook, "Interface", FALSE);
    pd->show_offline = check(page, "Show offline buddies", purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_OFFLINE));
    pd->show_empty = check(page, "Show empty groups", purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_EMPTY));
    pd->tray = check(page, "Show system tray icon (closing the buddy list hides it)",
                     purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_TRAY_ENABLED));
    pd->raise_new = check(page, "Raise the conversation window for new conversations",
                          purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_RAISE_NEW));
    pd->notify_msgs = check(page, "Desktop notification for new messages in background tabs",
                            purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_NOTIFY_MSGS));
    if(dw_feature_get(DW_FEATURE_NOTIFICATION) != DW_FEATURE_ENABLED)
        dw_window_disable(pd->notify_msgs);
    if(dw_feature_get(DW_FEATURE_TASK_BAR) == DW_FEATURE_UNSUPPORTED)
        dw_window_disable(pd->tray);

    /* ---- Conversations */
    page = new_page(pd->notebook, "Conversations", FALSE);
    pd->timestamps = check(page, "Show timestamps", purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_TIMESTAMPS));
    pd->reactions = check(page, "Show reaction notices by default (\"Reacted with ...\")",
                          purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_SHOW_REACTIONS));
    pd->emoji = check(page, "Convert :emoji_codes: to emoji by default",
                      purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_EMOJI_CODES));
    pd->send_typing = check(page, "Notify buddies that you are typing to them",
                            purple_prefs_get_bool("/purple/conversations/im/send_typing"));
    {
        char *path = dwp_emoji_user_map_path();
        char *text = g_strdup_printf("Custom emoji codes: %s (one \"code = emoji\" per line)", path);
        HWND label = dw_text_new(text, 0);

        dw_window_set_style(label, DW_DT_WORDBREAK, DW_DT_WORDBREAK);
        dw_box_pack_start(page, label, 500, 40, TRUE, FALSE, 4);
        g_free(text);
        g_free(path);
    }
    dw_box_pack_start(page, dwp_label("Per-conversation overrides are in the conversation's Options menu."),
                      DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 4);

    /* ---- Sounds */
    page = new_page(pd->notebook, "Sounds", TRUE);
    pd->sound_method = combo(page, "Method:", method_labels, COUNT(method_labels),
                             string_index(method_values, COUNT(method_values),
                                          purple_prefs_get_string(DWP_PREF_SOUND_METHOD)));
    pd->sound_command = entry(page, "Command (%s = file):", purple_prefs_get_string(DWP_PREF_SOUND_COMMAND), FALSE);
    pd->sound_while = combo(page, "Enable sounds:", while_labels, COUNT(while_labels),
                            CLAMP(purple_prefs_get_int("/purple/sound/while_status"), 1, 3) - 1);
    pd->sound_mute = check(page, "Mute all sounds", dwp_sound_muted());
    pd->sound_focus = check(page, "Play sounds when the conversation has focus",
                            purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_SOUND_FOCUS));
    group = dw_groupbox_new(DW_VERT, 2, "Sound Events (empty file = default)");
    dw_box_pack_start(page, group, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    events = dwp_sound_events(&count);
    pd->sounds = g_new0(SoundRow, count);
    pd->nsounds = count;
    for(z = 0; z < count; z++)
    {
        SoundRow *sr = &pd->sounds[z];
        char *en = dwp_sound_enabled_pref(&events[z]);
        char *fp = dwp_sound_file_pref(&events[z]);
        const char *file = purple_prefs_get_path(fp);

        sr->event = &events[z];
        row = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
        dw_box_pack_start(group, row, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
        sr->enabled = dw_checkbox_new(events[z].label, 0);
        dw_checkbox_set(sr->enabled, purple_prefs_get_bool(en));
        dw_box_pack_start(row, sr->enabled, 230, DW_SIZE_AUTO, FALSE, FALSE, 1);
        sr->file = dw_entryfield_new(file ? file : "", 0);
        dw_box_pack_start(row, sr->file, 120, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
        dwp_button(row, "...", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(sound_browse), sr);
        dwp_button(row, "Reset", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(sound_reset), sr);
        dwp_button(row, "Test", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(sound_test), sr);
        g_free(fp);
        g_free(en);
    }

    /* ---- Status / Idle */
    page = new_page(pd->notebook, "Status / Idle", FALSE);
    pd->idle_reporting = combo(page, "Report idle time:", idle_labels, COUNT(idle_labels),
                               string_index(idle_values, COUNT(idle_values),
                                            purple_prefs_get_string("/purple/away/idle_reporting")));
    pd->away_when_idle = check(page, "Change status to Away when idle",
                               purple_prefs_get_bool("/purple/away/away_when_idle"));
    pd->mins_before_away = spin(page, "Minutes before becoming idle:",
                                purple_prefs_get_int("/purple/away/mins_before_away"), 1, 1440);
    pd->auto_reply = combo(page, "Auto-reply:", reply_labels, COUNT(reply_labels),
                           string_index(reply_values, COUNT(reply_values),
                                        purple_prefs_get_string("/purple/away/auto_reply")));
    mins = g_strdup_printf("System idle source: %s", dwp_idle_backend_name());
    dw_box_pack_start(page, dwp_label(mins), DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 4);
    g_free(mins);

    /* ---- Logging */
    page = new_page(pd->notebook, "Logging", FALSE);
    pd->log_ims = check(page, "Log all instant messages", purple_prefs_get_bool("/purple/logging/log_ims"));
    pd->log_chats = check(page, "Log all chats", purple_prefs_get_bool("/purple/logging/log_chats"));
    pd->log_system = check(page, "Log all status changes to system log",
                           purple_prefs_get_bool("/purple/logging/log_system"));

    /* ---- Network */
    page = new_page(pd->notebook, "Network", FALSE);
    pd->stun = entry(page, "STUN server:", purple_prefs_get_string("/purple/network/stun_server"), FALSE);
    group = dw_groupbox_new(DW_VERT, 2, "Global Proxy");
    dw_box_pack_start(page, group, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    pd->proxy_type = combo(group, "Type:", proxy_labels, COUNT(proxy_labels),
                           string_index(proxy_values, COUNT(proxy_values), purple_prefs_get_string("/purple/proxy/type")));
    pd->proxy_host = entry(group, "Host:", purple_prefs_get_string("/purple/proxy/host"), FALSE);
    pd->proxy_port = spin(group, "Port:", purple_prefs_get_int("/purple/proxy/port"), 0, 65535);
    pd->proxy_user = entry(group, "User:", purple_prefs_get_string("/purple/proxy/username"), FALSE);
    pd->proxy_pass = entry(group, "Password:", purple_prefs_get_string("/purple/proxy/password"), TRUE);

    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Save", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(prefs_save), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(prefs_close), NULL);
    dw_signal_connect(pd->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(prefs_close), NULL);
    dwp_window_present(pd->window, 640, 540);
}
