===== BEGIN FILE: src/sound.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - sounds.
 *
 * Playback methods: "automatic" (Windows: PlaySound; Unix: pw-play, paplay
 * or aplay; OS/2: the configured command, else a beep), "command" (user
 * command, %s = file name), "beep" and "none".  Event filtering follows
 * Pidgin: per-event switches, the "sounds while" status rule, the focus
 * rule and a short mute after sign-on.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

#ifdef _WIN32
#include <windows.h>
#include <mmsystem.h>
#endif

static const DwpSoundEvent events[] = {
    { PURPLE_SOUND_BUDDY_ARRIVE,   "login",          "Buddy logs in",                        "login.wav",   TRUE  },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_BUDDY_LEAVE,    "logout",         "Buddy logs out",                       "logout.wav",  TRUE  },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_RECEIVE,        "im_recv",        "Message received",                     "receive.wav", TRUE  },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_FIRST_RECEIVE,  "first_im_recv",  "Message received begins conversation", "alert.wav",   FALSE },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_SEND,           "send_im",        "Message sent",                         "send.wav",    TRUE  },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_JOIN,      "join_chat",      "Person enters chat",                   "login.wav",   FALSE },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_LEAVE,     "left_chat",      "Person leaves chat",                   "logout.wav",  FALSE },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_YOU_SAY,   "send_chat_msg",  "You talk in chat",                     "send.wav",    FALSE },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_SAY,       "chat_msg_recv",  "Others talk in chat",                  "receive.wav", FALSE },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_POUNCE_DEFAULT, "pounce_default", "Default buddy pounce sound",           "alert.wav",   TRUE  },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_NICK,      "nick_said",      "Someone says your name in chat",       "alert.wav",   FALSE },
    { PURPLE_SOUND_GOT_ATTENTION,  "attention",      "Attention received",                   "alert.wav",   TRUE  },
};
#define N_EVENTS ((int)(sizeof(events) / sizeof(events[0])))

static int sound_handle;
static gint64 login_mute_until = 0;
static gboolean event_reached = FALSE;

const DwpSoundEvent *dwp_sound_events(int *count)
{
    if(count)
        *count = N_EVENTS;
    return events;
}

static const DwpSoundEvent *find_event(PurpleSoundEventID id)
{
    int z;

    for(z = 0; z < N_EVENTS; z++)
        if(events[z].id == id)
            return &events[z];
    return NULL;
}

char *dwp_sound_enabled_pref(const DwpSoundEvent *e)
{
    return g_strdup_printf(DWP_PREF_SOUND_ENABLED "/%s", e->pref);
}

char *dwp_sound_file_pref(const DwpSoundEvent *e)
{
    return g_strdup_printf(DWP_PREF_SOUND_FILE "/%s", e->pref);
}

static gboolean event_enabled(const DwpSoundEvent *e)
{
    char *pref;
    gboolean on;

    if(!e)
        return FALSE;
    pref = dwp_sound_enabled_pref(e);
    on = purple_prefs_get_bool(pref);
    g_free(pref);
    return on;
}

void dwp_sound_add_prefs(void)
{
    int z;

    purple_prefs_add_none(DWP_PREF_SOUND);
    purple_prefs_add_none(DWP_PREF_SOUND_ENABLED);
    purple_prefs_add_none(DWP_PREF_SOUND_FILE);
    purple_prefs_add_string(DWP_PREF_SOUND_METHOD, "automatic");
    purple_prefs_add_string(DWP_PREF_SOUND_COMMAND, "");
    purple_prefs_add_bool(DWP_PREF_SOUND_MUTE, FALSE);
    purple_prefs_add_bool(DWP_PREF_SOUND_FOCUS, TRUE);
    for(z = 0; z < N_EVENTS; z++)
    {
        char *en = dwp_sound_enabled_pref(&events[z]);
        char *file = dwp_sound_file_pref(&events[z]);

        purple_prefs_add_bool(en, events[z].default_enabled);
        purple_prefs_add_path(file, "");
        g_free(en);
        g_free(file);
    }
}

char *dwp_sound_default_file(const DwpSoundEvent *e)
{
    const char *const *dirs = g_get_system_data_dirs();
    char *path;
    int z;

    /* Pidgin installs its sounds as <datadir>/sounds/purple/<file>. */
    for(z = 0; dirs && dirs[z]; z++)
    {
        path = g_build_filename(dirs[z], "sounds", "purple", e->default_file, NULL);
        if(g_file_test(path, G_FILE_TEST_IS_REGULAR))
            return path;
        g_free(path);
    }
    path = g_build_filename(dw_app_dir(), "sounds", e->default_file, NULL);
    if(g_file_test(path, G_FILE_TEST_IS_REGULAR))
        return path;
    g_free(path);
    path = g_build_filename(purple_user_dir(), "sounds", e->default_file, NULL);
    if(g_file_test(path, G_FILE_TEST_IS_REGULAR))
        return path;
    g_free(path);
    return NULL;
}

char *dwp_sound_effective_file(const DwpSoundEvent *e)
{
    char *pref = dwp_sound_file_pref(e);
    const char *custom = purple_prefs_get_path(pref);
    char *ret = (custom && *custom) ? g_strdup(custom) : dwp_sound_default_file(e);

    g_free(pref);
    return ret;
}

gboolean dwp_sound_muted(void)
{
    return purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_SOUND_MUTE);
}

void dwp_sound_set_muted(gboolean mute)
{
    purple_prefs_set_bool(DWP_PREF_SOUND_MUTE, mute);
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- players */

static gboolean spawn(char **argv)
{
    GError *error = NULL;

    /* Output is not discarded: player errors stay visible on the terminal. */
    if(!g_spawn_async(NULL, argv, NULL, G_SPAWN_SEARCH_PATH, NULL, NULL, NULL, &error))
    {
        purple_debug_error("sound", "Could not run %s: %s\n", argv[0], error ? error->message : "unknown error");
        if(error)
            g_error_free(error);
        return FALSE;
    }
    return TRUE;
}

static gboolean play_with_command(const char *command, const char *file)
{
    int argc = 0, z;
    char **parsed = NULL;
    GPtrArray *args;
    GError *error = NULL;
    gboolean substituted = FALSE, ok;

    if(!command || !*command)
        return FALSE;
    if(!g_shell_parse_argv(command, &argc, &parsed, &error))
    {
        purple_debug_error("sound", "Bad sound command: %s\n", error ? error->message : "");
        if(error)
            g_error_free(error);
        return FALSE;
    }
    args = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(g_free);
    for(z = 0; z < argc; z++)
    {
        if(strstr(parsed[z], "%s"))
        {
            char **parts = g_strsplit(parsed[z], "%s", -1);

            g_ptr_array_add(args, g_strjoinv(file, parts));
            g_strfreev(parts);
            substituted = TRUE;
        }
        else
            g_ptr_array_add(args, g_strdup(parsed[z]));
    }
    g_strfreev(parsed);
    if(!substituted)
        g_ptr_array_add(args, g_strdup(file));
    g_ptr_array_add(args, NULL);
    ok = spawn((char **)args->pdata);
    g_ptr_array_free(args, TRUE);
    return ok;
}

static gboolean play_automatic(const char *file)
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    wchar_t *wfile = g_utf8_to_utf16(file, -1, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    BOOL ok = wfile ? PlaySoundW(wfile, NULL, SND_ASYNC | SND_FILENAME | SND_NODEFAULT) : FALSE;

    g_free(wfile);
    return ok ? TRUE : FALSE;
#elif defined(__OS2__)
    (void)file;
    return FALSE;       /* uses the command from Preferences, else a beep */
#else
    static const char *const players[] = { "pw-play", "paplay", "aplay", NULL };
    int z;

    for(z = 0; players[z]; z++)
    {
        char *path, *argv[3];

        if(strcmp(players[z], "aplay") == 0 && !g_str_has_suffix(file, ".wav") && !g_str_has_suffix(file, ".WAV"))
            continue;
        path = g_find_program_in_path(players[z]);
        if(!path)
            continue;
        g_free(path);
        argv[0] = (char *)players[z];
        argv[1] = (char *)file;
        argv[2] = NULL;
        return spawn(argv);
    }
    return FALSE;
#endif
}

static void play_file(const char *file)
{
    const char *method = purple_prefs_get_string(DWP_PREF_SOUND_METHOD);

    if(purple_strequal(method, "none"))
        return;
    if(purple_strequal(method, "beep") || !file || !*file || !g_file_test(file, G_FILE_TEST_IS_REGULAR))
    {
        if(!purple_strequal(method, "beep"))
            purple_debug_warning("sound", "Sound file not found: %s\n", file ? file : "(none)");
        dw_beep(880, 120);
        return;
    }
    if(purple_strequal(method, "command"))
    {
        if(!play_with_command(purple_prefs_get_string(DWP_PREF_SOUND_COMMAND), file))
            dw_beep(880, 120);
        return;
    }
    if(!play_automatic(file) &&
       !play_with_command(purple_prefs_get_string(DWP_PREF_SOUND_COMMAND), file))
        dw_beep(880, 120);
}

void dwp_sound_test(const char *file)
{
    play_file(file);
}

static void ui_play_file(const char *filename)
{
    if(!dwp_sound_muted())
        play_file(filename);
}

static void ui_play_event(PurpleSoundEventID id)
{
    const DwpSoundEvent *e = find_event(id);
    char *file;

    event_reached = TRUE;
    if(dwp_sound_muted() || !event_enabled(e))
        return;
    file = dwp_sound_effective_file(e);
    purple_debug_info("sound", "Playing '%s': %s (method %s)\n", e->pref, file ? file : "(no file)",
                      purple_prefs_get_string(DWP_PREF_SOUND_METHOD));
    play_file(file);
    g_free(file);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ event rules */

static gboolean status_allows(PurpleAccount *account)
{
    int rule = purple_prefs_get_int("/purple/sound/while_status");
    PurpleStatus *status;
    gboolean available;

    if(rule == 3 || !account)
        return TRUE;
    status = purple_account_get_active_status(account);
    if(!status || !purple_status_is_online(status))
        return TRUE;
    available = purple_status_is_available(status);
    return (available && rule == 1) || (!available && rule == 2);
}

static void request_event(PurpleSoundEventID id, PurpleAccount *account, PurpleConversation *conv)
{
    const DwpSoundEvent *e = find_event(id);
    const char *name = e ? e->pref : "unknown";

    if(dwp_sound_muted())
    {
        purple_debug_info("sound", "'%s' not played: sounds are muted\n", name);
        return;
    }
    if(!event_enabled(e))
    {
        purple_debug_info("sound", "'%s' not played: switched off in Preferences\n", name);
        return;
    }
    if(conv && !purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_SOUND_FOCUS) && purple_conversation_has_focus(conv))
    {
        purple_debug_info("sound", "'%s' not played: the conversation has focus\n", name);
        return;
    }
    if(!status_allows(account))
    {
        purple_debug_info("sound", "'%s' not played: account status rule\n", name);
        return;
    }
    event_reached = FALSE;
    purple_sound_play_event(id, account);
    if(!event_reached)
        purple_debug_info("sound", "'%s' not played: suppressed by libpurple "
                          "(same sound within 2 seconds, or a plugin)\n", name);
}

static gboolean login_muted(void)
{
    return g_get_monotonic_time() < login_mute_until;
}

static void play_conv_event(PurpleConversation *conv, PurpleSoundEventID id)
{
    request_event(id, conv ? purple_conversation_get_account(conv) : NULL, conv);
}

static void on_account_signed_on(PurpleAccount *account, gpointer data)
{
    (void)account;
    (void)data;
    login_mute_until = g_get_monotonic_time() + 10 * G_USEC_PER_SEC;
}

static void on_buddy_signed_on(PurpleBuddy *buddy, gpointer data)
{
    (void)data;
    if(!login_muted())
        request_event(PURPLE_SOUND_BUDDY_ARRIVE, purple_buddy_get_account(buddy), NULL);
}

static void on_buddy_signed_off(PurpleBuddy *buddy, gpointer data)
{
    (void)data;
    if(!login_muted())
        request_event(PURPLE_SOUND_BUDDY_LEAVE, purple_buddy_get_account(buddy), NULL);
}

static void on_received_im(PurpleAccount *account, char *sender, char *message, PurpleConversation *conv,
                           PurpleMessageFlags flags, gpointer data)
{
    (void)sender;
    (void)message;
    (void)data;
    if(flags & PURPLE_MESSAGE_DELAYED)
        return;
    if(!conv)
    {
        request_event(event_enabled(find_event(PURPLE_SOUND_FIRST_RECEIVE))
                      ? PURPLE_SOUND_FIRST_RECEIVE : PURPLE_SOUND_RECEIVE, account, NULL);
        return;
    }
    play_conv_event(conv, PURPLE_SOUND_RECEIVE);
}

static void on_sent_im(PurpleAccount *account, const char *receiver, const char *message, gpointer data)
{
    (void)message;
    (void)data;
    play_conv_event(purple_find_conversation_with_account(PURPLE_CONV_TYPE_IM, receiver, account),
                    PURPLE_SOUND_SEND);
}

static void on_received_chat(PurpleAccount *account, char *sender, char *message, PurpleConversation *conv,
                             PurpleMessageFlags flags, gpointer data)
{
    PurpleConvChat *chat;
    const char *nick;

    (void)account;
    (void)message;
    (void)data;
    if((flags & PURPLE_MESSAGE_DELAYED) || !conv)
        return;
    chat = PURPLE_CONV_CHAT(conv);
    nick = purple_conv_chat_get_nick(chat);
    if(sender && nick && purple_utf8_strcasecmp(sender, nick) == 0)
        return;
    if(sender && purple_conv_chat_is_user_ignored(chat, sender))
        return;
    if((flags & PURPLE_MESSAGE_NICK) && event_enabled(find_event(PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_NICK)))
        play_conv_event(conv, PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_NICK);
    else
        play_conv_event(conv, PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_SAY);
}

static void on_sent_chat(PurpleAccount *account, const char *message, int id, gpointer data)
{
    PurpleConnection *gc = purple_account_get_connection(account);

    (void)message;
    (void)data;
    play_conv_event(gc ? purple_find_chat(gc, id) : NULL, PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_YOU_SAY);
}

static void on_chat_buddy_joined(PurpleConversation *conv, const char *name, PurpleConvChatBuddyFlags flags,
                                 gboolean new_arrival, gpointer data)
{
    (void)name;
    (void)flags;
    (void)data;
    if(new_arrival)
        play_conv_event(conv, PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_JOIN);
}

static void on_chat_buddy_left(PurpleConversation *conv, const char *name, const char *reason, gpointer data)
{
    (void)name;
    (void)reason;
    (void)data;
    play_conv_event(conv, PURPLE_SOUND_CHAT_LEAVE);
}

static void on_got_attention(PurpleAccount *account, const char *who, PurpleConversation *conv,
                             guint type, gpointer data)
{
    (void)who;
    (void)type;
    (void)data;
    if(conv)
        play_conv_event(conv, PURPLE_SOUND_GOT_ATTENTION);
    else
        request_event(PURPLE_SOUND_GOT_ATTENTION, account, NULL);
}

static void ui_init(void)
{
    void *accounts = purple_accounts_get_handle();
    void *blist = purple_blist_get_handle();
    void *convs = purple_conversations_get_handle();

    purple_signal_connect(accounts, "account-signed-on", &sound_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_account_signed_on), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(blist, "buddy-signed-on", &sound_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_buddy_signed_on), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(blist, "buddy-signed-off", &sound_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_buddy_signed_off), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(convs, "received-im-msg", &sound_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_received_im), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(convs, "sent-im-msg", &sound_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_sent_im), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(convs, "received-chat-msg", &sound_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_received_chat), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(convs, "sent-chat-msg", &sound_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_sent_chat), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(convs, "chat-buddy-joined", &sound_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_chat_buddy_joined), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(convs, "chat-buddy-left", &sound_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_chat_buddy_left), NULL);
    purple_signal_connect(convs, "got-attention", &sound_handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(on_got_attention), NULL);
}

static void ui_uninit(void)
{
    purple_signals_disconnect_by_handle(&sound_handle);
}

PurpleSoundUiOps *dwp_sound_ui_ops(void)
{
    static PurpleSoundUiOps ops;
    static gboolean ready = FALSE;

    if(!ready)
    {
        memset(&ops, 0, sizeof(ops));
        ops.init = ui_init;
        ops.uninit = ui_uninit;
        ops.play_file = ui_play_file;
        ops.play_event = ui_play_event;
        ready = TRUE;
    }
    return &ops;
}

===== END FILE: src/sound.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/tray.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - system tray icon.
 *
 * Dynamic Windows reports tray clicks as DW_SIGNAL_BUTTON_PRESS on the
 * widget given to dw_taskbar_insert(); the buddy list owns a 1x1 render
 * widget for that (see dwp_blist_tray_anchor()).  On OS/2 the tray needs
 * a SysTray/xCenter tray server; without one the calls do nothing.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

static gboolean shown = FALSE;
static HICN current_icon = 0;
static char *current_tip = NULL;

gboolean dwp_tray_shown(void)
{
    return shown;
}

static HICN pick_icon(int *unread)
{
    PurpleSavedStatus *saved = purple_savedstatus_get_current();
    PurpleStatusPrimitive prim = saved ? purple_savedstatus_get_type(saved) : PURPLE_STATUS_AVAILABLE;
    gboolean any_online = FALSE;
    GList *l;

    *unread = dwp_conv_unread_count();
    for(l = purple_accounts_get_all_active(); l; l = g_list_delete_link(l, l))
        if(purple_account_is_connected(l->data))
            any_online = TRUE;
    if(*unread && dwp_chat_icon())
        return dwp_chat_icon();
    if(!any_online)
        prim = PURPLE_STATUS_OFFLINE;
    return dwp_status_icon(prim, FALSE) ? dwp_status_icon(prim, FALSE) : dwp_app_icon();
}

void dwp_tray_update(void)
{
    HWND anchor = dwp_blist_tray_anchor();
    int unread = 0;
    HICN icon;
    char *tip;
    PurpleSavedStatus *saved;

    if(!shown || !anchor)
        return;
    icon = pick_icon(&unread);
    saved = purple_savedstatus_get_current();
    if(unread)
        tip = g_strdup_printf("%s - %d unread conversation%s", DWP_NAME, unread, unread == 1 ? "" : "s");
    else
        tip = g_strdup_printf("%s - %s", DWP_NAME,
                              dwp_primitive_label(saved ? purple_savedstatus_get_type(saved) : PURPLE_STATUS_AVAILABLE));
    if(icon == current_icon && purple_strequal(tip, current_tip))
    {
        g_free(tip);
        return;
    }
    if(current_icon)
        dw_taskbar_delete(anchor, current_icon);
    if(icon)
        dw_taskbar_insert(anchor, icon, tip);
    current_icon = icon;
    g_free(current_tip);
    current_tip = tip;
}

static void tray_remove(void)
{
    HWND anchor = dwp_blist_tray_anchor();

    if(shown && anchor && current_icon)
        dw_taskbar_delete(anchor, current_icon);
    shown = FALSE;
    current_icon = 0;
    g_free(current_tip);
    current_tip = NULL;
}

void dwp_tray_apply_prefs(void)
{
    gboolean want = purple_prefs_get_bool(DWP_PREF_TRAY_ENABLED) &&
                    dw_feature_get(DW_FEATURE_TASK_BAR) != DW_FEATURE_UNSUPPORTED &&
                    dwp_app_icon() != 0;

    if(want && !shown)
    {
        shown = TRUE;
        dwp_tray_update();
    }
    else if(!want && shown)
    {
        tray_remove();
        /* Without a tray the buddy list must stay reachable. */
        dwp_blist_present();
    }
}

void dwp_tray_shutdown(void)
{
    tray_remove();
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ menu */

static void tm_toggle(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_blist_toggle(); }
static void tm_accounts(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_accounts_show(); }
static void tm_prefs(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_prefs_show(); }
static void tm_new_im(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_show_new_im(); }
static void tm_join(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_show_join_chat(); }
static void tm_quit(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s) { (void)a; (void)b; (void)s; dwp_core_request_quit(); }

static void tm_mute(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)a; (void)b; (void)s;
    dwp_sound_set_muted(!dwp_sound_muted());
    dwp_blist_sync_mute();
}

static void tm_status(gpointer prim, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)b; (void)s;
    dwp_blist_set_status_primitive((PurpleStatusPrimitive)GPOINTER_TO_INT(prim));
}

static void tm_unread(gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *s)
{
    (void)a; (void)b; (void)s;
    dwp_conv_present_first_unread();
}

static void tray_menu(void)
{
    static const PurpleStatusPrimitive prims[] = {
        PURPLE_STATUS_AVAILABLE, PURPLE_STATUS_AWAY, PURPLE_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE,
        PURPLE_STATUS_EXTENDED_AWAY, PURPLE_STATUS_INVISIBLE, PURPLE_STATUS_OFFLINE
    };
    HMENUI menu, status;
    size_t z;

    dwp_actions_reset();
    menu = dw_menu_new(0);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, dwp_blist_visible() ? "Hide ~Buddy List" : "Show ~Buddy List", tm_toggle, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    if(dwp_conv_unread_count())
        dwp_menu_add(menu, "Show ~Unread Messages", tm_unread, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_separator(menu);
    status = dw_menu_new(0);
    for(z = 0; z < sizeof(prims) / sizeof(prims[0]); z++)
        dwp_menu_add(status, dwp_primitive_label(prims[z]), tm_status, GINT_TO_POINTER(prims[z]), NULL, NULL);
    dw_menu_append_item(menu, "Change ~Status", DW_MENU_POPUP, 0, TRUE, FALSE, status);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "New ~Message...", tm_new_im, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "~Join a Chat...", tm_join, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_separator(menu);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "~Accounts", tm_accounts, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "Pr~eferences", tm_prefs, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, dwp_sound_muted() ? "Un~mute Sounds" : "~Mute Sounds", tm_mute, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_separator(menu);
    dwp_menu_add(menu, "~Quit", tm_quit, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    dwp_menu_popup_here(&menu, dwp_blist_window());
}

void dwp_tray_clicked(int button)
{
    dwp_note_activity();
    if(button & DW_BUTTON1_MASK)
    {
        /* Left click: show unread messages first, else toggle the list. */
        if(!dwp_conv_present_first_unread())
            dwp_blist_toggle();
    }
    else
        tray_menu();
}

===== END FILE: src/tray.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/util.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - shared helpers: strings, deferred tasks, menus, layout,
 * prompts and account/group combo boxes.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

#include <ctype.h>
#include <stdarg.h>

/* ================================================================ strings */

char *dwp_get_text(HWND handle)
{
    char *tmp, *ret;

    if(!handle)
        return g_strdup("");
    tmp = dw_window_get_text(handle);
    ret = g_strdup(tmp ? tmp : "");
    if(tmp)
        dw_free(tmp);
    return ret;
}

char *dwp_get_text_trimmed(HWND handle)
{
    return g_strstrip(dwp_get_text(handle));
}

static void strip_cr(char *s)
{
    char *in, *out;

    for(in = out = s; *in; in++)
        if(*in != '\r')
            *out++ = *in;
    *out = '\0';
}

char *dwp_mle_get_text(HWND mle)
{
    unsigned long size = 0;
    char *tmp, *buf, *ret;

    if(!mle)
        return g_strdup("");
    /* dw_window_get_text() handles MLEs on Windows and OS/2 (full UTF-8);
     * the GTK ports only support it for entry fields, so fall back to
     * dw_mle_export() there.  GTK counts characters, so size the buffer for
     * multi-byte UTF-8. */
    tmp = dw_window_get_text(mle);
    if(tmp && *tmp)
    {
        ret = g_strdup(tmp);
        dw_free(tmp);
        strip_cr(ret);
        return ret;
    }
    if(tmp)
        dw_free(tmp);
    dw_mle_get_size(mle, &size, NULL);
    if(!size)
        return g_strdup("");
    buf = g_malloc0(size * 6 + 16);
    dw_mle_export(mle, buf, 0, (int)size);
    ret = g_strdup(buf);
    g_free(buf);
    strip_cr(ret);
    if(!g_utf8_validate(ret, -1, NULL))
    {
        char *fixed = purple_utf8_salvage(ret);

        g_free(ret);
        ret = fixed;
    }
    return ret;
}

unsigned int dwp_mle_set_text(HWND mle, const char *text)
{
    dw_mle_clear(mle);
    if(!text || !*text)
        return 0;
    return dw_mle_import(mle, text, 0);
}

gboolean dwp_account_exists(PurpleAccount *account)
{
    return account && g_list_find(purple_accounts_get_all(), account) != NULL;
}

gboolean dwp_connection_exists(PurpleConnection *gc)
{
    return gc && g_list_find(purple_connections_get_all(), gc) != NULL;
}

gboolean dwp_conversation_exists(PurpleConversation *conv)
{
    return conv && g_list_find(purple_get_conversations(), conv) != NULL;
}

gboolean dwp_blist_node_exists(PurpleBlistNode *node)
{
    PurpleBlistNode *n;

    if(!node)
        return FALSE;
    for(n = purple_blist_get_root(); n; n = purple_blist_node_next(n, TRUE))
        if(n == node)
            return TRUE;
    return FALSE;
}

PurplePluginProtocolInfo *dwp_prpl_info(PurpleConnection *gc)
{
    PurplePlugin *prpl;

    if(!gc)
        return NULL;
    prpl = purple_connection_get_prpl(gc);
    return prpl ? PURPLE_PLUGIN_PROTOCOL_INFO(prpl) : NULL;
}

PurplePluginProtocolInfo *dwp_account_prpl_info(PurpleAccount *account, PurpleConnection **gc_out)
{
    PurpleConnection *gc = account ? purple_account_get_connection(account) : NULL;

    if(gc_out)
        *gc_out = gc;
    return dwp_prpl_info(gc);
}

char *dwp_account_display_name(PurpleAccount *account)
{
    if(!account)
        return g_strdup("");
    return g_strdup_printf("%s (%s)", purple_account_get_username(account),
                           purple_account_get_protocol_name(account));
}

const char *dwp_primitive_label(PurpleStatusPrimitive prim)
{
    switch(prim)
    {
        case PURPLE_STATUS_AVAILABLE:      return "Available";
        case PURPLE_STATUS_AWAY:           return "Away";
        case PURPLE_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE:    return "Do Not Disturb";
        case PURPLE_STATUS_EXTENDED_AWAY:  return "Extended Away";
        case PURPLE_STATUS_INVISIBLE:      return "Invisible";
        case PURPLE_STATUS_OFFLINE:        return "Offline";
        default:                           return purple_primitive_get_name_from_type(prim);
    }
}

PurpleStatusPrimitive dwp_account_primitive(PurpleAccount *account)
{
    PurpleStatus *status;

    if(!account || !purple_account_is_connected(account))
        return PURPLE_STATUS_OFFLINE;
    status = purple_account_get_active_status(account);
    return status ? purple_status_type_get_primitive(purple_status_get_type(status))
                  : PURPLE_STATUS_AVAILABLE;
}

PurpleStatusPrimitive dwp_buddy_primitive(PurpleBuddy *buddy, gboolean *idle)
{
    PurplePresence *presence;
    PurpleStatus *status;

    if(idle)
        *idle = FALSE;
    if(!buddy || !PURPLE_BUDDY_IS_ONLINE(buddy))
        return PURPLE_STATUS_OFFLINE;
    presence = purple_buddy_get_presence(buddy);
    if(idle)
        *idle = purple_presence_is_idle(presence);
    status = purple_presence_get_active_status(presence);
    return status ? purple_status_type_get_primitive(purple_status_get_type(status))
                  : PURPLE_STATUS_AVAILABLE;
}

char *dwp_mnemonic(const char *text)
{
    GString *out = g_string_new(NULL);
    const char *p;

    for(p = text ? text : ""; *p; p++)
    {
        if(*p == '_')
        {
            if(p[1] == '_')
            {
                g_string_append_c(out, '_');
                p++;
            }
            else
                g_string_append_c(out, '~');
        }
        else if(*p == '~')
            g_string_append_c(out, '-');
        else
            g_string_append_c(out, *p);
    }
    return g_string_free(out, FALSE);
}

char *dwp_strip_mnemonic(const char *text)
{
    GString *out = g_string_new(NULL);
    const char *p;

    for(p = text ? text : ""; *p; p++)
    {
        if(*p == '_')
        {
            if(p[1] == '_')
            {
                g_string_append_c(out, '_');
                p++;
            }
        }
        else
            g_string_append_c(out, *p);
    }
    return g_string_free(out, FALSE);
}

gboolean dwp_open_link(const char *url)
{
    static const char *const allowed[] = { "http:", "https:", "mailto:", "xmpp:", "irc:", "ircs:", NULL };
    int z;

    if(!url || !*url)
        return FALSE;
    for(z = 0; allowed[z]; z++)
    {
        if(g_ascii_strncasecmp(url, allowed[z], strlen(allowed[z])) == 0)
        {
            dw_browse(url);
            return TRUE;
        }
    }
    purple_debug_warning(DWP_UI_ID, "Refusing to open link with unsupported scheme\n");
    return FALSE;
}

char *dwp_filename_to_utf8(const char *name)
{
    char *ret;

    if(!name)
        return g_strdup("");
    ret = g_filename_to_utf8(name, -1, NULL, NULL, NULL);
    return ret ? ret : purple_utf8_salvage(name);
}

/* ================================================================== icons */

static HICN status_icons[8];
static gboolean icons_loaded = FALSE;
static HICN chat_icon_h, app_icon_h;

static HICN load_icon_from(const char *dir, const char *name)
{
    char *path = g_build_filename(dir, name, NULL);
    HICN icon = 0;

#ifndef _WIN32
    /* OS/2 tries "<name>.ico" first and only understands OS/2 icons there,
     * so ask for the PNG explicitly; GTK and macOS load PNG anyway. */
    {
        char *png = g_strdup_printf("%s.png", path);

        if(g_file_test(png, G_FILE_TEST_IS_REGULAR))
            icon = dw_icon_load_from_file(png);
        g_free(png);
    }
#endif
    /* Windows: .ico first; elsewhere whatever the port finds. */
    if(!icon)
        icon = dw_icon_load_from_file(path);
    g_free(path);
    return icon;
}

static char *exe_dir = NULL;

void dwp_set_exe_path(const char *argv0)
{
    char *path = NULL;

#ifdef __linux__
    path = g_file_read_link("/proc/self/exe", NULL);
#endif
    if(!path && argv0 && *argv0)
        path = g_strdup(argv0);
    g_free(exe_dir);
    exe_dir = path ? g_path_get_dirname(path) : NULL;
    g_free(path);
}

static HICN load_icon(const char *name)
{
    char *dir;
    HICN icon = 0;

    /* <exe dir>/icons (build tree, OS/2 and Windows installs),
     * <dw app dir>/icons (<prefix>/share/dwpurple/icons), <config>/ui-icons. */
    if(exe_dir)
    {
        dir = g_build_filename(exe_dir, "icons", NULL);
        icon = load_icon_from(dir, name);
        g_free(dir);
    }
    if(!icon)
    {
        dir = g_build_filename(dw_app_dir(), "icons", NULL);
        icon = load_icon_from(dir, name);
        g_free(dir);
    }
    if(!icon)
    {
        dir = g_build_filename(purple_user_dir(), "ui-icons", NULL);
        icon = load_icon_from(dir, name);
        g_free(dir);
    }
    return icon;
}

static void icons_load(void)
{
    static const char *const names[8] = {
        "offline", "available", "away", "busy", "xa", "invisible", "idle", "unknown"
    };
    int z;

    if(icons_loaded)
        return;
    icons_loaded = TRUE;
    for(z = 0; z < 8; z++)
        status_icons[z] = load_icon(names[z]);
    chat_icon_h = load_icon("chat");
    app_icon_h = load_icon("dwpurple");
    if(!app_icon_h)
        app_icon_h = status_icons[1];
}

HICN dwp_status_icon(PurpleStatusPrimitive prim, gboolean idle)
{
    icons_load();
    if(idle && prim != PURPLE_STATUS_OFFLINE && status_icons[6])
        return status_icons[6];
    switch(prim)
    {
        case PURPLE_STATUS_OFFLINE:       return status_icons[0];
        case PURPLE_STATUS_AVAILABLE:
        case PURPLE_STATUS_TUNE:
        case PURPLE_STATUS_MOOD:          return status_icons[1];
        case PURPLE_STATUS_AWAY:          return status_icons[2];
        case PURPLE_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE:   return status_icons[3];
        case PURPLE_STATUS_EXTENDED_AWAY: return status_icons[4];
        case PURPLE_STATUS_INVISIBLE:     return status_icons[5];
        default:                          return status_icons[7];
    }
}

HICN dwp_chat_icon(void)
{
    icons_load();
    return chat_icon_h;
}

HICN dwp_app_icon(void)
{
    icons_load();
    return app_icon_h;
}

void dwp_icons_free(void)
{
    /* Icons are released by the windowing system at exit; nothing to do
     * portably (dw_icon_free on a shared handle would break live widgets). */
}

static time_t last_activity = 0;

void dwp_note_activity(void)
{
    last_activity = time(NULL);
}

time_t dwp_last_activity(void)
{
    if(!last_activity)
        last_activity = time(NULL);
    return last_activity;
}

/* ========================================================= deferred tasks */

typedef struct {
    DwpTaskFunc func;
    gpointer data;
} DwpTask;

static GQueue task_queue = G_QUEUE_INIT;
static HTIMER task_timer = 0;

static int DWSIGNAL task_timer_cb(void *unused)
{
    int budget = 64;

    (void)unused;
    dwp_gui_lock();
    /* Pop one task at a time: a task may start a nested main loop
     * (modal dialog) during which this timer can fire again. */
    while(budget-- > 0 && !g_queue_is_empty(&task_queue))
    {
        DwpTask *task = g_queue_pop_head(&task_queue);
        DwpTaskFunc func = task->func;
        gpointer data = task->data;

        g_free(task);
        func(data);
    }
    dwp_gui_unlock();
    return TRUE;
}

void dwp_defer(DwpTaskFunc func, gpointer data)
{
    DwpTask *task;

    g_return_if_fail(func != NULL);
    task = g_new0(DwpTask, 1);
    task->func = func;
    task->data = data;
    g_queue_push_tail(&task_queue, task);
}

void dwp_tasks_start(void)
{
    if(!task_timer)
        task_timer = dw_timer_connect(30, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(task_timer_cb), NULL);
}

void dwp_tasks_stop(void)
{
    /* Run what is left so windows are destroyed and memory released. */
    task_timer_cb(NULL);
    if(task_timer)
    {
        dw_timer_disconnect(task_timer);
        task_timer = 0;
    }
}

typedef struct {
    HWND window;
    GDestroyNotify free_data;
    gpointer data;
} CloseLater;

static void close_later_task(gpointer p)
{
    CloseLater *cl = p;

    if(cl->window)
        dw_window_destroy(cl->window);
    if(cl->free_data)
        cl->free_data(cl->data);
    g_free(cl);
}

void dwp_window_close_later(HWND window, GDestroyNotify free_data, gpointer data)
{
    CloseLater *cl = g_new0(CloseLater, 1);

    if(window)
        dw_window_hide(window);
    cl->window = window;
    cl->free_data = free_data;
    cl->data = data;
    dwp_defer(close_later_task, cl);
}

/* ============================================================ menu actions */

typedef struct {
    DwpActionFunc func;
    gpointer a, b;
    char *str;
} DwpAction;

static GPtrArray *popup_actions = NULL;
static int menu_syncing = 0;

static void action_free(gpointer p)
{
    DwpAction *act = p;

    g_free(act->str);
    g_free(act);
}

void dwp_actions_reset(void)
{
    if(popup_actions)
        g_ptr_array_set_size(popup_actions, 0);
}

static int DWSIGNAL action_clicked(HWND window, void *data)
{
    DwpAction *act = data;
    DwpActionFunc func;
    gpointer a, b;
    char *str;

    (void)window;
    /* State changes made by dwp_menu_set_state() are not user clicks. */
    if(menu_syncing || !act || !act->func)
        return FALSE;
    /* Copy first: the action may rebuild menus and release this record. */
    func = act->func;
    a = act->a;
    b = act->b;
    str = g_strdup(act->str);
    dwp_note_activity();
    func(a, b, str);
    g_free(str);
    return FALSE;
}

HWND dwp_menu_add(HMENUI menu, const char *title, DwpActionFunc func,
                  gpointer a, gpointer b, const char *str)
{
    HWND item;
    DwpAction *act;

    item = dw_menu_append_item(menu, title, DW_MENU_POPUP, 0, TRUE, FALSE, DW_NOMENU);
    if(!item || !func)
        return item;
    if(!popup_actions)
        popup_actions = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(action_free);
    act = g_new0(DwpAction, 1);
    act->func = func;
    act->a = a;
    act->b = b;
    act->str = g_strdup(str);
    g_ptr_array_add(popup_actions, act);
    dw_signal_connect(item, DW_SIGNAL_CLICKED, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(action_clicked), act);
    return item;
}

HWND dwp_menu_add_static(HMENUI menu, const char *title, unsigned long id, int check,
                         DwpActionFunc func, gpointer a)
{
    HWND item = dw_menu_append_item(menu, title, id ? id : DW_MENU_AUTO, 0, TRUE, check, DW_NOMENU);

    if(item && func)
    {
        DwpAction *act = g_new0(DwpAction, 1);

        act->func = func;
        act->a = a;
        /* Lives for the life of the program. */
        dw_signal_connect(item, DW_SIGNAL_CLICKED, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(action_clicked), act);
    }
    return item;
}

void dwp_menu_separator(HMENUI menu)
{
    dw_menu_append_item(menu, DW_MENU_SEPARATOR, DW_MENU_POPUP, 0, TRUE, FALSE, DW_NOMENU);
}

void dwp_menu_set_state(HMENUI menu, unsigned long id, unsigned long state)
{
    if(!menu)
        return;
    menu_syncing++;
    dw_menu_item_set_state(menu, id, state);
    menu_syncing--;
}

void dwp_menu_popup_here(HMENUI *menu, HWND parent)
{
    long x = 0, y = 0;

    dw_pointer_query_pos(&x, &y);
    dw_menu_popup(menu, parent, (int)x, (int)y);
}

/* ================================================================== layout */

HWND dwp_window_new(const char *title, int width, int height)
{
    HWND win = dw_window_new(HWND_DESKTOP, title,
                             DW_FCF_SYSMENU | DW_FCF_TITLEBAR | DW_FCF_SIZEBORDER |
                             DW_FCF_MINMAX | DW_FCF_TASKLIST);
    HICN icon = dwp_app_icon();

    if(icon)
        dw_window_set_icon(win, icon);
    if(width > 0 && height > 0)
        dw_window_set_size(win, width, height);
    return win;
}

HWND dwp_label(const char *text)
{
    HWND label = dw_text_new(text ? text : "", 0);

    dw_window_set_style(label, DW_DT_VCENTER, DW_DT_VCENTER);
    return label;
}

HWND dwp_form_row(HWND box, const char *label, HWND widget)
{
    HWND row = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);

    dw_box_pack_start(row, dwp_label(label), 150, DW_SIZE_AUTO, FALSE, TRUE, 2);
    if(widget)
        dw_box_pack_start(row, widget, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, TRUE, 2);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, row, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 1);
    return row;
}

HWND dwp_button(HWND box, const char *text, void *func, void *data)
{
    HWND button = dw_button_new(text, 0);

    dw_box_pack_start(box, button, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, FALSE, FALSE, 2);
    if(func)
        dw_signal_connect(button, DW_SIGNAL_CLICKED, func, data);
    return button;
}

HWND dwp_text_block(HWND box, const char *text, int width, int max_height)
{
    char *copy = g_strdup(text ? text : ""), *crlf, **parts;
    int z, lines = 0, cols, height;
    HWND mle;

    /* Normalise to \n, then to the \r\n the platform edit controls expect. */
    strip_cr(copy);
    parts = g_strsplit(copy, "\n", -1);
    g_free(copy);
    /* Rough wrapped line count: ~7 pixels per character. */
    cols = width > 90 ? (width - 20) / 7 : 10;
    for(z = 0; parts[z]; z++)
        lines += 1 + (int)(g_utf8_strlen(parts[z], -1) / cols);
    crlf = g_strjoinv("\r\n", parts);
    g_strfreev(parts);
    height = lines * 18 + 14;
    if(height < 34)
        height = 34;
    if(max_height > 0 && height > max_height)
        height = max_height;
    mle = dw_mle_new(0);
    dw_mle_set_editable(mle, FALSE);
    dw_mle_set_word_wrap(mle, TRUE);
    dw_mle_import(mle, crlf, 0);
    dw_mle_set_cursor(mle, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, mle, width, height, TRUE, TRUE, 3);
    g_free(crlf);
    return mle;
}

HWND dwp_box_reset(HWND holder, HWND old, int type)
{
    HWND box;

    if(old)
    {
        dw_box_unpack(old);
        dw_window_destroy(old);
    }
    box = dw_box_new(type, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(holder, box, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    return box;
}

void dwp_spacer(HWND box)
{
    dw_box_pack_start(box, 0, 1, 1, TRUE, FALSE, 0);
}

void dwp_window_present(HWND window, int width, int height)
{
    int sw = dw_screen_width(), sh = dw_screen_height();

    if(width > 0 && height > 0)
    {
        if(width > sw)
            width = sw;
        if(height > sh)
            height = sh;
        dw_window_set_pos_size(window, (sw - width) / 2, (sh - height) / 2, width, height);
    }
    dw_window_show(window);
    dw_window_raise(window);
}

int dwp_combo_index(HWND combo, char **items, int count)
{
    int index = dw_listbox_selected(combo);
    char *text;
    int z;

    text = dwp_get_text(combo);
    /* An edited combo entry leaves a stale index; trust the text first. */
    if(index >= 0 && index < count && items && purple_strequal(items[index], text))
    {
        g_free(text);
        return index;
    }
    for(z = 0; z < count && items; z++)
    {
        if(purple_strequal(items[z], text))
        {
            g_free(text);
            return z;
        }
    }
    g_free(text);
    return (index >= 0 && index < count) ? index : -1;
}

void dwp_combo_fill(HWND combo, char **items, int count, int selected)
{
    int z;

    dw_listbox_clear(combo);
    for(z = 0; z < count; z++)
        dw_listbox_append(combo, items[z]);
    if(selected >= 0 && selected < count)
    {
        dw_listbox_select(combo, selected, TRUE);
        dw_window_set_text(combo, items[selected]);
    }
    else
        dw_window_set_text(combo, "");
}

/* ================================================================ prompts */

typedef struct {
    HWND window, entry;
    DwpPromptFunc ok;
    gpointer data;
} Prompt;

static int DWSIGNAL prompt_ok(HWND button, void *data)
{
    Prompt *p = data;
    char *text = dwp_get_text(p->entry);
    DwpPromptFunc ok = p->ok;
    gpointer udata = p->data;

    (void)button;
    p->ok = NULL;
    dwp_window_close_later(p->window, g_free, p);
    if(ok)
        ok(udata, text);
    g_free(text);
    return FALSE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL prompt_cancel(HWND button, void *data)
{
    Prompt *p = data;

    (void)button;
    p->ok = NULL;
    dwp_window_close_later(p->window, g_free, p);
    return TRUE;
}

void dwp_prompt(const char *title, const char *label, const char *initial, gboolean masked,
                DwpPromptFunc ok, gpointer data)
{
    Prompt *p = g_new0(Prompt, 1);
    HWND box, buttons, okb;

    p->ok = ok;
    p->data = data;
    p->window = dwp_window_new(title, 0, 0);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(p->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, dwp_label(label), DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    p->entry = masked ? dw_entryfield_password_new(initial ? initial : "", 0)
                      : dw_entryfield_new(initial ? initial : "", 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, p->entry, 300, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    okb = dwp_button(buttons, "OK", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(prompt_ok), p);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Cancel", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(prompt_cancel), p);
    dw_signal_connect(p->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(prompt_cancel), p);
    dw_window_click_default(p->entry, okb);
    dwp_window_present(p->window, 380, 130);
    dw_window_set_focus(p->entry);
}

typedef struct {
    HWND window;
} MessageWin;

static int DWSIGNAL message_close(HWND button, void *data)
{
    MessageWin *m = data;

    (void)button;
    dwp_window_close_later(m->window, g_free, m);
    return TRUE;
}

void dwp_message(const char *title, const char *primary, const char *secondary)
{
    MessageWin *m = g_new0(MessageWin, 1);
    HWND box, buttons, okb;

    m->window = dwp_window_new(title ? title : DWP_NAME, 0, 0);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(m->window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    if(primary && *primary)
    {
        HWND text = dw_text_new(primary, 0);
        dw_window_set_style(text, DW_DT_WORDBREAK, DW_DT_WORDBREAK);
        dw_box_pack_start(box, text, 380, 40, TRUE, FALSE, 4);
    }
    if(secondary && *secondary)
        dwp_text_block(box, secondary, 380, 200);
    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    okb = dwp_button(buttons, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(message_close), m);
    dw_signal_connect(m->window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(message_close), m);
    dwp_window_present(m->window, 420, secondary && *secondary ? 260 : 140);
    dw_window_set_focus(okb);
}

gboolean dwp_confirm(const char *title, const char *fmt, ...)
{
    va_list args;
    char *text;
    int rc;

    va_start(args, fmt);
    text = g_strdup_vprintf(fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
    rc = dw_messagebox(title, DW_MB_YESNO | DW_MB_QUESTION, "%s", text);
    g_free(text);
    return rc == DW_MB_RETURN_YES;
}

/* ========================================================= account combo */

struct _DwpAccountCombo {
    HWND combo;
    GPtrArray *accounts;
    GPtrArray *labels;
    gboolean online_only;
    DwpAccountFilter filter;
    DwpAccountChanged changed;
    gpointer changed_data;
    gboolean filling;
    int handle;      /* address used as the purple signal handle */
};

static int DWSIGNAL account_combo_select(HWND combo, int index, void *data)
{
    DwpAccountCombo *ac = data;

    (void)combo;
    (void)index;
    if(!ac->filling && ac->changed)
        ac->changed(ac->changed_data, dwp_account_combo_get(ac));
    return FALSE;
}

static void account_combo_signal(PurpleAccount *account, gpointer data)
{
    (void)account;
    dwp_account_combo_refill(data);
}

DwpAccountCombo *dwp_account_combo_new(gboolean online_only, DwpAccountFilter filter)
{
    DwpAccountCombo *ac = g_new0(DwpAccountCombo, 1);
    void *handle = purple_accounts_get_handle();

    ac->combo = dw_combobox_new("", 0);
    ac->accounts = g_ptr_array_new();
    ac->labels = g_ptr_array_new_with_free_func(g_free);
    ac->online_only = online_only;
    ac->filter = filter;
    dw_signal_connect(ac->combo, DW_SIGNAL_LIST_SELECT, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(account_combo_select), ac);
    purple_signal_connect(handle, "account-signed-on", &ac->handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(account_combo_signal), ac);
    purple_signal_connect(handle, "account-signed-off", &ac->handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(account_combo_signal), ac);
    purple_signal_connect(handle, "account-added", &ac->handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(account_combo_signal), ac);
    purple_signal_connect(handle, "account-removed", &ac->handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(account_combo_signal), ac);
    purple_signal_connect(handle, "account-enabled", &ac->handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(account_combo_signal), ac);
    purple_signal_connect(handle, "account-disabled", &ac->handle, PURPLE_CALLBACK(account_combo_signal), ac);
    dwp_account_combo_refill(ac);
    return ac;
}

HWND dwp_account_combo_widget(DwpAccountCombo *ac)
{
    return ac->combo;
}

void dwp_account_combo_refill(DwpAccountCombo *ac)
{
    PurpleAccount *current = dwp_account_combo_get(ac);
    GList *l;
    int z, sel = -1;

    ac->filling = TRUE;
    g_ptr_array_set_size(ac->accounts, 0);
    g_ptr_array_set_size(ac->labels, 0);
    for(l = purple_accounts_get_all(); l; l = l->next)
    {
        PurpleAccount *account = l->data;

        if(ac->online_only && !purple_account_is_connected(account))
            continue;
        if(ac->filter && !ac->filter(account))
            continue;
        g_ptr_array_add(ac->accounts, account);
        g_ptr_array_add(ac->labels, dwp_account_display_name(account));
    }
    for(z = 0; z < (int)ac->accounts->len; z++)
        if(g_ptr_array_index(ac->accounts, z) == current)
            sel = z;
    if(sel < 0 && ac->accounts->len)
        sel = 0;
    dwp_combo_fill(ac->combo, (char **)ac->labels->pdata, (int)ac->labels->len, sel);
    ac->filling = FALSE;
}

PurpleAccount *dwp_account_combo_get(DwpAccountCombo *ac)
{
    int index;
    PurpleAccount *account;

    if(!ac->accounts || !ac->accounts->len)
        return NULL;
    index = dwp_combo_index(ac->combo, (char **)ac->labels->pdata, (int)ac->labels->len);
    if(index < 0)
        return NULL;
    account = g_ptr_array_index(ac->accounts, index);
    return dwp_account_exists(account) ? account : NULL;
}

void dwp_account_combo_set(DwpAccountCombo *ac, PurpleAccount *account)
{
    int z;

    for(z = 0; z < (int)ac->accounts->len; z++)
    {
        if(g_ptr_array_index(ac->accounts, z) == account)
        {
            ac->filling = TRUE;
            dw_listbox_select(ac->combo, z, TRUE);
            dw_window_set_text(ac->combo, g_ptr_array_index(ac->labels, z));
            ac->filling = FALSE;
            return;
        }
    }
}

void dwp_account_combo_on_change(DwpAccountCombo *ac, DwpAccountChanged cb, gpointer data)
{
    ac->changed = cb;
    ac->changed_data = data;
}

void dwp_account_combo_free(DwpAccountCombo *ac)
{
    if(!ac)
        return;
    purple_signals_disconnect_by_handle(&ac->handle);
    ac->changed = NULL;
    g_ptr_array_free(ac->accounts, TRUE);
    g_ptr_array_free(ac->labels, TRUE);
    /* The widget belongs to its window; signal data must stay valid until
     * that window is destroyed, so release the struct from the task queue. */
    dwp_defer(g_free, ac);
}

/* =========================================================== group combo */

HWND dwp_group_combo_new(const char *initial)
{
    HWND combo = dw_combobox_new(initial ? initial : "", 0);
    PurpleBlistNode *node;

    for(node = purple_blist_get_root(); node; node = purple_blist_node_get_sibling_next(node))
        if(PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_GROUP(node))
            dw_listbox_append(combo, purple_group_get_name((PurpleGroup *)node));
    if(!initial || !*initial)
    {
        PurpleBlistNode *first = purple_blist_get_root();

        dw_window_set_text(combo, first && PURPLE_BLIST_NODE_IS_GROUP(first)
                                  ? purple_group_get_name((PurpleGroup *)first) : "Buddies");
    }
    return combo;
}

PurpleGroup *dwp_group_find_or_create(const char *name)
{
    PurpleGroup *group;

    if(!name || !*name)
        name = "Buddies";
    group = purple_find_group(name);
    if(!group)
    {
        group = purple_group_new(name);
        purple_blist_add_group(group, NULL);
    }
    return group;
}

===== END FILE: src/util.c =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/xfer.c =====
/*
 * DWPurple - file transfer window and xfer UI ops.
 *
 * Every listed transfer holds a reference, so a row can never point to a
 * freed PurpleXfer; "Remove Finished" drops the references.
 */
#include "dwpurple.h"

typedef struct {
    HWND window, list;
    gboolean visible, refresh_pending;
    gint64 last_refresh;
} XferWin;

static XferWin xw;
static GList *xfers = NULL;

static char *size_text(goffset size)
{
    return g_format_size((guint64)(size < 0 ? 0 : size));
}

static const char *status_text(PurpleXfer *xfer)
{
    switch(purple_xfer_get_status(xfer))
    {
        case PURPLE_XFER_STATUS_NOT_STARTED:    return "Waiting";
        case PURPLE_XFER_STATUS_ACCEPTED:       return "Accepted";
        case PURPLE_XFER_STATUS_STARTED:        return "Transferring";
        case PURPLE_XFER_STATUS_DONE:           return "Finished";
        case PURPLE_XFER_STATUS_CANCEL_LOCAL:   return "Cancelled";
        case PURPLE_XFER_STATUS_CANCEL_REMOTE:  return "Cancelled by peer";
        default:                                return "Unknown";
    }
}

static void refresh(void)
{
    PurpleXfer *selected = NULL;
    GList *l;
    int count, row = 0;
    void *alloc;

    xw.refresh_pending = FALSE;
    xw.last_refresh = g_get_monotonic_time();
    if(!xw.window)
        return;
    selected = (PurpleXfer *)dw_container_query_start(xw.list, DW_CRA_SELECTED | DW_CR_RETDATA);
    dw_container_clear(xw.list, TRUE);
    count = (int)g_list_length(xfers);
    if(!count)
        return;
    alloc = dw_container_alloc(xw.list, count);
    for(l = xfers; l; l = l->next, row++)
    {
        PurpleXfer *xfer = l->data;
        char *file = (char *)(purple_xfer_get_filename(xfer) ? purple_xfer_get_filename(xfer) : "");
        char *peer = (char *)(purple_xfer_get_remote_user(xfer) ? purple_xfer_get_remote_user(xfer) : "");
        char *dir = purple_xfer_get_type(xfer) == PURPLE_XFER_SEND ? "Send" : "Receive";
        char *status = (char *)status_text(xfer);
        char *total = size_text((goffset)purple_xfer_get_size(xfer));
        char *done = size_text((goffset)purple_xfer_get_bytes_sent(xfer));
        char *progress = g_strdup_printf("%d%% (%s of %s)", (int)(purple_xfer_get_progress(xfer) * 100.0),
                                         done, total);

        dw_container_set_item(xw.list, alloc, 0, row, &file);
        dw_container_set_item(xw.list, alloc, 1, row, &peer);
        dw_container_set_item(xw.list, alloc, 2, row, &dir);
        dw_container_set_item(xw.list, alloc, 3, row, &status);
        dw_container_set_item(xw.list, alloc, 4, row, &progress);
        dw_container_set_row_title(alloc, row, file);
        dw_container_set_row_data(alloc, row, xfer);
        g_free(progress);
        g_free(done);
        g_free(total);
    }
    dw_container_insert(xw.list, alloc, count);
    if(selected && g_list_find(xfers, selected))
        dw_container_cursor_by_data(xw.list, selected);
}

static void refresh_task(gpointer unused)
{
    (void)unused;
    if(!xw.refresh_pending)
        return;
    /* Progress updates can be very frequent: at most ~3 refreshes/second. */
    if(g_get_monotonic_time() - xw.last_refresh < 300000)
    {
        dwp_defer(refresh_task, NULL);
        return;
    }
    refresh();
}

static void schedule_refresh(void)
{
    if(!xw.window || xw.refresh_pending)
        return;
    xw.refresh_pending = TRUE;
    dwp_defer(refresh_task, NULL);
}

static PurpleXfer *selected_xfer(void)
{
    PurpleXfer *xfer;

    if(!xw.window)
        return NULL;
    xfer = (PurpleXfer *)dw_container_query_start(xw.list, DW_CRA_SELECTED | DW_CR_RETDATA);
    return g_list_find(xfers, xfer) ? xfer : NULL;
}

static int DWSIGNAL xfer_cancel(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurpleXfer *xfer = selected_xfer();

    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    if(xfer && !purple_xfer_is_completed(xfer) && !purple_xfer_is_canceled(xfer))
        purple_xfer_cancel_local(xfer);
    schedule_refresh();
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL xfer_copy_path(HWND w, void *data)
{
    PurpleXfer *xfer = selected_xfer();
    const char *path;

    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    if(xfer && (path = purple_xfer_get_local_filename(xfer)))
    {
        char *utf8 = dwp_filename_to_utf8(path);

        dw_clipboard_set_text(utf8, (int)strlen(utf8));
        g_free(utf8);
    }
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL xfer_clear(HWND w, void *data)
{
    GList *l, *next;

    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    /* Clear the rows first: unref may free the transfer. */
    if(xw.window)
        dw_container_clear(xw.list, TRUE);
    for(l = xfers; l; l = next)
    {
        PurpleXfer *xfer = l->data;

        next = l->next;
        if(purple_xfer_is_completed(xfer) || purple_xfer_is_canceled(xfer))
        {
            xfers = g_list_delete_link(xfers, l);
            purple_xfer_unref(xfer);
        }
    }
    refresh();
    return TRUE;
}

static int DWSIGNAL xfer_close(HWND w, void *data)
{
    (void)w;
    (void)data;
    if(xw.window)
    {
        dw_window_hide(xw.window);
        xw.visible = FALSE;
    }
    return TRUE;
}

static void ensure_window(void)
{
    unsigned long flags[5];
    char *titles[5] = { "File", "Peer", "Direction", "Status", "Progress" };
    HWND box, buttons;
    int z;

    if(xw.window)
        return;
    for(z = 0; z < 5; z++)
        flags[z] = DW_CFA_STRING | DW_CFA_LEFT | DW_CFA_HORZSEPARATOR | DW_CFA_SEPARATOR;
    xw.window = dwp_window_new("File Transfers", 0, 0);
    box = dw_box_new(DW_VERT, 4);
    dw_box_pack_start(xw.window, box, 0, 0, TRUE, TRUE, 0);
    xw.list = dw_container_new(0, FALSE);
    dw_container_setup(xw.list, flags, titles, 5, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, xw.list, 600, 220, TRUE, TRUE, 2);
    buttons = dw_box_new(DW_HORZ, 0);
    dw_box_pack_start(box, buttons, DW_SIZE_AUTO, DW_SIZE_AUTO, TRUE, FALSE, 2);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Cancel Transfer", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(xfer_cancel), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Copy Path", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(xfer_copy_path), NULL);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Remove Finished", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(xfer_clear), NULL);
    dwp_spacer(buttons);
    dwp_button(buttons, "Close", DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(xfer_close), NULL);
    dw_signal_connect(xw.window, DW_SIGNAL_DELETE, DW_SIGNAL_FUNC(xfer_close), NULL);
}

void dwp_xfers_show(void)
{
    gboolean first = !xw.window;

    ensure_window();
    refresh();
    if(first)
        dwp_window_present(xw.window, 680, 320);
    else
    {
        dw_window_show(xw.window);
        dw_window_raise(xw.window);
    }
    xw.visible = TRUE;
}

/* ================================================================ ui ops */

static void ui_new_xfer(PurpleXfer *xfer)
{
    (void)xfer;
}

static void ui_destroy(PurpleXfer *xfer)
{
    /* Only reached for transfers we never listed (we hold a reference). */
    if(g_list_find(xfers, xfer))
    {
        if(xw.window)
            dw_container_clear(xw.list, TRUE);
        xfers = g_list_remove(xfers, xfer);
        schedule_refresh();
    }
}

static void ui_add_xfer(PurpleXfer *xfer)
{
    if(g_list_find(xfers, xfer))
        return;
    purple_xfer_ref(xfer);
    xfers = g_list_append(xfers, xfer);
    if(!xw.visible)
        dwp_xfers_show();
    else
        schedule_refresh();
}

static void ui_update_progress(PurpleXfer *xfer, double percent)
{
    (void)xfer;
    (void)percent;
    schedule_refresh();
}

static void ui_cancel(PurpleXfer *xfer)
{
    (void)xfer;
    schedule_refresh();
}

PurpleXferUiOps *dwp_xfer_ui_ops(void)
{
    static PurpleXferUiOps ops;
    static gboolean ready = FALSE;

    if(!ready)
    {
        memset(&ops, 0, sizeof(ops));
        ops.new_xfer = ui_new_xfer;
        ops.destroy = ui_destroy;
        ops.add_xfer = ui_add_xfer;
        ops.update_progress = ui_update_progress;
        ops.cancel_local = ui_cancel;
        ops.cancel_remote = ui_cancel;
        ready = TRUE;
    }
    return &ops;
}

===== END FILE: src/xfer.c =====
